# Chapter 3: Character Classes
{{wide
Your Character Class is more than just a profession; it represents who your character is, and perhaps even what they believe in. There are eight Character Classes, each with subclasses in AK5e, summarised below:

:
![Caster](https://arknights.wiki.gg/images/e/e6/Caster.png?d45df1&20240920032400) {width:100px,margin-left:0cm,margin-right:0.2cm,wrapRight} **Caster.** Wield potent Originium Arts, unleashing elemental forces up close or from afar. 
:
Then specialise in versatility, causing your Arts to chain, blending technology and Arts together, huge single bursts of power, inflicting Status Conditions, or inflicting area-of-effect Damage.
::::
![Defender](https://arknights.wiki.gg/images/0/0d/Defender.png?86409b&20240920032415) {width:100px,margin-left:0cm,margin-right:0.2cm,wrapRight} **Defender.** :Wielder of the heaviest armour and shields, you stand up to extreme punishment to be the last warrior standing.
:
Then specialise in wielding Arts to oppose Casters, duelling the strongest foes, becoming personal artillery, healing your fellow warriors, protecting others, or using ranged weapons alongside a shield.
::
![Guard](https://arknights.wiki.gg/images/7/74/Guard.png?339f06&20240920032430) {width:100px,margin-left:0cm,margin-right:0.2cm,wrapRight}**Guard.** The masters of melee, you are at home deep in the thickest fights, striving for martial  perfection however you can. 
:
Then you specialise in combining Arts and martial skill, fighting with your fists, attacking multiple enemies at once, crushing foes with your overwhelming fortitude, duelling isolated enemies, trading your health for power, instructing others, building explosive transient strength, dancing through the battlefield, reaping weak foes, or becoming a master of weapons.
:
![Marskman](https://arknights.wiki.gg/images/8/8f/Sniper.png?a673ec&20240920032458) {width:100px,margin-left:0cm,margin-right:0.2cm,wrapRight} **Marskman.** Blow away enemies at range, drowning them in a hail of shots or burying them with a single decisive strike. 
:
You are specialised in engaging air units, wielding explosives, piercing thick defences, sniping priority targets, using Arts to fling melee weapons, employing heavily over-tuned weapons, amplifying your attacks with Arts, and weapons that fire in a spread.
:
![Medic](https://arknights.wiki.gg/images/2/26/Medic.png?4b1da3&20240920032444) {width:100px,margin-left:0cm,margin-right:0.2cm,wrapRight} **Medic.** You heal your allies and save lives, pulling the dying and eventually even the dead back to the realm of the living.
:
You are specialised in healing singular targets for large amounts, applying special effects with your healing, triaging the wounded, or shielding your allies from harm.
::
![Specialist](https://arknights.wiki.gg/images/0/06/Specialist.png?f39e27&20240920032510) {width:100px,margin-left:0cm,margin-right:0.2cm,wrapRight} **Specialist.** A master of a single trade or concept, possessing a singular focus, you are unmatched within your chosen field.
:
Your chosen field is one of the following: cloak and dagger work for protection or assassination, moving your enemies against their will, experimenting with unstable drugs, inventing makeshift tools and weapon modifications, taking to the air with a jetpack, confounding enemies with a living doll, or setting traps for enemies to blunder into.
:
![Supporter](https://arknights.wiki.gg/images/b/b6/Supporter.png?9657c0&20240920032537) {width:100px,margin-left:0cm,margin-right:0.2cm,wrapRight} **Supporter.** You prop your allies from the backlines through magic or technology, reaching greater heights together. 
:
You do this through specialising in creating useful machines for your allies, through combining Arts and music, cursing your foes, engaging in grand esoteric rituals, and summoing constructs to fights alongside you.
:
![Vanguard](https://arknights.wiki.gg/images/f/fd/Vanguard.png?564eb3&20240920032549) {width:100px,margin-left:0cm,margin-right:0.2cm,wrapRight} 
<br>**Vanguard.** Range ahead of other warriors, fighting at your pace and on your terms. 
:
You provide tactical flexibility to your team through striking from the shadows at priority targets, smashing the foe before they can react, disrupting the formation of your enemy, inspiring allies and frightening enemies with your battle standard, and employing advanced tactics to dictate the battle. 

}}
\page
{{pageNumber,auto}}

{{wide

## Caster
A smirk comes across the girl's face as the smoke clears. The corridor before her is filled with charred men and scorched armour, the remnants of her Arts still smouldering in the air like embers. One of the Power Armoured soldiers rises to their feet, pointing his weapon her way; her smirk grows yet wider.
:
Across the world, a feline steps into her hair salon alongside a new client. With a spring in her step, she gets the client sat down; her tools and supplies floating towards her, hovering in an orbit around her. 
:
Further still, a Durin's hands shake with concentration, her fingers locked in a white-knuckle grip on her staff. In front of her, a gigantic scrap construct dances to the beat of music, puppeted by invisible strings.  


{{tocExcludeH3 
### Exceptional to a Man
}}

Not anyone can be a Caster. While most people on Terra are capable of using Arts to some extent, the ability to use Arts alone does not make you a Caster; Casters are defined by their exceptional ability with Arts. In military organisations, one must be combat-effective with just the strength of their Arts to be considered a Caster. 
:
Those that become Casters often have trodden their own path to get there. Some are naturally talented in the extreme, some require intense study and training to develop their skills; most are somewhere in between. As a result, Casters tend to be exceptional people; people with drive, and a story to tell, or perhaps one yet to be written.
}}

{{tocExcludeH3 
### Class Features
}}

As a Caster, you gain the following Class Features:

#### Hit Points
**Hit Dice:** 1d6 per Caster Level<br>
**Hit Points at 1st Level:** 6 + your Constitution Modifier<br>
**Hit Points at Higher Levels:** 1d6 (or 4) + your Constitution Modifier per Caster Level after 1st

#### Proficiencies
**Armor:** Light Shields<br>
**Weapons:** Light Melee <br>
**Tools:** A Tool Kit/Vehicle Proficiency of your Choice<br>
**Saving Throws:** Choose two from the following: Intelligence, Wisdom, Charisma <br>
**Skills:** Three from: Arts, Lore, Investigation, Tech, Insight, Perception

#### Equipment
You start with the following Equipment, in addition to any Equipment granted by your Background. 

- **A)** 1x Light Melee Weapon OR **B)** 1x Light Shield
- **A)** 1x Arts Wand OR **B)** 1x Arts Staff
- 1x Tool Kit that you are Proficient with

OR

- 1300 LMD

{{align-text:left
#### 1st Level: Caster Branch
You choose a Caster Branch which most closely fits the style you envision for yourself. Choose one from the following list; further details on these Branches can be found at the end of the Class Description. The Branch you choose grants you Features at Level 1, 3, 6, 10, and 15.
- **Core:** Core Casters cast a wide net, exchanging specialisation for versatility. They are jack of all trades, but masters of none.
- **Chain:** Chain Casters bounce their Arts from target to target, turning one target into several. Woe betide foes clustered together. 
- **Mech-Accord:** By combining their Arts with technology, these Casters can become a one man army.
- **Mystic:** All Casters are explosive in their force; Mystic Casters take this to its extreme, dealing devastating damage to one target. 
- **Primal:** The strength of a Primal Caster lingers in the air, continuing to harm long after the Arts have struck; Primal Casters excel at causing Status Effects.
- **Splash:** Splash Casters are the worst nightmare of massed infantry, dealing horrific damage over large areas.
}}


\column

#### 1st Level: Arts Casting
You can cast Arts in a way most Terrans cannot. 
:
***Arts Pool:*** Choose one of the Standard Arts Pools; you learn all of the Arts in that Pool, and they are always Prepared.
:
***Arts Points:*** The Caster Features table shows how many Arts Points (AP) you have to cast Arts of Level 1 or higher. You regain all expended Arts Points when you finish a Long Rest.
:
***Maximum Arts Level:*** The Caster Features table shows the maximum Level you can cast an Art at, based on your Caster Level. If an Art's Level is above your current Maximum Art Level, you cannot cast it, even if it is Prepared. 
:
***Spellcasting Ability:*** When you gain this Feature, choose either Intelligence, Wisdom, or Charisma; that is your Spellcasting Ability.

#### 2nd Level: Arts Detection
Your studies as a Caster have left you sensitive to the traces Arts leave behind. You learn the Detect Magic Art; it is always Prepared, and you may cast it without expending any Arts Points.

#### 2nd Level: Basic Operator Skill
You learn a Basic Operator Skill from the Basic Caster Skills table, or from any other Basic Skill table available to you.

#### 3rd Level: Basic Talent
You gain a Basic Talent of your choice from the Basic Caster Talents table. When you reach 8th Level, you may either pick a different Basic Talent to gain, or upgrade your Basic Talent, gaining its Module Effect.

#### 4th Level: Ability Score Improvement
When you reach 4th Level, and again at 6th, 8th, 10th, 12th, 16th, and 19th Level, you can increase one Ability Score of your choice by 2, or you can increase two Ability Scores of your choice by 1. As normal, you can't increase an Ability Score above 20 using this feature.

#### 5th Level: Anti-Arts Training
Your training as a Caster allows you to fend off hostile Arts as well as manifest your own.
:
You learn the Counterspell and Dispel Magic Arts, and they are always Prepared.

#### 5th Level: Growing Power
As you strength increases, you can manifest Arts that once strained you with increasing ease. When you cast an Art at a lower level than your Maximum Art Level, that Art costs 1 fewer Arts Point, to a minimum of 0. Beginning at 11th Level, those Arts instead cost 2 fewer Arts Points.

\page
{{pageNumber,auto}}

{{wide

#### 7th Level: Advanced Operator Skill
You learn an Advanced Operator Skill from the Advanced Caster Skills table, or from any other Advanced Skill table available to you.

#### 9th Level: Advanced Talent
You gain an Advanced Talent of your choice from the Advanced Caster Talents table. When you reach 13th Level, you may either pick a different Advanced Talent to gain, or upgrade your Advanced Talent, gaining its Module Effect.
:
#### 11th Level: Additional Arts Pool
You have mastered enough about Arts to branch out. Choose an Arts Pool from the Standard Arts Pools that you not chosen previously; you learn all of the Arts in that Pool, and they are always Prepared.
:
#### 14th Level: Elite Operator Skill
You learn an Elite Operator Skill from the Elite Caster Skills table, or from any other Elite Skill table available to you.
:
#### 18th Level: Unending
Whenever you spent Arts Points, roll 1d6. If the number you roll is equal to the number expended, the Arts Points you spent are refunded.
:
#### 20th Level: Doublecast
Your potency as a Caster is the thing of legend, your name one for the history books. 
:
Once per Short Rest, when you cast an Art with a Casting Time of an Action, you may immediately cast a second Art as part of the same Action. This second Art ignores restrictions on casting multiple levelled Arts during a single Turn.
}}

For this casting only, that Art is considered to require Concentration. At the beginning of each of your Turns, the effects of that Art are reapplied to the same location as if you had cast it again; Damage dealt by these secondary “casts” ignore Resistances. ### Core
Versatility is the name of the game for Core Casters. Whereas other Casters are specialists, focusing in on a particular aspect of Arts, Core Casters choose to spread a wider net. While they make sacrifices for this versatility, do not mistake this for weakness; a Core Caster is not a master of any one field, but they are certainly no novice.

#### 1st Level: Versatile Caster
You are already more versatile than most Casters. You gain the following benefits:

**Additional Arts Pool.** Choose an Arts Pool from the Standard Arts Pools that you have not chosen previously; you learn all of the Arts in that Pool, and they are always Prepared.

**Rabbit from the Hat.** As an Action once per Long Rest, you may choose an Art from any Standard Arts Pool. The Art must be of equal or lower Level than your Maximum Art Level. Until you complete a Long Rest, you may cast that Art as if it was part of one of your known Arts Pools.


\column

#### 3rd Level: Power from Within
You can meditate as a Short Rest Activity, as if you were casting an Art as a Ritual. At the end of this meditation, you regain expended Arts Points equal to half your Caster Level, rounding up. 
:
Once you have used this Feature, you cannot do so again until you finish a Long Rest.

#### 6th Level: Empowered Arts

Once per Turn, when you roll to determine Damage dealt by an Art you cast this Turn, you can cause that Art to deal additional Damage equal to your Proficiency Bonus. If it was a Levelled Art, it also deals additional Damage equal to your Spellcasting Ability Modifier. 

#### 10th Level: Master of the Elements
Your expertise with your chosen Arts grants you the following benefits:

**Elemental Being.** You gain Resistance to the Damage Type associated with your chosen Standard Arts Pools, as shown in the table below. 

**Maverick.** When you cast an Art using Arts Points, you may replace any Damage Types on that Art with one of the Damage Types associated with one of your chosen Standard Arts Pools.

| Arts Pool | Damage Type |
|:---------:|:-----:|
| Corrosion | Acid |
| Crude | Force |
| Earth | Piercing |
| Electric | Lightning |
| Flame | Fire |
| Fluid | Bludgeoning |
| Frost | Cold |
| Light | Radiant |
| Psychic | Psychic |
| Spatial | Thunder |
| Wind | Slashing |
:
#### 15th Level: Savant
Your Arts are like your signature, signed in carnage. 
:
You can conduct a ritual as a Long Rest Activity to choose a Signature Art from your known Arts of 5th Level or less. Your Signature Art gains the following benefits:

- You can reroll any result on a dice of 3 or less on a Damage Roll, but must use the new result.
- Damage dealt by that Art ignores Damage Reduction and Resistance.
- If the Art cannot deal Damage, it costs 2 fewer Arts Points to cast, to a minimum of 0.

{{align-text:left
You may only have one Signature Art at a time; when you select another, the previous Signature Art loses all benefits.
}}

{{imageMaskCorner20,--offsetX:-46%,--offsetY:-38%,--rotation:0
  <img 
  src='https://arknights.wiki.gg/images/f/f2/Ho%27olheyak_Skin_1.png' 
  style='position:absolute;top:300px;right:150px;width:1000px;;' />
}}

### Chain

The strength of a Chain Caster is in the name; your Arts flash from target to target in an instant, turning one target into several. Unlike some of your Caster brethren, however, you understand that Arts are not just a force for destruction. In more subtle applications, they can empower and uplift allies just as easily as they can smite foes.
:
#### 1st Level: Chain Art
When you cast an Art that can only affect a single Creature, you can cause that Art to chain to up to two other Creatures within 15 feet of the original Target; these additional Creatures are also considered to be targets of that Art.
:
You can use this Feature a number of times equal to your Proficiency Bonus, and regain all expended uses when you complete a Long Rest.

{{note
##### Arts that can only affect a single Creature
Many Features in this Branch refer to Arts that only affect a single Creature. For these purposes, this excludes any Art that could do one of the following things to multiple Creatures as a result of being cast once:
- Deal Damage
- Restore Hit Points
- Grant Temporary Hit Points
- Apply a positive or negative effect
}}

#### 3rd Level: Subtle Spark
The same talent that allows you to chain your Arts allows you to leave behind subtle marks on friend and foe alike. When you cast an Art that can only affect a single Creature, you can cause that Art to apply an additional effect (detailed below) to all Creatures it affects; these additional effects consume Sparks.
:
**Sparks:** You have 6 Sparks, which you expend to apply additional effects. You recover all expended Sparks whenever you finish a Long Rest.
:
You gain 2 additional Sparks at 7th Level (Total of 8), 11th Level (Total of 10), and 14th Level (Total of 12).
:
**Saving Throws:** Some additional effects require their targets to make a Saving Throw to resist its effects. The Saving Throw DC is the same as your Spell Save DC, unless stated otherwise.
:


\column

#### 6th Level: Ouroboros
Some would assume that your expertise does not extend to singular foes. They would be wrong, as your Arts can chain upon themselves.
:
When you use your Chain Art Feature, instead of causing the Art to chain to additional Creatures, you may instead give the target Creature Disadvantage on Saving Throws against that Art this Turn. 

#### 10th Level: Subtle Spark Upgraded
:
The application of your Sparks is as subtle as ever; the effects anything but.
:
The effects of your Subtle Spark Feature are improved; when you apply an additional effect using your Subtle Spark Feature, you may instead apply the upgraded effect. 
:
#### 15th Level: Great Chain of Being
:
Your Chain Arts are engraved into your very being, and you manifest them effortlessly. 
:
When you use your Chain Art Feature, you may chain to one additional Creature (for a total of 3). Furthermore, whenever you complete a Short Rest, you regain 1 expended use of your Chain Art Feature.

  <img 
  src='https://arknights.wiki.gg/images/8/8f/Leizi.png' 
  style='position:absolute;top:510px;right:-60px;width:500px;;' />

\page
{{pageNumber,auto}}

<img 
  src='https://arknights.wiki.gg/images/9/99/Passenger.png' 
  style='position:absolute;top:70px;right:280px;width:900px' />

\column

#### Additional Effects
The Additional Effects applied by your Subtle Spark Feature are listed in alphabetical order.
:
##### Accelerate (1 Spark)
The Movement Speed of affected Creatures is increased by 10ft until the beginning of your next Turn.
:
**Upgrade:** Until the start of your next Turn, when an affected Creature makes a Dexterity Saving Throw, they may roll 1d4 and add it to the result of the roll.
:
##### Bolster Defences (1 Spark)
Affected Creatures gain 2 DR against all Damage until the beginning of your next Turn.
:
**Upgrade:** The DR gained from this effect is increased to 4.
:
##### Decelerate (1 Spark)
The Affected Creatures must succeed on a Constitution Saving Throw against your Spell Save DC, or their Movement Speed is reduced by 10 until the start of your next Turn.
:
**Upgrade:** Until the start of your next Turn, when a Creature that failed the Constitution Saving Throw makes a Dexterity Saving Throw, they must roll 1d4 and subtract it from the result of the roll.
:
##### Enervate (1 Spark)
The Affected Creatures must succeed on a Constitution Saving Throw against your Spell Save DC, or suffer a -1 penalty to all Attack and Damage Rolls until the beginning of your next Turn.
:
**Upgrade:** Instead of the -1 penalty; until the start of your next Turn, when a Creature that failed the Constitution Saving Throw makes an Attack or Damage Roll, they roll 1d4 and subtract that from the result.
:
##### Innervate (1 Spark)
Affected Creatures gain a +1 bonus to their Attack and Damage Rolls until the beginning of your next Turn.
:
**Upgrade:** Instead of the +1 bonus, affected Creatures may roll 1d4 whenever they make an Attack or Damage Roll, and add it to the result.
:
##### Shred Defences (1 Spark)
The Affected Creatures must succeed on a Constitution Saving Throw against your Spell Save DC, or any DR they have is reduced by 2 by until the beginning of your next Turn.
:
**Upgrade:** The DR reduction from this effect is increased to 4.


\page
{{pageNumber,auto}}

### Mech-Accord

In the world of Terra, technology and magic is practically indistinguishable. Most technology functions on Originium, and making Originium function requires a very basic grasp of Arts. Mech-Accord Casters take this to its ultimate conclusion. 

{{note
##### Equipment and Proficiency Exchange
When you choose this Branch, you may exchange any Tool Kit or Vehicle Proficiency you have for Proficiency with the Engineering Kit.
:
Additionally, when you choose this Branch, you may exchange one Toolkit from your Starting Equipment for an Engineering Kit.
}}



\column

#### 1st Level: Indistinguishable from Magic
 You have created a drone known as an Arts Master, a flying construct operated by, and capable of chanelling, your Arts. The Arts Master is not sentient, but is considered allied to you and anyone allied to you. You can only have one Arts Master at a time.

**The Arts Master in Combat.** In Combat, the Arts Master acts during your Turn. It can use Reactions on its own, but it will not move or take Actions unless you use your Bonus Action to command it to non-verbally either move to a location of your choice, or take one Action in its Stat Block. To command your Arts Master in this way, you must be within 100 feet of it.

**Restoring or Replacing the Arts Master.** As a Construct, the Arts Master cannot regain Hit Points in most ways (See Page 9). 
:
If an Arts Master is reduced to 0 Hit Points, it is destroyed. You can rebuild a destroyed Arts Master as a Long Rest Activity if you retain the destroyed Arts Master's components. If they are not retrieved, replacing the components costs 500 LMD. 

:
{{monster,frame,
## Arts Master Drone
*Small Construct (Drone), Neutral Alignment*
___
**Armor Class** :: 17
**Hit Points**  :: 25 + your Technology Modifier
**Speed**       :: 30ft Hover.
___
|  STR  |  DEX  |  CON  |  INT  |  WIS  |  CHA  |
|:-----:|:-----:|:-----:|:-----:|:-----:|:-----:|

**Condition Immunities** :: Charmed, Frightened, Poisoned, Necrotised, Nervous Impairment
**Senses**               :: Darkvision 60 ft., passive Perception 6
**Languages**            :: -
**Challenge**            :: None (0 XP) {{bonus **Proficiency Bonus** Your PB}}
___

***Arts-Linked.*** The Drone's Creator can see what this Drone sees.

### Actions
***Originium Spark.*** *Ranged Weapon Attack:* Uses your Spell Attack Modifier, Range 30/100ft., one target. *Hit*  5 (2d4) + your Spellcasting Ability Modifier Force Damage.
:
### Reactions
***Wolfpack Doctrine.*** *Trigger:* This Arts Master's Creator casts an Art. *Response:* The Arts Master makes an Originium Spark Attack against a Creature it can see.

}}

**Technology Expertise.** You gain Proficiency in the Technology Skill; if you are already Proficient, you instead gain Expertise. Additionally, you gain Proficiency in Vehicles (Drones).


<img 
  src='https://arknights.wiki.gg/images/Goldenglow.png?bc79a8' 
  style='position:absolute;top:170px;right:130px;width:900px;clip-path:rect(auto auto auto auto);' />






\page
{{pageNumber,auto}}


#### 3rd Level: Jury Rigging

Your drones are so infused with your Arts that their metal can mold like putty in your hands.
:
As a Bonus Action, you can use your Arts to enact emergency repairs on one of your Arts Masters; it must be within 100 feet of you, and you must be able to see it. Expend an Amount of Arts Points up to twice your Proficiency Bonus, and roll 1d8 for each AP expended; that Arts Master recovers Hit Points equal to the total amount rolled, and gains any excess healing as Temporary Hit Points.


#### 6th Level: Synergetic Casting
You have refined the connection you form with your drones, allowing you to strike as one. You gain the following benefits:

**Arts Translocation.** When you cast an Art, you may cast it as if you were in the space of one of your Arts Master Drones, instead of your own. However, if you do, that Art costs 1 additional Arts Point.

**Cantrip Duplication.** Once per Turn, when you cast a Cantrip, you can expend 1 Arts Point to cast that Cantrip again as if you were in the space of one of your Arts Master Drones, targeting the same Creature or area. If you do so, that Arts Master Drone cannot make any Attacks until the beginning of your next Turn.

\column

#### 10th Level: Army of One
Your mastery of Arts Master drones now allows you to control more than one. You gain the following benefits:

**Additional Arts Drone.** The maximum number of Arts Master Drones you can have at one time is increased to 2. When you use your Bonus Action to command your Arts Master, you may command all of your Arts Master's at once.

**Residual Energy Recyclers.** When you cast a levelled Art, until the end of your Turn, your Arts Master Drones deal additional Force Damage with their Originium Spark Action equal to the number of Arts Points you expended to cast that Art. 
:
#### 15th Level: Death from Above
Your drones are now capable of manifesting extremely potent Arts on your behalf.
:
When you cast an Art of 3rd Level or less as an Action, you can immediately use your Bonus Action to cast that Art again as if you were in the space of one of your Arts Master Drones.

{{imageMaskEdge8,--offset:-0%,--rotation:-12
<img 
  src='https://arknights.wiki.gg/images/e/e3/Minimalist_Elite_2.png' 
  style='position:absolute;top:100px;right:-300px;width:1300px' />
}}












\page
{{pageNumber,auto}}

### Mystic
:
Mystic Casters are patient hunters, storing up their strength for a singular devastating burst. They lull their foe into a false approximation of their strength, before blowing away such preconcieved notions in a singular, painful moment. 

\column



#### 1st Level: Mystic Charge 
You learn to store your arcane strength for a singular moment. 
:
At the end of your Turn, if you have spent Arts Points this Turn, but have not expended any Mystic Charge this Turn, you gain 1 Mystic Charge. 

Alternatively, you may conduct a minute long Ritual to generate Mystic Charge. You must remain stationary for the duration, and it is treated as if you were concentrating on a Spell. At the end of the Ritual, you may expend up to 3 Arts Points; you gain 1 Mystic Charge for each expended, up to your maximum Mystic Charge.

The maximum amount of Mystic Charge you can have simultaneously is 2. You lose all Mystic Charge upon finishing a Long Rest. 

:
While you have any Mystic Charges, you can use your Action to expend all of your Mystic Charges. When you do so, you may then cast a Cantrip you know with a Casting Time of Action as part of this Action. Then, you may cast that Cantrip again for each Mystic Charge expended, also as part of this Action. Cantrips cast using this Feature cannot score a Critical Hit.

#### 3rd Level: Potent Cantrip

When you cast a Cantrip and you miss a Creature with the Attack Roll, or a Creature succeeds on a Saving Throw against that Cantrip, that Creature still takes half of the Cantrip’s Damage, but suffers no additional effects.

#### 6th Level: Power Surge
Your strength is further concentrated into a single moment of power. 
:
You gain a +1 Bonus to your Spell Attack Rolls and Spell Save DC for Cantrips for each Mystic Charge you have expended during the current Turn.

#### 10th Level: Turbocharger
Familiarity with your unconventional delivery of Arts has allowed you to "warm up" faster. You gain the following benefits: 

**Maximum Mystic Charge.** Your maximum Mystic Charge is increased to 3.

**Quick Charge.** Whenever you roll Initiative, you gain 1 Mystic Charge. 

#### 15th Level: Feedback Loop
You have become so skilled with storing your own power that you can do so even as you expend it. 
:
When you cast an Art using Arts Points, you gain 1 Mystic Charge. If that Art was of 6th Level or higher, you gain 1 additional Mystic Charge.


{{imageMaskEdge1,--offset:3%,--rotation:90
<img 
  src='https://arknights.wiki.gg/images/3/35/Ebenholz.png' 
  style='position:absolute;top:170px;right:140px;width:900px;clip-path:rect(auto auto 95% auto);' />
}}



\page
{{pageNumber,auto}}

### Primal
The might of a Primal Caster's Arts lingers, like an infection in a wound. As a Primal Caster, you excel in debilitating and wounding your foes, leaving them scrambling and confused. Then, when their guard is down, you strike like a wild beast; going straight for the throat.
:
#### 1st Level: Residual Suffering
You can cause your Arts to not immediately dissipate, causing lasting harm.
:
When you hit a Creature with a Spell Attack, or a Creature fails a Saving Throw against an Art cast by you, you may cause that Creature (no Action required) to suffer a Status Condition until the end of your next Turn, based on the Damage Type(s) dealt by that Art. If there are multiple Damage Types dealt, you may choose one effect to apply
.

| Damage Type | Condition          | 
|:-----------:|:------------------:|
|Acid         | Corroded           |
|Bludgeoning  | Frightened         |
|Cold         | Frozen             |
|Fire         | Burning            |
|Force        | Blinded            |
|Lightning    | Nervous Impairment |
|Necrotic     | Necrotised         |
|Piercing     | Bleeding           |
|Psychic      | Charmed            |
|Radiant      | Blinded            |
|Slashing     | Bleeding           |
|Thunder      | Frightened         |
:

You can use this Feature a number of times equal to twice your Proficiency Bonus, and regain all expended uses upon finishing a Long Rest.
:
#### 3rd Level: Debilitate and Destroy
You excel at crushing enemies who are already reeling from your assault.
:
When you deal Damage to a Creature that has a negative Status Condition, that Creature loses 1d8 Hit Points. You can only deal this Damage to each Creature once per Turn.
:
This Damage is increased to 2d8 at Level 7, and 3d8 at Level 14.

\column

#### 6th Level: Elemental Burst
You learn to inflict indirect harm without committing to a larger Art.
:
As a Bonus Action during your Turn; choose a point you can see within 100 feet, and a Condition listed in your Residual Suffering Feature. All Creatures within a 20-foot-radius Sphere centred on that point must succeed on a Dexterity Saving Throw against your Spell Save DC or suffer the chosen Condition until the end of your next Turn.
:
Once you have used this Feature, you must complete a Short Rest before you may do so again.


<img 
  src='https://arknights.wiki.gg/images/Nymph.png?de28b4' 
  style='position:absolute;top:300px;right:-230px;width:700px;clip-path:rect(auto auto auto 7%);' />





\page
{{pageNumber,auto}}

{{wide
#### 10th Level: Enduring Affliction
Status Conditions inflicted by you are more potent. When you inflict any of the following Conditions using your Residual Suffering or Elemental Burst Features, the corresponding effect is added to that Condition.
:
| Condition         | Additional Effect | 
|:-----------------:|:-----------------:|
|Bleeding           | The dice is increased one step to d8, from d6. When the Condition is removed, the Creature loses 2d8 Hit Points.
|Blinded            | The Creature glows with 10ft of Blinding Light, cannot become Invisible, and cannot take the Hide Action.
|Burning            | The Creature takes 1d8 additional Fire Damage, and cannot gain Hit Points. 
|Charmed            | The Creature's Speed is reduced to 0.
|Corroded           | The Creature's DR is set to 0. 
|Frightened         | The Creature has Disadvantage on Attack Rolls.
|Frozen             | The Creature automatically fails Dexterity Saving Throws.
|Necrotised         | The Creature cannot take Reactions.
|Nervous Impairment | The Creature loses 3 Hit Points the first time it moves each Turn, takes an Action, or takes a Bonus Action.
:
#### 15th Level: Cardinal Punishment
None can evade your primeval fury. You gain the following benefits:

**Improved Elemental Burst.** When you use your Elemental Burst Feature, your Spell Save DC is treated as being 5 higher. 

**Suffering Unmatched.** You may use your Residual Suffering Feature even against Creatures who succeed on a Saving Throw against your Arts. 


}}

{{imageMaskEdge1,--offset:-8%,--rotation:0
<img 
  src='https://arknights.wiki.gg/images/Blaze_the_Igniting_Spark_Elite_2.png?9a79ee' 
  style='position:absolute;top:170px;right:-90px;width:1000px;clip-path:rect(20% auto auto auto);' />
}}


\page
{{pageNumber,auto}}

{{wide

### Splash
:
Precision is never the goal of a Splash Caster. Their goal is to wield their power unrestrained and uncontrolled, lashing their foes with unshackled Arts. Or at least, that is how it appears; in reality, the Arts of a Splash Caster are tightly controlled and refined to inflict as much damage as possible.
:
#### 1st Level: Controlled Detonation
Your area-of-effect Arts are more difficult to evade.
:
You gain a +2 Bonus to your Spell Save DC when casting Arts that target an area.

{{note,wide
##### Arts that Target an Area
Many Features in this Branch refer to Arts that target an Area. For these purposes, this is any Art that targets a Sphere, Cone, Cube, Cylinder, or Line.
}}

#### 3rd Level: Splashing Cantrips
When you would deal Damage to only one Creature with a Damaging Cantrip, you may choose to halve the Damage dealt by that Cantrip. If you do, all Creatures within 10 feet of the original target must succeed on a Dexterity Saving Throw against your Spell Save DC, or take the halved Damage of that Cantrip. This is considered to be casting an Art that targets an Area for the purpose of your Controlled Detonation Feature.
:
If you are within the area of your Splashing Cantrip, you automatically pass the Saving Throw.
:
#### 6th Level: Sculpt Arts
You can better control the area of your Arts; to shield Creatures of your choice from the worst fury of your Arts, or to expose them to it. You gain the following benefitsL 

**Safe Zone.** When you cast an Art that targets an area, you can choose a number of Creatures in that area equal to 1 plus the Level the Art was cast at. The chosen Creatures automatically succeed on their Saving Throws against the Art, and they take no Damage if they would normally take half Damage on a successful Save.

**Overwhelming Detonation.** You can increase the area that your Arts affect. When you cast an Art that targets an Area, you can expend a number of additional Arts Points up to twice your Proficiency Bonus to increase the size of the affected area for each Arts Point expended. How an Art is affected depends on the area it affects: 
- **Cone:** The length of the Cone is increased by 10 feet for every additional Arts Point expended.
- **Cube:** The side length of the Cube is increased by 10 feet for every additional Arts Point expended.
- **Line:** The length of the line is increased by 20 feet for every additional Arts Point expended.
- **Sphere:** The radius of the Sphere is increased by 5 feet for every additional Arts Point expended.

#### 10th Level: Implements of Destruction
Your mastery of area-of-effect Arts is such that you can manifest them even from unfamiliar Arts Pools. You gain the following benefits:

**Additional Arts.** You learn two Arts of your choice from any of the Standard Arts Pools. They do not have to be from the same Arts Pool, but they all must target an area. These Arts are always Prepared.

**Economics of Violence.** Whenever you cast an Art of 5th Level or lower that targets an area, that Art costs one fewer Arts Point, to a minimum of 1. This reduction is applied after the reduction from your Growing Power Feature.

}}

{{imageMaskEdge8,--offset:7%,--rotation:0

}}





\page
{{pageNumber,auto}}

#### 15th Level: Unavoidable
Your area-of-effect Arts are so potent that few can ever hope to avoid them. You gain the following benefits:

**All-Consuming.** Once per Short Rest, when you cast an Art that targets an area, you can cause all Creatures affected to have Disadvantage on their Saving Throws against that Art this Turn.

**Maximum Burst.** Creatures (except Creatures immune to that Art) cannot take less than half of the rolled Damage from your Arts that target an Area, even if they are Resistant or have an Ability that allows them to reduce the Damage (such as Evasion), or have Damage Reduction.

  style='position:absolute;top:-160px;right:-300px;width:1700px' />
}}

## Defender

With a deafening roar, the planted explosives collapse the ceiling inwards. Without pause, the green-haired Oni leaps forward, her shield the only protection between the crushing weight and her comrade. When the dust settles, she remains, half-dead; her ally unharmed beneath her. 
:
Calmly, methodically, the Vouivre pushes forward. The pistol in her hand barks twice, and another civilian, rendered mindless by Originium mutation, collapses in a heap. The next comes forward, slamming its body weight against her shield. She staggers but does not fall, channelling her Arts through the special casting unit in her shield. With a faint smell of ozone, electricity arcs from her shield, launching the insensate mutant away. She raises her pistol again; her aim does not falter.


{{tocExcludeH3 
### The Hammer and The Anvil
The title of "Defender" invokes a particular image; heavy-set, heavy-clad warriors bearing massive shields, and bearing the brunt of the dangerous attacks. While this is indeed true for many Defenders, the spectrum of those who call themselves by that name is far too vast to be encompassed by such a simple image. Defenders are not just found in the front lines, meeting foes arm-against-arm, being found anywhere something requires protection. As for what that something may be, and how they defend it, is a matter of personal consideration.
}}

:
{{tocExcludeH3 
### Class Features
}}

As a Defender, you gain the following Class Features:

#### Hit Points
**Hit Dice:** 1d12 per Defender Level<br>
**Hit Points at 1st Level:** 12 + your Constitution Modifier<br>
**Hit Points at Higher Levels:** 1d12 (or 7) + your Constitution Modifier per Defender Level after 1st

#### Proficiencies
**Armor:** All Armour, All Shields<br>
**Weapons:** All Melee Weapons<br>
**Tools:** A Tool Kit/Vehicle Proficiency of your Choice<br>
**Saving Throws:** Constitution + one of your choice from the following: Strength, Wisdom<br>
**Skills:** Three from: Athletics, Lore, Tech, Perception, Intimidation, Wilderness

\column

#### Equipment
You start with the following Equipment, in addition to any Equipment granted by your Background. 

- **A)** 1x Light Melee Weapon OR **B)** 1x Light Shield
- **A)** 1x Heavy Melee Weapon OR **B)** 1x Light Shield OR **C)** 1x Heavy Shield
- **A)** Combat Gear OR **B)** Defender Armour
- 1x Tool Kit you are Proficient with.

OR

- 1300 LMD

##### The Defender

| 12th  |  +4  | Ability Score Improvement, Branch Feature    | 6
| 13th  |  +5  | Advanced Talent Module, Reactive             | 6
| 14th  |  +5  | Elite 2- Elite Operator Skill                | 8
| 15th  |  +5  | Branch Feature                               | 8
| 16th  |  +5  | Ability Score Improvement                    | 8
| 17th  |  +6  | Stand Behind Me                              | 8
| 18th  |  +6  | Indestructible                               | 8
| 19th  |  +6  | Ability Score Improvement                    | 8
| 20th  |  +6  | Adamantine Defence                           | 10
}}



\page
{{pageNumber,auto}}

{{wide

#### 1st Level: Defender Branch

You choose a Branch that most closely fits the style you envision for yourself. Choose one from the following list; further details on these Branches can be found at the end of the Class Description. The Branch you choose grants you Features at Level 1, 4, 9, 12, and 15.

- **Artshield:** Artshields take a stand against Casters, the typical enemy of a Defender, by wielding their own power against them.
- **Dreadnought:** Dreadnoughts are mighty and implacable, wielding their considerable fortitude as a hammer to smash the defences of the mightiest foes in single combat.
- **Fortress:** Hammering their foe and supporting their allies with artillery from a fortified position, these warriors are an anchor around which entire battle-plans can be built.
- **Guardian:** Wielding Healing Arts alongside their shields, Guardians aim to both prevent and cure harm that comes to their allies.
- **Juggernaut:** As the name may suggest, a Juggernaut is an unstoppable force that plows through lesser foes. They need no shield, reinforcing their defences with unique Arts, and freeing themselves to use heavier weapons.
- **Protector:** Archetypal Defenders, Protectors seek to draw the attention of the enemy away from their allies and onto themselves, and then render the attacker defenceless for their allies' counterblow. 
- **Sentry:** Preferring to attack at range, the Sentry combines long range firepower with their own defences, without sacrificing mobility.

#### 1st Level: Resilience
As a Defender, you can draw upon your innate fortitude to act quickly when it is most required. You have an amount of Resilience shown in the Resilience column of the Defender Features Table, and recover all expended Resilience when you finish a Long Rest. 
:
Resilience is used by your other Class Features and Branch Features, as directed by those Features, to improve your Action Economy or imbue additional effects.

#### 1st Level: Shelter
Your calling is defence, and so defend you shall. As an Action during your Turn, you may take the Shelter Action. Until the beginning of your next Turn, Attacks against allied Creatures (except yourself) within 5 feet of you are made at Disadvantage.
:
If you expend 1 Resilience, you may instead take the Shelter Action as a Bonus Action. 

}}

<img 
  src='https://arknights.wiki.gg/images/c/c4/Guerrilla_Shieldguard.png?483a31&20230927003229' 
  style='position:absolute;top:600px;right:400px;width:400px' />

\column
:

#### 2nd Level: Basic Operator Skill
You learn a Basic Operator Skill from the Basic Defender Skills table, or from any other Basic Skill table available to you.

#### 3rd Level: Basic Talent
You gain a Basic Talent of your choice from the Basic Defender Talents table. When you reach 8th Level, you may either pick a different Basic Talent to gain, or upgrade your Basic Talent, gaining its Module Effect.

#### 4th Level: Ability Score Improvement
When you reach 4th level, and again at 6th, 8th, 10th, 12th, 16th, and 19th level, you can increase one Ability Score of your choice by 2, or you can increase two Ability Scores of your choice by 1. As normal, you can’t increase an Ability Score above 20 using this feature.

#### 5th Level: Extra Attack
When you take the Attack Action, you can make an additional Attack as part of that Action.

\page
{{pageNumber,auto}}

{{wide

#### 7th Level: Advanced Operator Skill
You learn an Advanced Operator Skill from the Advanced Defender Skills table, or from any other Advanced Skill table available to you.

#### 9th Level: Advanced Talent
You gain an Advanced Talent of your choice from the Advanced Defender Talents table. When you reach 13th Level, you may either pick a different Advanced Talent to gain, or upgrade your Advanced Talent, gaining its Module Effect.

#### 11th Level: Leaping Defence
You may leap to the defence of your allies. When a allied Creature you can see within a distance equal to half your Speed is hit by an Attack, you may move to an unoccupied space within 5 feet of that allied Creature as a Reaction; this movement does not provoke Opportunity Attacks. If you do so, you instead become the target of that Attack. and that Attack hits you regardless of your Armour Class.
:
After using this Feature, you may expend 1 Resilience to regain use of your Reaction.

#### 13th Level: Reactive
To better respond to threats, you must always be prepared. You may make two Reactions instead of only one, and regain use of both at the beginning of your Turn. You may still only use one Reaction in response to a given trigger.

#### 14th Level: Elite Operator Skill
You learn an Elite Operator Skill from the Elite Defender Skills table, or from any other Elite Skill table available to you.

#### 17th Level: Stand Behind Me
When you use your Leaping Defence Feature, the triggering Creature may move up to 15 feet; this movement does not Provoke Opportunity Attacks, but they must finish the movement further away from the Attacking Creature.

#### 18th Level: Indestructible
You know best how to wield your considerable defences. When you would take Damage as a result of a hostile action, you can use this Feature as a Reaction; double any DR you have against that instance of Damage.
:
Once you have used this Feature, you may not do so again until you finish a Short Rest, or expend 2 Resilience during your Turn (no Action required) to restore your use of it.

#### 20th Level: Adamantine Defence
Even a mortal blow cannot put you down. Any Critical Hit against you becomes a non-Critical Hit.
}}

  src='https://arknights.wiki.gg/images/1/1b/MN-Nearl_Sisters_Stand_Together.png?650b4c&20230926235359' 
  style='position:absolute;top:600px;right:0px;width:900px' />
}}

Once during each Creature's Turn, when a Creature makes an Attack against you, you can move half of your Speed towards that Creature and make a Melee Attack against them if possible (no Action required). 	| 3 Rounds


}}

| Tough	| Your Hit Point Maximum increases by an amount equal to twice your Character Level. When you gain a Character Level thereafter, your Hit Point Maximum increases by an additional two points.	| Whenever you expend a Hit Dice to restore your Hit Points, you recover additional Hit Points equal to your Proficiency Bonus.

### Artshield
An Artshield is a specialist in the field of combatting Casters. They combine heavy equipment and rigorous training to develop the necessary strength to weather the storm, augementing their significant strength with their own unique Arts. When hostile Arts threaten their allies, Artshields stand to take the brunt, before retailiating with disruptive strikes.

#### 1st Level: Against the Storm
You develop tactics for countering Arts Users, resisting their powers and employing their own strength against them. You gain the following benefits:

**Arts Resistance.** You have Advantage on Saving Throws made against Arts.

**Spellshield.** Creatures benefitting from your Shelter Feature have Advantage on Saving Throws against Arts you can see. Additionally, if a Creature benefitting from your Shelter Feature would take half Damage as a result of succeeding on a Saving Throw against an Art you can see, you can expend 2 Resilience (no Action required) to cause them to take no damage from that Art instead.


{{imageMaskEdge1,--offset:2%,--rotation:90
<img 
  src='https://arknights.wiki.gg/images/thumb/5/57/Dur-nar_Elite_2.png/1024px-Dur-nar_Elite_2.png?4c733c' 
  style='position:absolute;top:395px;right:0px;width:1100px;clip-path:rect(8% auto 91% auto);' />
}}

{{imageMaskEdge1,--offset:23%,--rotation:0
<img 
  src='https://arknights.wiki.gg/images/thumb/5/57/Dur-nar_Elite_2.png/1024px-Dur-nar_Elite_2.png?4c733c' 
  style='position:absolute;top:395px;right:0px;width:1100px;clip-path:rect(8% auto 91% auto);' />
}}







\column
#### 4th Level: Weapons of the Enemy
You have all but made the weapons of your enemy into your own. You gain the following benefits:

**Arts Absorption.** When you take Arts Damage, you may absorb some of the energy from that Art (no Action required). After you have absorbed energy in this way, you may expel it as an Action during your Turn. All Creatures within a 10ft Square originating from you must make a Dexterity Saving Throw against your Martial DC. On a failed Save, they take 3d6 Force Damage; on a successful Save, they take half as much Damage. 
:
This Damage is increased to 6d6 at Level 7, and 9d6 at Level 14.

**Empowered Strikes.** Your Melee Weapon Attacks deal an additional 1d6 Force Damage. While you have an active Operator Skill, your Melee Weapon Attacks instead deal an additional 2d6 Force Damage.


#### 9th Level: Oppressive Force
You have further refined your technique for shutting down hostile Casters; the best defence is a good offence. You gain the following benefits:

**Reactive Defence.** When a Unit within your Reach casts an Art, you may make an Attack of Opportunity against them.

**Concentration Breaker.** When you damage a Creature that is Concentrating on an Art, it has Disadvantage on the Saving Throw it makes to maintain Concentration.

#### 12th Level: Stormchaser
Your enduring enmity of enemy Casters leads you to the forefront, an elemental hell few but you can endure. You intend to change that. You gain the following benefits:

**Improved Arts Resistance.** You always have DR against Arts Damage equal to half of your DR against Physical Damage, rounding up.

**Leaping Ward.** You can now use your Leaping Defence Feature when a Creature within a distance equal to half your Speed would make a Saving Throw against an Art or Art-like effects. If you do so, both you and that allied Creature are considered to be Resistant to the Damage dealt by that Art this Turn.

\page
{{pageNumber,auto}}

#### 15th Level: Eye of the Storm
You have mastered your resistance to Arts so completely that you can nullify them utterly. 
:
As a Reaction, you may create a spherical barrier centred on you with a radius of up to 20 feet (your choice). This barrier is visible, appearing silvery and translucent, and lasts until the end of your next Turn. For the duration, all Creatures within the barrier cannot be affected by Arts, Art-like Effects, or take Arts Damage. If there are any Arts affecting a Creature when they enter the area, or when they begin their Turn within the area, those effects are ended.
:
Once you have used this Feature, you may not do so again until you have finished a Long Rest.

\column

{{note
##### Note: Art-like Effects
For the purpose of your Eye of the Storm Feature, an Art-Like effect encompasses abilities and effects that would be considered "magical" in nature for the purpose of rules, but are not actually Arts/Spells. While these Creatures do not exist within AK5e, a Vampire's Charm Ability or a Dragon's Breath Attack would be considered Art-Like effects. In this rulebook, these Abilities have (Art) in their name.
}}

{{imageMaskEdge1,--offset:1%,--rotation:270
<img 
  src='https://arknights.wiki.gg/images/Hoshiguma_the_Breacher_Elite_2.png?8e3a20' 
  style='position:absolute;top:-0px;right:-220px;width:1300px;' />
}}

{{imageMaskEdge1,--offset:-15%,--rotation:0
<img 
  src='https://arknights.wiki.gg/images/Hoshiguma_the_Breacher_Elite_2.png?8e3a20' 
  style='position:absolute;top:-0px;right:-220px;width:1300px;' />
}}
