### Dreadnought

Defenders treading the path of the Dreadnought seek always to bare their fangs at the strongest. For them, the fortitude they develop is not the goal, but merely a means for them to withstand enough punishment to reach their desired foe, and crush them mercilessly.

#### 1st Level: Dreadnought's Advance
You learn to assume a stance that best brings to bear your monstrous strength.
:
As a Bonus Action, you can enter a state designed to roll over enemies, known as the Advance. The Advance lasts for one minute, but ends early if your Turn ends and you have not made an Attack, dealt Damage, forced a Creature to make a Saving Throw, or taken Damage since your last Turn.
:
While the Advance is active, you gain the following benefits:
- When you make an Attack Roll or are Attacked, you gain 1 Skill Point. This is increased to 2 at 7th level, and 3 at 14th level.
- Your Melee Attacks deal additional Damage equal to your Proficiency Bonus.

{{align-text:left
Once you have entered the Advance, you may not do so again until you finish a Short Rest, or expend 2 Resilience (no Action required) during your Turn to restore your use of it.
}}

#### 4th Level: Crownsplitter
You can focus your strength even more than the average warrior, doling out blows that can crack concrete with regularity. 
:
While you have an Active Operator Skill, your Melee Attacks deal additional Damage equal to your Strength Modifier. If the target of that Melee Attack is an Elite or Leader Creature, this additional Damage is increased to twice your Strength Modifier. 

#### 9th Level: Wrecking Wall
Once per Long Rest as an Action, you can choose a Creature you can see within five feet of you; that Creature must make a Constitution Saving Throw against your Martial DC. On a failed Save, they take 10d6 Bludgeoning Damage and are Stunned; they may reattempt the Saving Throw at the end of each of their Turns, ending the Condition on a success. On a successful Save, they take half as much Damage, and are not Stunned. 
:
Damage from this Feature ignores Damage Reduction and Resistances.

\column

#### 12th Level: Sword of Damocles
You punish those powerful foes who would dare to ignore you. You gain the following benefits: 

**Leaping Offence.** When you use your Leaping Defence Feature, if the Attacking Creature is an Elite or Leader Creature, you can expend 1 Resilience to make an Attack of Opportunity against them after their Attack (no Action required).

**Tear them Down.** When an Elite or Leader Creature within your Reach makes an Attack against a Creature that is not you, or forces them to make a Saving Throw, you can make an Attack of Opportunity against them (no Action required). 

#### 15th Level: Legendary Might 
Your strength is the stuff of legend, and your blows carve themselves into history. Your Strength Score is increased by 6, to a maximum of 26.

{{imageMaskCorner27,--offsetX:45%,--offsetY:-30%,--rotation:0
<img 
  src='https://arknights.wiki.gg/images/a/a7/Aurora_Skin_1.png?83b6d7&20230927014839' 
  style='position:absolute;top:200px;right:-370px;height:1200;width:1300px;clip-path:rect(14% auto auto 39%);' />
}}














\page
{{pageNumber,auto}}

{{wide

### Fortress
Fortress Defenders turn the sluggish nature of Defenders into their strength, pounding their foes with explosives from a distance as they slowly advance. It is not uncommon for Defenders treading this path to be the core of their team, a hardpoint to rally around when hell breaks loose.

{{note
##### Equipment Exchange
When you choose this Branch, you may exchange 1x Light Melee Weapon or 1x Light Shield from your Starting Equipment for 1x Grenade Launcher.
}}



#### Level 1: Fire Support Platform
You are an anchor around which the team is based, laying down supporting fire from a secure position. 

**Additional Weapon Proficiencies.** You gain Proficiency with the Grenade Launcher and Rocket Launcher.

**Entrench.** At the beginning of your Turn, you can declare that you will Entrench at your current position until the beginning of your next Turn. While you are Entrenched, your speed becomes 0 and cannot be increased, but you gain the following benefits:

:: **Barrage.** When you take the Attack Action, you can ignore the Two-Handed and Brace Qualities for Grenade Launchers and Rocket Launchers for the duration of that Action.

:: **Danger Close.** Whenever you force other Creatures to make a Saving Throw, you can choose a number of those Creatures equal to your Proficiency Bonus; those Creatures automatically pass the Saving Throw, and they take no Damage if they would normally take half Damage on a successful Save.

#### Level 4: Fire for Effect
Specialised ammunition, combined with your Arts, allow you to apply a variety of different effects from the barrel of your launcher, called Munitions. These Munitions are detailed below under Munitions Options, and are fuelled by special dice called Munitions Dice.
:
**Munitions Dice:** You have five Munitions Dice, which are d4s. You regain all expended Munitions Dice when you finish a Long Rest. 
:
Your Munitions Dice becomes a d6 when you reach Defender Level 7, and a d8 when you reach Defender Level 14.
:
**Loading Munitions:** As an Action while you have a Grenade Launcher or Rocket Launcher equipped, you may load a Munition into it by expending 1 Munitions Dice; available Munitions can be found on the next page. If you also expend 1 Resilience, you may do so as a Bonus Action instead. Multiple Munitions can be loaded into the same Weapon, up to a maximum equal to your Proficiency Bonus, but the same Munition cannot be loaded multiple times. Any loaded Munitions are lost if you unequip that Weapon or become Incapacitated.
:
**Firing Munitions:** When you take the Attack Action while you have Munitions loaded, you can fire all Munitions loaded into a weapon you are wielding at a point within 100 feet; you do not need to be able to see this point, but if you cannot, neither you or the point can be under a roof. All Creatures within 15 feet of the chosen point must make a Dexterity Saving Throw against your Martial DC. On a failed Save, they take Damage equal to the Damage of the weapon used + the additional Damage from the loaded Munitions, and also suffer the additional effects of all loaded Munitions. On a successful Save, they take half as much Damage, and suffer no other effects.
:
Once a Munition has been fired, it is no longer loaded.
:


}}

{{imageMaskEdge1,--offset:25%,--rotation:0
<img 
  src='https://arknights.wiki.gg/images/8/8e/Firewhistle_Elite_2.png?47cdb9&20230926223820' 
  style='position:absolute;top:630px;right:-00px;height:1200;width:800px' />
}}



\page
{{pageNumber,auto}}

#### Level 9: Moving Fortress
The effectiveness of Artillery entirely depends on a good position to fire from. Entrenching no longer reduces your Speed to 0; instead, it reduces your Speed by 20 feet.

#### Level 12: Stronghold
The essence of a fortification is both to protect and to provide a location to attack from; you have refined your technique to facilitate both of these things.

**Fortification.** Once per Turn, while you are Entrenched, you may take the Shelter Action without expending an Action.

**Stop their Advance.** When a Creature that you can see moves within 100 feet of you while you have any Munitions loaded, you can expend one Resilience to fire your loaded Munitions at that Creature as a Reaction; you must choose a point as close to that Creature as possible as the target for your Munitions.

#### Level 15: Creeping Barrage
Your technique is such that you have become an endless stream of ordanance, raining hell from on far. 
:
When you roll Initiative and have less than two Munitions Dice remaining, you regain Munitions Dice until you have two. 

<img 
  src='https://arknights.wiki.gg/images/5/58/Horn.png?b77fd9&20230926233232' 
  style='position:absolute;top:450px;right:300px;width:600px' />


\column

#### Munition Options
The Munitions available to the Fortress Branch are listed in alphabetical order.

##### Concussive Munition
**Damage:** Thunder Damage equal to one roll of your Munitions Dice.
:
**Additional Effect:** Until the end of their next Turn, affected Creatures' Speed is halved, and they have the Dazed Condition.

##### Corrosive Munition
**Damage:** Acid Damage equal to one rolls of your Munitions Dice.
:
**Additional Effect:** Affected Creatures gain the Corroded Condition until they use an Action during their Turn to remove it.

##### Flare Munition
**Damage:** Fire Damage equal to one roll of your Munitions Dice.
:
**Additional Effect:** Until the end of your next Turn, affected Creatures cannot benefit from the Invisible Condition, and Attack Rolls against those Creatures are made at Advantage.

##### Explosive Munition
**Damage:** Acid, Fire, or Thunder Damage (your Choice) equal to two rolls of your Munitions Dice.
:
**Additional Effect:** None.

##### Incendiary Munition
**Damage:** Fire Damage equal to one roll of your Munitions Dice.
:
**Additional Effect:** Affected Creatures gain the Burning Condition.

##### Shrapnel Munition
**Damage:** Slashing Damage equal to one roll of your Munitions Dice.
:
**Additional Effect:** Affected Creatures gain the Bleeding Condition.

\page
{{pageNumber,auto}}

### Guardian
A Defender's role is to protect; all Defenders would agree, but what they protect is a matter of individual taste. For some, it's themselves, others their allies; Guardian Defenders fall in the latter camp with fervour. It is not enough to simply prevent harm coming to them, but to cure any harm that *does* befall them.

#### 1st Level: Guardian of Life
You can cast Arts; the information below details how you use those rules as a Guardian.
:
***Arts Points:*** The Guardian Arts Casting table shows how many Arts Points you have to cast your Level 1+ Arts. You regain all expended Arts Points when you finish a Long Rest. 
:
***Maximum Arts Level:*** The Guardian Arts Casting table shows the maximum Level you can cast an Art at, based on your Defender Level.
:
***Spellcasting Ability:*** When you gain this Feature, choose either Intelligence, Wisdom, or Charisma; that is your Spellcasting Ability.
:
***Spellcasting Focus:*** Shields equipped by you gain the Casting Unit Property for the duration.

{{classTable,frame,decoration
##### Guardian Arts Casting
| Level | Maximum Art Level | Arts Points |
|:-----:|:-----------------:|:-----------:|
| 1st   |         1         |      3      |
| 2nd   |         1         |      3      |
| 3rd   |         1         |      4      |
| 4th   |         1         |      4      |
| 5th   |         2         |      6      |
| 6th   |         2         |      6      |
| 7th   |         2         |      7      |
| 8th   |         2         |      7      |
| 9th   |         3         |      10     |
| 10th  |         3         |      10     |
| 11th  |         3         |      11      |
| 12th  |         3         |      11      |
| 13th  |         4         |      12     |
| 14th  |         4         |      12     |
| 15th  |         4         |      15     |
| 16th  |         4         |      15     |
| 17th  |         5         |      16     |
| 18th  |         5         |      16     |
| 19th  |         5         |      17     |
| 20th  |         5         |      17     |

}}

***Guardian Arts Pool:*** When you gain this Feature, you learn all of the Arts from the Healing Arts Special Arts Pool of 5th Level or less, including all Cantrips in that Pool. These Arts are always prepared.

\column

#### 4th Level: Not Today
When the moment calls, you can dig deep into your reserves to protect those you care about. 
:
Once per Turn, you can expend any amount of Resilience (no Action required) to reduce the Arts Point cost of the next Art you cast by the amount of Resilience expended, to a minimum of 0.


{{imageMaskEdge6,--offset:4%,--rotation:270
<img 
  src='https://arknights.wiki.gg/images/thumb/d/d1/Saria_Skin_1.png/1024px-Saria_Skin_1.png?544f9f' 
  style='position:absolute;top:150px;right:-450px;width:1200px' />
}}





\page
{{pageNumber,auto}}



#### 9th Level: Anaesthesia
Your Arts are built not for long term care, but urgent, instant first aid, enough to get someone back on their feet.
:
When you restore Hit Points to a Creature, you can also grant 2d6 Temporary Hit Points to that Creature. If you expend 1 Resilience, you can grant 4d6 Temporary Hit Points instead. 

#### 12th Level: Aura of Mercy
Whenever an allied Creature within 10 feet of you recovers Hit Points, they recover additional Hit Points equal to your Proficiency Bonus. 

#### 15th Level: The Best Medicine
You may have dedicated a part of yourself to the healing of others, but you are still a frontline fighter; woe betide those who forget that. 
:
You can activate this Feature as a Bonus Action. For the next minute, whenever you make a Melee Attack against a Creature, you may restore 2d6 Hit Points to a Creature of your choice that you can see within 30 feet. 
:
Once you have used this Feature, you may not do so again until you complete a Long Rest.

{{imageMaskEdge8,--offset:4%,--rotation:270
<img 
  src='https://arknights.wiki.gg/images/c/ca/Nearl_Elite_2.png?2a824c&20230926225639' 
  style='position:absolute;top:-200px;right:-400px;width:1300px' />
}}

{{imageMaskEdge8,--offset:5%,--rotation:0
<img 
  src='https://arknights.wiki.gg/images/c/ca/Nearl_Elite_2.png?2a824c&20230926225639' 
  style='position:absolute;top:-200px;right:-400px;width:1300px' />
}}


\page
{{pageNumber,auto}}

### Juggernaut
A good offense is an unbeatable defence, or so goes the creed of the Juggernaut. Cladding themselves in the heaviest armour, and reinforcing it with specialised Arts, the Juggernaut turns themselves into a boulder that rolls over all who oppose them.

#### 1st Level: Superheavy
While you are wearing Heavy Armour, you have an Arts Barrier surrounding your person. When you would take Damage from another Creature, a layer of the Barrier shatters, halving that instance of Damage. You may reform a layer of the Barrier as an Action during your Turn, or as a Bonus Action by expending 1 Resilience.
:
The maximum layers of Barrier you can have is 1. This maximum is increased to 2 at 7th Level, and 3 at 14th Level.

<img 
  src='https://arknights.wiki.gg/images/a/a4/Mudrock.png?95b848&20230927012624' 
  style='position:absolute;top:360px;right:270px;width:600px' />

\column

#### 4th Level: Implacable
You cannot be stopped, and punish those who attempt to do so. 

**Rolling Boulder.** Your Speed cannot be reduced by another Creature. If you gain a Condition that would reduce your Speed, you suffer all other effects of that Condition, but your Speed is not reduced.

**Shatterstorm.** When a layer of your Barrier shatters, roll 1d8 and add your Constitution Modifier; you deal that much Force Damage to Creatures of your choice within 10 feet of you. 

#### 9th Level: Bullrush
As an Action during your Turn, choose a point you can see within 30 feet. You move in a single straight line to that location; you can move through Creatures and objects of equal size or smaller than you as part of this movement. Any Creature or Object you moved through during this movement must make a Dexterity Saving Throw. On a failed Save, they take 4d12 Bludgeoning Damage, and are knocked 10 feet directly away from you. On a successful Save, they take half as much Damage, and are not knocked back. 
:
Once you have used this Feature, you may not do so again until you complete a Short Rest, or expend 2 Resilience (no Action required) to restore your use of it.

#### 12th Level: Overwhelming Might
While you have any layers of your Barrier, your Melee Attacks deal an additional die of Damage as Force Damage.

#### 15th Level: Groundbreaker
As an Action, you slam the ground with such force that it cracks and splinters. All other Creatures touching the ground within 30 feet of you must make a Strength Saving Throw against your Martial DC. On a failed Save, they take 8d12 Thunder Damage; on a successful Save, they take half as much Damage. The affected area is then considered to be Difficult Terrain for anyone except you.
:
Once you have used this Feature, you may not do so again until you finish a Long Rest.

\page
{{pageNumber,auto}}

### Protector
As the name suggests, Protector Branch Defenders leap directly to the defence of their allies. They are not concerned with offensive capability, disabling and disorientating foes for their allies to mop up. 

{{note
##### Protector DC
Many Protector Features do not use your Martial DC, but instead a Protector DC. Your Protector DC is calculated as 8 + Your Constitution Modifier + Your Proficiency Bonus.
}}

\column

#### 1st Level: Centre of Attention
As an Action, or as a Bonus Action if you expend 2 Resilience, Creatures of your choice within 10 feet of you must succeed on a Wisdom Saving Throw against your Protector DC, or be Taunted by you until the end of their next Turn. A Taunted Creature must Attack you during its Turn, if it is able to.
:
You can use this Feature a number of times equal to your Constitution Modifier, and regain all expended uses upon finishing a Long Rest. 

#### 4th Level: Anchor Point
Your expertise is in the protecting of others, and it shows. While you are wielding a Shield with the Bulwark Property, you gain the following benefits:

**Impenetrable.** When you or a Creature benefitting from your Shelter Action would take Damage, you can expend 1 Resilience as a Reaction to grant that Creature Damage Reduction against that Damage equal to half of your Physical Damage Reduction (this effect is not cumulative). You may expend 1 further Resilience (no Action required) to refund use of your Reaction.

**Improved Shelter.** The Radius of your Shelter Action is increased to 10 feet from 5 feet. Additionally, Creatures benefitting from your Shelter Action have Advantage on Strength and Dexterity Saving Throws against effects you can see.

#### 9th Level: Stone Wall
When a Creature hits you with a Melee Attack while you are wielding a shield, you can attempt to knock them off Balance as a Reaction. That Creature must succeed on a Strength Saving Throw against your Protector DC or take  1d8 + your Constitution Modifier Bludgeoning Damage, and be knocked Off Balance until the end of your next Turn.

Attacks against Creatures that have been knocked Off Balance are made at Advantage.

#### 12th Level: War of Attrition
You don't have to defeat your foes, per se. You just have to outlast them.

**Punishment.** When you hit a Creature with an Attack, or a Creature fails a Saving Throw against you, that Creature has Disadvantage on the next Attack Roll it makes before the end of your next Turn.

**Unending Defence.** If your Turn ends and you have no Resilience remaining, you recover 1 Resilience.

#### 15th Level: Total Shutdown
You are unbreakable; this wall shall never fall.

**Improved Taunt.** You are Resistant to Damage dealt by Creatures that are Taunted by you. 

**Greater Improved Shelter.** Creatures that are benefitting from your Shelter Feature are Resistant to the first instance of Damage they take before the start of your next Turn.

{{imageMaskEdge7,--offset:6%,--rotation:85
<img 
  src='https://arknights.wiki.gg/images/8/8b/Croissant_Elite_2.png?a5ed2b&20230927011429' 
  style='position:absolute;top:250px;right:200px;width:900px' />
}}


\page
{{pageNumber,auto}}

### Sentry
The emergence of the Sentry represents a modern invention, and a shift in thinking. As compact, powerful ranged weapons like firearms and other projectors continue to advance, there grows a need for someone to take point, as it were. Someone who can still make use of these technological advancements, but does not fear reprisal from the enemy- and so was the Sentry born.

{{note
##### Equipment Exchange
When you choose this Branch, you may exchange 1x Light Melee Weapon or 1x Light Shield from your Starting Equipment for A) 1x Light Ranged Weapon or B) 1x Heavy Ranged Weapon; these weapons cannot have the Firearm Property.
}}

#### 1st Level: Pavise
Establish a forward position, attack, move, repeat. These are the Sentries's lessons.

**Additional Weapon Proficiencies.** You gain Proficiency with Light Ranged Weapons and Heavy Ranged Weapons.

**Plant Pavise.** As a Bonus Action while you have a Heavy Shield equipped, you can plant your shield in the ground. Allied Creatures, including you, within 10 feet of your planted shield gain the benefit of your Shelter Action, so long as the attack originates from the opposite side of the shield from that Creature's perspective. Additionally, your planted shield functions as Half Cover.

You can recover your shield as an Interact with Object while you are within 5 feet of it, and you can only have one shield planted at a time. Other Creatures cannot move your planted Shield.

#### 4th Level: Marksman Training
You learn to better blend your identity as a ranged Defender, creating a potent hybrid. 

**Operator Skill Access.** Whenever you gain a new Operator Skill, you may instead choose a Skill from the appropriate Operator Skills table available to the Marksman Class.

**Additional Fighting Style.** You gain a Fighting Style from one of the options available to the Marksman Class.

#### 9th Level: Advance and Fire
When a Creature makes an Attack against you, or a Creature benefitting from your Shelter Action, you can make a Ranged Weapon Attack against the Attacking Creature as a Reaction. After making this Attack, you can expend 1 Resilience to refund use of your Reaction.


\column

#### 12th Level: Pointman
Whether defending the backline or the frontline, Sentry Defenders are equally at home. You gain the following benefits:

**Bar the Gates.** When you use your Leaping Defence Feature while you are wielding a Heavy Shield, you can use your Plant Pavise Feature as part of that Reaction. If you are instead within 5 feet of your planted shield when you would use Leaping Defence, you can expend 1 Resilience to recover your shield before making the movement; you may still plant it again as part of that Leaping Defence.

**Heads Down.** When a Creature within 5 feet of your planted shield would make a Dexterity Saving Throw against an effect they can see, you can expend 1 Resilience (no Action required) to cause that Creature to automatically pass that Saving Throw.

#### 15th Level: Firebase
None cross your field of fire unharmed. You gain the following benefits:

**Retributor.** Once per Round, you may use your Advance and Fire Feature without expending use of your Reaction.

**Torrent of Lead, Wall of Steel.** Once per Long Rest, you may activate this Feature during your Turn (no Action required). Until the end of your next Turn, when you make a Ranged Attack as a result of your Advance and Fire Feature, you can make an additional Attack (no Action required) against the same Creature; these Attacks cannot generate further Attacks.

{{imageMaskEdge1,--offset:2%,--rotation:270
<img 
  src='https://arknights.wiki.gg/images/d/d8/Underflow.png?d17df1&20240605104759' 
  style='position:absolute;top:560px;right:-120px;width:650px' />
}}










\page

## Guard 
In water-logged Siracusa, a Lupo leaps into the fray alongside her fellow mafioso, a blade in each hand as she strikes at foes near and far. Ostensibly, their fight is for honour, but she cares not.

In rime-bound Sami, a Sarkaz's Arts infused blade dispels demons with every swipe, burning corruption from the land. Every strike brings her closer to her truth.

In glittering Kazimierz, two Knights cross blades to the cheers of thousands. Each feels a palpable disdain for the fight, but they cannot, will not, back down.

Each of these people, heroes and villains both, are Guards; masters of the martial, the cut and thrust of close quarters combat. What is it that they guard; a way of life, their pride, an ideal? Only they know, but one thing is certain- they will fight for it until their very last breath.


{{tocExcludeH3 
### Born For Battle
To be a Guard is to be sword and shield both. In the world of Terra, a world where brutal melee is commonplace amongst conflict both local and international, versatile fighters are an essential component of any fighting force, large or small. A Defender may hold the line, but it is the Guard who will push back the foe. A Marksman may strike down hordes of enemies at range, but it is the Guard who will seize the position. 
No matter what comes over the horizon- rebels, terrorists, monsters, demons, or worse- a Guard is ready.
}}

\column

{{tocExcludeH3 
### Class Features
}}

As a Guard, you gain the following Class Features:

#### Hit Points
**Hit Dice:** 1d10 per Guard Level<br>
**Hit Points at 1st Level:** 10 + your Constitution Modifier<br>
**Hit Points at Higher Levels:** 1d10 (or 6) + your Constitution Modifier per Guard Level after 1st

#### Proficiencies
**Armor:** Light Armour, Medium Armour, Light Shields<br>
**Weapons:** All Melee Weapons<br>
**Tools:** Maintenance Kit<br>
**Saving Throws:** Strength + one of your choice from the following: Constitution, Dexterity<br>
**Skills:** Three from: Acrobatics, Athletics, Lore, Insight, Intimidation, Wilderness

#### Equipment
You start with the following Equipment, in addition to any Equipment granted by your Background. 

- **A)** 1x Heavy Melee Weapon OR **B)** 1x Light Melee Weapon
- **A)** 1x Light Melee Weapon OR **B)** 1x Light Shield
- **A)** Reinforced Outfit OR **B)** Combat Gear
- One Kit you are Proficient with.

OR

- 1300 LMD


{{classTable,frame,decoration,wide
##### The Guard
| Level | Proficiency Bonus | Features |    
|:-----:|:-----------------:|:--------:|
|  1st  |  +2  | Elite 0- Guard Branch, Fighting Style, Push the Advantage |
|  2nd  |  +2  | Basic Operator Skill  |
|  3rd  |  +2  | Basic Talent, Unrelenting  |
|  4th  |  +2  | Ability Score Improvement, Branch Feature  |
|  5th  |  +3  | Extra Attack |
|  6th  |  +3  | Ability Score Improvement  |
|  7th  |  +3  | Elite 1- Advanced Operator Skill  |
|  8th  |  +3  | Ability Score Improvement, Basic Talent Module  |
|  9th  |  +4  | Advanced Talent    |
| 10th  |  +4  | Ability Score Improvement, Branch Feature   |
| 11th  |  +4  | Unrelenting Upgraded     |
| 12th  |  +4  | Ability Score Improvement      |
| 13th  |  +5  | Advanced Talent Module, Branch Feature      |
| 14th  |  +5  | Elite 2- Elite Operator Skill  |
| 15th  |  +5  | Unrelenting Aggression |
| 16th  |  +5  | Ability Score Improvement    |
| 17th  |  +6  | Branch Feature   |
| 18th  |  +6  | Eternal Aggression      |
| 19th  |  +6  | Ability Score Improvement        |
| 20th  |  +6  | Annihilation Protocol      |
}}

{{pageNumber,auto}}

\page

#### 1st Level: Guard Branch

You choose a Branch that most closely fits the style you envision for yourself. Choose one from the following list; further details on these Branches can be found at the end of the Class Description. The Branch you choose grants you Features at Level 1, 4, 10, 13, and 17.

- **Artsblade:** Channelling powerful Arts into the wielding of their weapons, Artsblades tread the line between Caster and Guard to lethal effect.
- **Brawler:** A Brawler needs no weapons, crushing foes with the power of their weighty fists and specialised brawling techniques.
- **Centurion:** Engaging multiple enemies simultaneously, these warriors thrive when surrounded by foes; all the better to cleave through.
- **Crusher:** Slow but utterly overwhelming, the Crusher's defences are minimal, but their offence unstoppable.
- **Duellist:** A specialist skilled in defeating singular powerful foes, warding off their attacks and grinding them down.
- **Instructor:** Not every warrior desires to stand alone for their moment of glory. Others prefer to stand shoulder to shoulder; the Instructor is one such sort, supporting their allies alongside their assault.
- **Liberator:** Whereas other Guards are explosive, Liberators are a rumbling volcano; patient, inactive, ever strengthening, ever growing closer to that brief moment of unparalleled destruction.
- **Lord:** Untouchable, unshakable, the Lord's specialised technique allows them to engage their enemies in melee or at range, at their leisure. 
- **Musha:** Independent and irrepressible, these Guards rely less on support from their allies to see them through battles, but on their own sustain.
- **Reaper:** The bane of lesser foes, Reapers build up to a devastating crescendo as the battle grinds on.
- **Weaponmaster:** Whether wielding one weapon or many, Weaponmasters excel in rapid, multi-hitting attacks.

\column

#### 1st Level: Fighting Style
You adopt a particular style of fighting as your speciality. Choose one of the following options (You can't take a Fighting Style option more than once, even if you later get to choose again).

##### Brawler
On each of your Turns, you may make an Unarmed Strike as a Bonus Action. 

##### Deadly Fighter
The first time each Turn you make an Attack Roll using a Weapon with the Deadly Property, the Critical Range of that Attack is increased by 1.

##### Defensive
When you are hit by an Attack while you have a Melee Weapon or Shield equipped, you may use your Reaction to increase your AC by 1d4 for the duration of that Attack, possibly causing that Attack to miss.

##### Duelling
You have a +1 bonus to Attack and Damage Rolls for Melee Attacks against the first Creature you Attack each Turn. 

##### Great Weapon Fighting
You gain a +2 bonus to Damage rolls you make with Melee Weapons that you are wielding with two hands. The weapon must have the Two-Handed or Versatile Quality for you to gain this benefit.

##### Lightly Armed
When you are wielding a Melee Weapon in one hand and no other Weapons or Shields, you gain a +2 Bonus to Attack Rolls with that Weapon.

##### Ranger
You gain a +2 Bonus to Attack Rolls you make with Ranged or Thrown Weapons.

##### Spearwall
When you make a Melee Attack while it is not your Turn, that Attack deals an additional dice of Damage. 

##### Two-Weapon Fighting
When you engage in Two-Weapon Fighting, you can add your Ability Modifier to the Damage of the additional Attack(s), and you may draw or stow two one-handed weapons when you would normally be able to draw or stow only one.


{{pageNumber,auto}}

\page
{{pageNumber,auto}}  


#### 1st Level: Press the Attack
As a Guard, you are the mainstay in any assault. Others defend, others facilitate, you push onwards. You gain access to special Press the Attack options, and gain additional ones from your Branch, that are fuelled by your Aggression.
:
**Aggression:** You have 4 Aggression, which you expend to use Press the Attack options. You recover all expended Aggression when you complete a Short or Long Rest.
:
You gain 2 additional Aggression at 7th Level (total of 6), and at 14th Level (total of 8).
:
**Expending Aggression:** Each Press the Attack option dictates the circumstances in which you can use it, and how much Aggression is required to use it. Some options allow you to spend more than the minimum Aggression to amplify the effects. 
:
****Press the Attack: Forestall (1-3 Aggression)****
You can use this Press the Attack Option as a Reaction when you are hit by an Attack; for each Aggression you expend, your Armour Class is increased by a value equal to your Proficiency Bonus against the triggering Attack, possibly causing it to miss. 
:
****Press the Attack: Smite (1-3 Aggression)****
You can use this Press the Attack Option when you hit with an Attack (no Action required); for each Aggression you expend, that Attack deals additional Damage equal to your Proficiency Bonus.
:
****Press the Attack: Strike True (1-3 Aggression)****
You can use this Press the Attack Option when you make an Attack Roll (no Action required); for each Aggression you expend, you gain a bonus to that Attack Roll equal to your Proficiency Bonus.

#### 2nd Level: Basic Operator Skill
You learn a Basic Operator Skill from the Basic Guard Skills table, or from any other Basic Skill table available to you.

#### 3rd Level: Unrelenting
You can push yourself beyond your normal limits for but a moment. You may use this Feature as a Reaction when another Creature ends their Turn; you may immediately make a single Attack.
:
Once you use this Feature, you must finish a Long Rest before you can use it again.

#### 3rd Level: Basic Talent
You gain a Basic Talent of your choice from the Basic Guard Talent table. When you reach 8th Level, you may either pick a different Basic Talent to gain, or upgrade your Basic Talent, gaining its Module Effect.

\column

#### 4th Level: Ability Score Improvement
When you reach 4th level, and again at 6th, 8th, 10th, 12th, 16th, and 19th level, you can increase one Ability Score of your choice by 2, or you can increase two Ability Scores of your choice by 1. As normal, you can’t increase an Ability Score above 20 using this feature.

{{imageMaskEdge1,--offset:1%,--rotation:270
<img 
  src='https://arknights.wiki.gg/images/8/8c/Sharp.png' 
  style='position:absolute;top:160px;right:-50px;width:540px' />
}}

\page

#### 5th Level: Extra Attack
You can attack twice instead of once whenever you take the Attack Action during your Turn.

#### 7th Level: Advanced Operator Skill
You learn an Advanced Operator Skill from the Advanced Guard Skills table, or from any other Advanced Skill table available to you.

\column


#### 9th Level: Advanced Talent
You gain an Advanced Talent of your choice from the Advanced Guard Talent table. When you reach 13th Level, you may either pick a different Advanced Talent to gain, or upgrade your Advanced Talent, gaining its Module Effect.

#### 11th Level: Unrelenting Upgraded
You gain the following benefits:

**More Unrelenting.** You can now use your Unrelenting Feature twice, and regain all expended uses when you complete a Long Rest.

**Pursuit Unrelenting.** When you would use your Unrelenting Feature, you may expend an additional use of it to take an Action or Bonus Action in place of the Attack you would normally make.

#### 14th Level: Elite Operator Skill
You learn an Elite Operator Skill from the Elite Guard Skills table, or from any other Elite Skill table available to you.

#### 15th Level: Unrelenting Aggression
You gain the following Press the Attack option:
:
**Press the Attack: Unrelenting Aggression (2 Aggression)** You can use this Press the Attack Action as a Reaction when you miss a Creature with a Melee Attack; roll the Damage for that Attack, but do not add your Ability Score Modifier to the result. The Creature takes Damage equal to the amount rolled.

#### 18th Level: Eternal Aggression
Always on the assault; where you stand, enemies fall. You may not have the pace of a vanguard, but you are a steady machine, cutting a swathe through their ranks.
:
When you expend a use of your Unrelenting Feature, roll 1d4; you recover expended Aggression equal to the amount rolled.

#### 20th Level: Annihilation Protocol
Limits are made to be broken; yours have been shattered, never to reform. When you roll Initiative and have no uses of Unrelenting remaining, you regain one use.

{{pageNumber,auto}}
\page

{{wide

### Artsblade
Many Guards use Arts to supplement their strength and cover their weaknesses; Artsblades take this to its logical conclusion, wielding the power of the elements through their skill-at-arms.

#### 1st Level: Sword and Sorcery
You weave Art and Armament together with an artist's skill.
:
***Spellsword Arts:*** Choose one of the Standard Arts Pools; you learn all levelled Arts from that Arts Pool of 5th Level or less. However, you cannot cast these Arts in the usual way; they can only be cast by using your Branch Features, and are referred to as Spellsword Arts.
:
***Arts Points/Spellsword Points:*** You do not expend Arts Points to cast Spellsword Arts. To cast levelled Arts, you expend Spellsword Points; the Artsblade table shows how many Spellsword Points you have to cast your Arts. You regain all expended Spellsword Points when you finish a Short or Long Rest.
:
***Maximum Arts Level:*** The Artsblade table shows the maximum level you can cast a Spellsword Art at, based on your Guard Level. When you cast a Spellsword Art, you must cast it at the maximum level you can cast that Art at.

}}

{{classTable,frame,decoration
##### Artsblade Table
| Level | Maximum Art Level | Spellsword Points |
|:-----:|:-----------------:|:-----------:|
| 1st   |         1         |      1      |
| 2nd   |         1         |      1      |
| 3rd   |         1         |      1      |
| 4th   |         1         |      1      |
| 5th   |         2         |      1      |
| 6th   |         2         |      1      |
| 7th   |         2         |      2      |
| 8th   |         2         |      2      |
| 9th   |         3         |      2      |
| 10th  |         3         |      2      |
| 11th  |         3         |      2      |
| 12th  |         3         |      2      |
| 13th  |         4         |      2     |
| 14th  |         4         |      3     |
| 15th  |         4         |      3     |
| 16th  |         4         |      3     |
| 17th  |         5         |      3     |
| 18th  |         5         |      3     |
| 19th  |         5         |      3     |
| 20th  |         5         |      3     |

}}



**Press the Attack: Augmented Assault (2 Aggression)** You may use this Press the Attack option during your Turn (no Action required) if you have not cast a Spellsword Art this Turn. You may cast two Spellsword Arts this Turn, but both must be Blade Art.

\column

***Casting Spellsword Arts:*** When you make a Melee Weapon Attack during your Turn, you can expend 1 Spellsword Point to cast a Spellsword Art with a Casting Time of Action or Bonus Action as part of that Attack. An Art cast in this way is infused into that Attack instead of being cast normally; how that Art resolves depends on the Art in question. You can only cast a Spellsword Art once per Turn.

{{note
##### Resolving Spellsword Arts
- If the Art requires a Spell Attack Roll (such as Ice Knife or Acidify Blood), you use the result of the Attack Roll to determine the effect of both the Attack and Art. 
- If the Art requires a Saving Throw (Such as Acid Splash or Lightning Bolt), you use your Martial DC as your Spell Save DC. The Creature you attacked is still required to make a Saving Throw even if the triggering Attack misses.
- If the Art targets Self (such as Blade Art, or Ashardalon's Stride), then that Art still targets Self
- If the Art targets one or more Creatures but does not deal Damage to those Creature (such as Heroism, Mage Armour, or Acid Douse), you choose targets for that Art from Creatures within 10 feet of yourself, including yourself if desired.
- If the Art would normally affect an Area (such as Fireball or Ice Knife), only the Creature you attacked is affected.
- If the Art leaves behind an effect in an area (such as Ice Sheet or Repulsion Field), then that effect is only created in the current space of the Creature you attacked.
- If the Art would create something in an unoccupied space (such as Melting Mine or Earthen Grasp), you can create that something in an unoccupied space within 10 feet of the Creature you attacked.
}}

{{pageNumber,auto}}
\page



#### 1st Level: Resonance
Originium Arts is woven into your very form. You gain the following benefits:

**Arts-Touched.** You gain Proficiency in the Arts Skill, or Expertise if you already have Proficiency in it.

**Blade Arts.** You learn the Blade Arts Cantrip, shown below. It is considered to be a Spellsword Art for you, but does not cost a Spellsword Point to cast. Additionally, Melee Weapons being wielded by you are considered to have the Casting Unit Property.

**Resonance.** Your Blade Art Cantrip gains an additional effect depending on the Damage Type dealt. Once a Creature has made a Saving Throw against your Blade Art, they do not need to make that Saving Throw again until the beginning of your next Turn.



| Damage Type (Arts Pool) | Effect | 
|:------------:|:-----:|
|Acid (Corrosion)| A Creature that takes Acid Damage from this Cantrip takes Acid Damage at the end of their next Turn equal to the Acid Damage they took from this Cantrip.
|Bludgeoning (Earth)| Your Reach is doubled until the end of the Turn, including for this Cantrip.
|Cold (Frost)| A Creature that takes Damage from this Cantrip must pass a Constitution Saving Throw against your Martial DC or have its Speed halved until the end of its next Turn.
|Fire (Flame)|A Creature that takes Damage from this Cantrip must pass a Dexterity Saving Throw against your Martial DC or suffer the Burning Condition.
|Force (Crude)| Your Melee Attacks have a +1 bonus to their Attack and Damage Rolls, including for this Cantrip, until the end of your next Turn. 
|Lightning (Electric)| When you hit a Creature with this Cantrip, choose a different Creature you can see within 15 feet of the original Creature; that Creature takes Lightning Damage equal to half of the Lightning Damage taken by the original Creature. 
|Piercing (Fluid)| A Creature that takes Damage from this Cantrip must succeed a Constitution Saving Throw against your Martial DC, or they cannot take Reactions until the end of their next Turn. 
|Psychic (Psychic)| A Creature that takes Damage from this Cantrip loses 1d6 Hit Points.
|Radiant (Light)| When you cast this Cantrip, you can cause Creatures of your choice within 10 feet of you to take Radiant Damage equal to your Proficiency Bonus.
|Slashing (Wind)| A Creature that takes Damage from this Cantrip must pass a Constitution Saving Throw against your Martial DC or begin Bleeding.
|Thunder (Spatial)| A Creature that takes Damage from this Cantrip must pass a Strength Saving Throw against your Martial DC or be knocked directly away from you 20 feet.




\column

{{note
#### Blade Art 
*Evocation Cantrip*

**Casting Time:** :: Action
**Range:**        :: Self
**Components:**   :: S
**Duration:**     :: Instant

This Cantrip can only be cast as a Spellsword Art by an Artsblade. When it is cast, the Attack that this Art was cast as part of deals 1d6 additional Arts Damage. You choose what Damage Type this Damage is; when you cast this Art, choose one of the Arts Pools you have selected for your Spellsword Arts. The Damage Type corresponds to the chosen Arts Pool.

| Arts Pool | Damage Type |
|:---------:|:-----------:|
| Corrosion | Acid        |
| Crude     | Force       |
| Earth     | Bludgeoning |
| Electric  | Lightning   |
| Flame     | Fire        |
| Fluid     | Piercing    |
| Frost     | Cold        |
| Light     | Radiant     |
| Psychic   | Psychic     |
| Spatial   | Thunder     |
| Wind      | Slashing    |

:
**Cantrip Upgrade:** The Additional Damage increases by 1d6 when you reach levels 5 (2d6), 11 (3d6), and 17 (4d6).
}}


#### 4th Level: Cut Through Chaos
You and your weapon are so entwined with the power of Originium Arts that your offence can become defence, slicing through fire and flesh alike. You can use this Feature in three ways:
:
- When a Creature makes a Spell Attack Roll against you, you can make a Melee Attack as a Reaction; if the result of your Attack Roll is greater than the Spell Attack Roll, that Spell Attack misses you.
- When you would make a Saving Throw against an Art you can see, you can instead make a Melee Attack as a Reaction; if the result of your Attack Roll is greater than the DC of that Saving Throw, you are considered to have succeeded on that Saving Throw.
- When the conditions are met to cast a Spellsword Art you know with a Casting Time of Reaction, you may expend your Reaction to cast that Art without expending a Spellsword Point.

{{align-text:left
You can use this Feature a number of times equal to your Proficiency Bonus, and regain all expended uses when you complete a Long Rest.
}}








{{pageNumber,auto}}
\page

#### 10th Level: Sharpened Mind, Sharpened Weapon
Originium Arts thrum through your every aspect, unlocking new potential. You gain the following benefits:

**Additional Arts Pools.** Choose two Standard Arts Pools other than the one you selected for your Sword and Sorcery Feature. You learn all levelled Arts from that Arts Pool of 3rd Level or less. These are Spellsword Arts, and are subject to the same restrictions.

**Improved Resonance.** Your Resonance Feature is improved:

| Damage Type (Arts Pool) | Effect | 
|:------------:|:-----:|
|Acid (Corrosion)| A Creature that takes Acid Damage from this Cantrip suffers the Corroded Condition until the end of their next Turn.
|Bludgeoning (Earth)| Creatures that take Damage from this Cantrip, or are hit by a Melee Attack from you while your Reach is increased by the effect of this Cantrip, must succeed on a Strength Saving Throw against your Spell Save DC or be knocked Prone.
|Cold (Frost)| A Creature that fails the Saving Throw against this Art has Disadvantage on their Attack Rolls until the end of their next Turn.
|Fire (Flame)| After you have cast this Art, your Melee Weapon Attacks deal 1d10 additional Fire Damage until the end of your next Turn.
|Force (Crude)| The bonus to Attack Rolls and Damage Rolls is increased to +3.
|Lightning (Electric)|  When you hit a Creature with this Cantrip, you may choose up to two different Creatures you can see within 15 feet instead of one.
|Piercing (Fluid)| A Creature that fails the Saving Throw can only take an Action or a Bonus Action during their next Turn, not both.
|Psychic (Psychic)| A Creature that takes Damage from this Cantrip instead loses 2d6 Hit Points.
|Radiant (Light)| The radius of this effect is doubled.
|Slashing (Wind)| Instead of a Saving Throw, a Creature that takes Damage from this Cantrip suffers the Bleeding Condition.
|Thunder (Spatial)| If a Creature knocked back by this Cantrip collides with another Creature or an object, both take 3d10 Thunder Damage.

\column

#### 13th Level: High Fantasy
The line between wielding your Arts and wielding your weapons has blurred so greatly as to become a mere mirage. You gain the following benefits:

**Clear Path.** When you use your Cut Through Chaos Feature, you take no Damage if you succeed on the Saving Throw, and only half Damage if you fail.

**Unleash.** When you use your Augmented Assault Feature to cast a Spellsword Art that has an area of a cone, cube, sphere, or line, you can cause that Art to affect its normal area instead of only affecting the target; you are immune to the Damage of Arts you cast in this way. The Art otherwise affects all Creatures in the area as normal. Cubes and Spheres are centered on the target Creature; Cones and Lines originate from you and must include the target Creature.

{{wide

#### 17th Level: Power Overwhelming
Your Arts cannot be contained, nor your blade restrained. You gain the following Features:

**Ceaseless.** Whenever you roll Initiative, if you have less Spellsword Points than your Maximum, you gain 1 Spellsword Point.

**True Spellsword.** Whenever you expend a Spellsword Point, your Melee Attacks are considered to deal Arts Damage until the beginning of your next Turn.

**Ultimate Blade Art.** Once per Long Rest, at the beginning of your Turn you can Activate this Feature (no Action required). Until the end of your next Turn, the restriction on how many times per Round you can cast Spellsword Arts is removed. 

}}

### Brawler
A Brawler understands that neither raw strength, blinding speed, or base cunning can win the day; To score that knockout, you need a combination of all three. As comfortable without a weapon as with one, they approach every fight with a KO in mind.

#### 1st Level: Martial Artist

Your training has granted you a unique set of skills for down and dirty brawling.  You gain access to special Basic Brawling Techniques (detailed below) that are fuelled by your Aggression. 

#### 1st Level: Guns Out

While unencumbered, you can fight and move freely. While you are not wearing Medium or Heavy Armour, you gain the following benefits:

**Bob and Weave.** Your base Armour Class is equal to 10 + your Strength and Dexterity Modifiers.

**Dexterous Attacks.** You can use your Dexterity modifier instead of your Strength modifier for the Attack and Damage rolls of your Unarmed Strikes. In addition, when you make an Athletics Check to Grapple or Shove a Creature, you can use your Dexterity Modifier instead of Strength.

**Art of the Fist.** The Damage of your Unarmed Strikes is increased to 1d4 + your Ability Modifier. This is increased to 1d6 at 7th Level, and 1d8 at 14th Level. 

#### 4th Level: Rain of Blows
Not much can stand up to the "ol' one-two".

**Denting Blows.** Your Unarmed Strikes have the Crush (3) Property.

**Flurry.** When you take the Attack Action, you may make one Unarmed Strike as part of that Action in addition to your normal Attacks.

#### 10th Level: No Ordinary Pugilist
You are no back alley thug. Your hands and feet are phantasmagoric; an express pass to unconsciousness. You gain the following benefits:

**Always Ready.** At the beginning of your Turn during combat, you regain one expended Aggression.

**Master Brawler.** You gain access to the Advanced Brawling Techniques listed below.

\column

#### 13th Level: Kick them while they're Down
Honour is for tournaments; a true fighter never passes up a free hit. 
:
When you hit a Creature that has a detrimental Status Condition (such as Frightened, Stunned, Grappled, Prone, or Burning) with a Unarmed Strike, that Unarmed Strike deals additional Bludgeoning Damage equal to your Proficiency Bonus. 

#### 17th Level: Mountainbreaker
You unleash your furore in such a concentrated assault that few foes can hope to withstand it. 
:
As an Action, you may either; 
- Make an Unarmed Strike against a Creature within your Reach. If it hits, you may make another Unarmed Strike against it, and so on, until you miss or have made 10 Attacks; at which point the sequence ends. Each Attack after the first suffers a cumulative -2 to hit. 
- Make an Unarmed Strike against a Creature within your Reach; if it hits, the Damage of that Attack is multiplied by five. Additionally, if the Creature is no more than one size larger than you, they are knocked 20 feet directly away from you and fall Prone. 

{{align-text:left
Once you use this Feature, you must complete a Long Rest before you may do so again.
}}

 <img 
  src='https://arknights.wiki.gg/images/d/de/Indra_Skin_1.png' 
  style='position:absolute;top:430px;right:-320px;width:800px' />
  
{{pageNumber,auto}}  
\page
#### Basic Brawling Techniques
The Techniques available to the Brawler Branch are listed in alphabetical order. All Brawling Techniques are considered to be applications of your Press the Attack Feature.

 <img 
  src='https://arknights.wiki.gg/images/Flint.png?9dc029' 
  style='position:absolute;top:130px;right:170px;width:820px' />

\column

**Body Check (1 Aggression)** <br>
When you hit a Creature with an Unarmed Strike, you may use this Technique (no Action required) to attempt to Shove that Creature. On a success, the distance you shove that Creature is doubled.
:

**Cross Counter (1 Aggression)**<br>
When a Creature makes a Melee Attack against you, you can use this Technique as a Reaction to increase your Armour Class by a value equal to your Proficiency Bonus against that Attack, possibly causing it to miss. If that Attack misses you, you can make an Unarmed Attack against that Creature as part of this Reaction.
:
**Feint (1 Aggression)**<br>
You may use this Technique as an Bonus Action; choose one Creature within your Reach; your next Unarmed Strike against this Creature before the end of your next Turn is made at Advantage.
:
**Follow Up (1 Aggression)**<br>
When you hit a Creature with an Unarmed Strike, you can use this Technique (no Action required) to make an Unarmed Strike against the same Creature. You can only use this Technique once per Turn.
:
**Footwork (1 Aggression)**<br>
After making a Melee Attack, you can use this Technique (no Action required) to move up to half of your Movement Speed. This Movement does not provoke Opportunity Attacks.
:
**Guard Up (1 Aggression)**<br>
You may use this Technique as a Bonus Action; you take the Dodge Action as part of that Bonus Action.
:
**Slip Off (2 Aggression)**<br>
When you would take Damage from an Attack you can see, you can use this Technique as a Reaction to halve that Attack’s Damage against you.
:
**Tag In (2 Aggression)**<br>
If there is a Willing Creature (that is not Incapacitated) within your Reach, you may use this Technique to swap positions with them. This Movement still requires you to spend Movement, and does not provoke Opportunity Attacks. 
:
Additionally, either you or the Creature you swapped with (your choice) may then make a single Melee Attack against a valid target.
:
You may only use Tag In once per Turn.

{{pageNumber,auto}}  
\page

#### Advanced Brawling Techniques

**Chokehold (2 Aggression)**<br>
When you are Grappling a Creature no more than one Size Category larger than you, you may use this Technique as an Action; that Creature loses Hit Points equal to your Strength or Dexterity Score (your choice). Cannot be used on Creatures who do not breathe.
:
**CQC (2 Aggression)**<br>
When you hit a Creature with an Unarmed Strike, you may use this Technique (no Action required) to attempt to Grapple that Creature as part of that Attack.
:
**Disarm (3 Aggression)**<br>
When you are Grappling a Creature, you may use this Technique as an Action; the Creature you are Grappling must pass a Strength Saving Throw against your Martial DC or be forced to drop an item of your choice.
:
**Human Shield (2 Aggression)**<br>
When you are Grappling a Creature no more than one Size Category larger than you, you may use this Technique as an Action. That Creature must succeed on a Strength Saving Throw against your Martial DC or become Restrained by you. When you Restrain a Creature in this way, you swing them around to face a direction of your choice; you are considered to be in Three-Quarters Cover against Attacks from that direction, and if an Attack against you from that direction misses, it hits the Restrained Creature instead. You may change the direction in which you are presenting the Restrained Creature as an Action during your Turn. The Restrained Creature may reattempt the Saving Throw as an Action during their Turn, ending the Condition and the Grapple on a success. You may not Attack a Creature you have Restrained in this way.
:
**Menace (2 Aggression)**<br>
You can use this Technique as an Action; choose a Creature within 30 feet that can see or hear you. That Creature must succeed a Wisdom Saving Throw against your Martial DC or become Frightened of you. A Creature may reattempt the Saving Throw at the end of each of their Turn, ending the Condition on a success.
:
**Skullshaker (2 Aggression)**<br>
You may use this Technique as an Action; make an Unarmed Strike. If it hits, the Damage of that Attack is doubled, and the target is Dazed until the end of their next Turn.
:
**Weigh In (2 Aggression)**<br>
When you hit a Creature with a Melee Attack, you may use this Technique to attempt to goad them. They must succeed on a Wisdom Saving Throw against your Martial DC or have Disadvantage on all Attack Rolls against Creatures that aren’t you until the end of their next Turn.

 <img 
  src='https://arknights.wiki.gg/images/Mountain.png?edc664' 
  style='position:absolute;top:100px;right:-80px;width:550px;clip-path:rect(auto auto auto 10%);' />

{{pageNumber,auto}}

\page
### Centurion

Centurions are battle incarnate; a whirling tempest of weapons eager to leap into the massed ranks of the foe, and wreak their havoc. The more foes bought against them, the greater the challenge. The greater the challenge, the greater the glory.

{{note
##### Centurion DC
Some Centurion Features do not use your Martial DC, but instead a Centurion DC. Your Centurion DC is calculated as 8 + Your Intimidation Modifier.
}}

#### 1st Level: Cyclone Strike

Your training allows you to face down groups of enemies with frightening efficacy. 
:
When you make a Melee Weapon Attack against only one Creature, you may cause that Attack to cleave to another Creature; that Creature must both be within your Reach, and within 10 feet of the original Creature. If the result of that Attack Roll is greater than the second Creature's Armour Class, they are hit by that Attack, independent of the Attack hitting the original Creature. Once you have used this Feature, you may not do so again until the beginning of your next Turn.
:
From 7th Level, you may cleave to two other Creatures instead of just one. From 14th Level, you can use this Feature once on each Turn.

#### 1st Level: Shock Trooper
You are an object of fear, a butcher set loose. You gain the following benefits:

**Intimidating.** You gain Proficiency in the Intimidation Skill, or Expertise if you already had Proficiency.

**Long-Arm.** Melee Weapons you are wielding are considered to have the Reach Property during your Turn. If they already have that Property, their Reach is increased by 5 feet during your Turn.

****Press the Attack: Brutal Execution (2 Aggression)**** You can use this Press the Attack option as a Bonus Action or Reaction immediately after you kill a Creature; you end them with such physicality that hostile Creatures within 15 feet of you must succeed on a Wisdom Saving Throw against your Centurion DC or Psychic Damage equal to your Intimidation Modifier and become Frightened of you until the end of your next Turn. If this Damage reduces a Creature to 0 Hit Points, they fall Unconscious rather than die.


\column

#### 4th Level: Mow Them Down
It is equally dangerous to flee from you as it is to assail you. You gain the following benefits:

**Fearmonger.** You have Advantage on Attack Rolls against Frightened Creatures, and Frightened Creature have Disadvantage on Attack Rolls against you.

**Ferocious Fling.** Once per Short Rest, when you reduce a Creature to 0 Hit Points that is both:
- The same size or smaller than you
- Within your Reach
  
{{align-text:left
You may launch them as a projectile at another Creature within 30 feet of you (no Action required). This is a Ranged Weapon Attack using your Strength for the Attack and Damage Rolls; on a hit, the Attack deals Bludgeoning Damage equal to the launched Creature's Constitution Modifier.
}}
:
If you used Brutal Execution on the Creature you threw, your use of this Feature is refunded.
<img 
  src='https://arknights.wiki.gg/images/Broca.png?db212a' 
  style='position:absolute;top:400px;right:50px;width:330px' />

{{pageNumber,auto}}

\page

\column

#### 10th Level: Guillotine
One neck is the same as any other when your weapon finds it.
:
As an Action, you attempt to strike a lethal blow at a Creature within your Reach using a weapon you are wielding; that Creature must make a Dexterity Saving Throw against your Martial DC. On a failed Save, they take Xd6 Damage of the same Damage Type as the weapon you are wielding, where X is equal to twice your Proficiency Bonus, and begin Bleeding. Additionally, if the Creature that failed was an Elite or Leader Creature, other hostile Creatures within 30 feet of them must succeed on a Wisdom Saving Throw against your Centurion DC or become Frightened of you until the end of your next Turn. On a successful Save, they suffer half as much Damage, and no other effects.
:
Once you have used this Feature, you must finish a Long Rest before you can do so again.

#### 13th Level: Shredder
21 to 1? You like those odds.
:
When you deal Damage to a Creature with a Melee Weapon Attack, choose a different Creature within 10 feet of the original Creature; that Creature takes Slashing Damage equal to your Strength Modifier. This Damage is doubled against Creatures who are Frightened of you.

#### 17th Level: Storm of Blood
Kill, and kill again. The song of battle gives way to a storm of blood, and only you remain.
:
You may use this Feature as an Action; Creatures of your choice you can see within 15 feet of you must make a Dexterity Saving Throw against your Martial DC. On a failed Save, they take 10d8 Slashing Damage and are Frightened of you until the end of your next Turn; while they are Frightened of you, they must move directly away from you on their Turn if able. On a successful Save, they take half as much Damage, and are not Frightened. Furthermore, after you have used this Feature, you gain the benefit of your Long-Arm Feature until the end of your next Turn (even when it is not your Turn), and hitting a Creature with an Attack of Opportunity refunds use of your Reaction.
:
Once you have used this Feature, you may not do so again until you have finished a Long Rest.

{{imageMaskEdge1,--offset:1%,--rotation:90
<img 
  src='https://arknights.wiki.gg/images/3/30/Gavial_the_Invincible.png' 
  style='position:absolute;top:40px;right:100px;width:1000px' />

}}



{{pageNumber,auto}}

\page
### Crusher
True power needs no defence and no justification. Crushers embody this belief in form and function, weathering the storm with sheer vitality, and punishing those who dare to stand before their inexorable advance.

#### 1st Level: Towering Might
You are fortitude incarnate, a moving mountain. It is a fool's errand to attempt to contain your mass. You gain the following benefits:

**Extreme Fortitude.** Whenever you roll a Hit Die, roll two dice and discard the lowest result.

**Maximum Bulk.** Your Hit Point maximum increases by 3. Whenever you gain a Level thereafter, your Hit Point maximum increases by an additional 3.

#### 1st Level: Crushing Blows
Crushers place their all into each attack; weight, strength, and soul. You gain the following benefits:

**Death's Passing.** When you make a Melee Attack with a Heavy Melee Weapon as part of the Attack Action and miss, the Creature you attacked still takes half of the Damage they would have taken as Thunder Damage.

**Heavy Metal.** The Damage of Heavy Melee Weapons being wielded by you is increased by 1 die.

**Singular Strike.** When you take the Attack Action, you can only make one Attack as part of that Action, even if you would normally be able to make more. If the Attack you make is a Melee Attack, for each additional Attack you would otherwise have been able to make, that Attack's Damage is increased by the following:
- The weapon's Damage
- Your Strength Modifier
- Bonus Damage from the weapon being a +1/+2/+3 weapon.

{{note
##### Example: Singular Strike
You take the Attack Action using a Greatsword (2d6) and a Strength Modifier of 5. Because of your Heavy Metal Feature, the Damage of the Greatsword becomes 3d6.
- If you would be capable of making one Attacks, the Damage of this Attack is 3d6+5.
- If you would be capable of making two Attacks, the Damage of this Attack is 6d6+10.
- If you would be capable of making three Attacks, the Damage of this Attack is 9d6+15.
- If you would be capable of making four Attacks, the Damage of this Attack is 12d6+20.
}}

\column
:::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::
#### 4th Level: Foecrusher

Whenever you make a Melee Attack, you can choose to make that Attack a Foecrusher. A Foecrusher has one of the following effects (your Choice):

**Staggering Strike.** A Creature hit by this Attack has its Speed halved until the end of its next Turn.

**Dazing Strike.** A Creature hit by this Attack has the Dazed Condition until the end of their next Turn.

**Renewing Strike.** If you hit with this Attack, you may expend and roll one of your Hit Dice, and recover Hit Points equal to the amount rolled plus your Constitution Modifier. 

**Smashing Strike.** A Creature that takes Damage from this Attack is pushed 15 feet away from you; you may then move up to half your Speed directly towards that Creature without provoking Opportunity Attacks.
:
You may use this Feature a number of times equal to your Proficiency Bonus, and regain all expended uses whenever you finish a Short Rest.
:
**Press the Attack: Overbear (2 Aggression)** You may use this Press the Attack option when you make an Attack a Foecrusher (no Action required); that Attack deals 1d10 additional Damage, and you may choose a second Foecrusher Effect to apply to that Attack without expending a use of that Feature. You must choose a different Effect than the original.

<img 
  src='https://arknights.wiki.gg/images/1/12/Hoederer.png' 
  style='position:absolute;top:80px;right:-10px;width:400px' />

{{pageNumber,auto}}

\page

#### 10th Level: Divided when Conquered
The sweeping arc of your weapon demolishes ranks of your foes, cutting through the weak and unready with terrifying ease. You gain the following benefits:

**Additional Reach.** Heavy Melee Weapons you are wielding are considered to have the Reach Property.

**Horde Breaker.** When you reduce a Creature within your Reach to 0 Hit Points with a Melee Attack using a Heavy Melee Weapon, you may repeat that Attack against another Creature within your Reach and within 10 feet of the original Creature. However, if this Attack hits, do not roll Damage; instead, the Damage is equal to the excess Damage from the original Attack. If this Attack also reduces a Creature within your Reach to 0 Hit Points, you may continue to make these Attacks until you make an Attack that does not reduce a Creature to 0 Hit Points, there are no other Creatures within 10 feet of the last Creature you attacked, or you have no excess Damage remaining. 

#### 13th Level: Heroic Blow
When you make a Melee Attack during your Turn, you may expend a use of your Foecrusher Feature to make that Attack a Heroic Blow. If you do so, when you roll Damage for that Attack, you may reroll any amount of the dice, and use either result. Additionally, that Attack gains the Crush (X) Property, where X is equal to twice your Proficiency Bonus. If it already has the Crush (X) Property, add the values of X together and use that value for X.

#### 17th Level: Heroic Killing Blow
Your foes see your weapon in motion, and know that they are already dead. You gain the following benefits:

**Execution.** If you reduce a Creature to fewer than 50 Hit Points with a Heroic Blow, they die.

**Realmrending Blow.** Your Heroic Blows ignore Resistance to Physical Damage.

{{imageMaskEdge1,--offset:2%,--rotation:270
<img 
  src='https://arknights.wiki.gg/images/Ulpianus_Skin_1.png?e4baf1' 
  style='position:absolute;top:-130px;right:-370px;width:1300px' />
}}

{{imageMaskEdge1,--offset:17%,--rotation:0

  src='https://arknights.wiki.gg/images/Ulpianus_Skin_1.png?e4baf1'

{{pageNumber,auto}}

\page
### Duellist

Duellists excel at engaging singular foes, matching their strength and skill against the mightiest their enemies have to offer. They often range ahead of Defenders and other Guards to find their quarry, laying them low with powerful and precise strikes. 

#### 1st Level: Singular Foe
As a Duellist, you single out your foes before utterly dismantling them with precise strikes. You gain the following benefits:

**Duelling Blades.** Greatswords and Polearms are considered to have the Finesse Property while wielded by you. 

**Rules of Engagement.** As a Bonus Action, you may declare a hostile Creature you can see to be your Opponent. While you have an Opponent, you can earn Points in your duel with that Opponent; you can earn Points in the following ways, up to a maximum of 10:
- +1 Point when you declare your Opponent. Once you have gained Points in this way, you cannot do so again until the end of your next Turn.
  - You gain an additional Point if you are not wielding a Heavy Melee Weapon; this additional Point is lost if you equip a Heavy Melee Weapon after gaining the Point.
- +1 Point at the beginning of your Turn if you have dealt Damage to your Opponent since the beginning of your last Turn. 
  - You gain an additional Point if your Opponent has NOT dealt Damage to you since your last Turn.

{{align-text:left
When your Opponent is reduced to 0 Hit Points, you accept their surrender, a minute passes without either of you dealing Damage to the other, or you declare a new Opponent, you lose all Points you have earned.
:
You can expend your Points to carry out Engagements, detailed below. You cannot use the same Engagement more than once per Turn.
}}


#### 1st Level: Parry and Riposte
With a deft flick, you brush aside the clumsy blows of your foe. You gain the following benefits:

**Parry.** The first time each Round you use the Forestall Press the Attack option, it does not consume your Reaction, and costs 1 fewer Aggression (minimum of 0). 

**Press the Attack: Reposte (1 Aggression).** You may use this Press the Attack Option as a Reaction when a Creature within your Reach misses you with an Attack; make an Attack of Opportunity against that Creature.


\column



#### 4th Level: Principles of Defence
Parry and Counter; these are the principles that will keep you alive. You gain the following benefits:

**Every Victory, Another Step.** When your Opponent is reduced to 0 Hit Points or you accept their surrender, you regain 1 expended Aggression. 

**Press the Attack: Lightning Reflexes (1 Aggression).** You can use this Press the Attack Option as a Reaction when you would be required to make a Saving Throw against an effect you can see; you can instead make a Dexterity Saving Throw against that effect. 

**Punish every Opening.** Once per Turn, you may use your Press the Attack: Reposte Feature without expending your Reaction when you succeed on a Saving Throw if the Creature that caused you to make that Saving Throw is within your Reach. 

#### 10th Level: Duellist's Advance
The amount of Points you gain when you declare an Opponent is increased to 2. Additionally, the amount of Points you gain at the beginning of your Turn if you have dealt Damage to your Opponent since the beginning of your last Turn is increased to 2.

#### 13th Level: Grand Challenge
When you declare a Creature as your opponent, you may also declare a Grand Challenge (no Action required). For the next minute, or until your Opponent changes, dies, or surrenders, you gain the following benefits:

**Corps a Corps.** Whenever your Opponent deals Damage to a Creature that is not you, you may, if possible, make an Attack of Opportunity against them (no Action Required); you cannot do so again until the beginning of your next Turn.

**Displacement.** You are Resistant to all Damage dealt by Creatures other than your Opponent. 
:
When you hit your Opponent with a Melee Attack while you have 10 Points, you can make that Attack a Coup de Grâce;that Creature takes 8d8 Slashing or Piercing Damage (your choice) that is not reduced by Resistance or Damage Reduction, and the Grand Challenge ends; that Creature remains your Opponent if they survive.
:
Once you have used this Feature, you may not do so again until you complete a Long Rest.

#### 17th Level: Master Duellist
Your mind is no longer so singular as to consider every opponent a seperate challenge; the world is your opponent. When you declare a Creature as your Opponent but do not issue a Grand Challenge, or when your Opponent dies or surrenders, you no longer lose the Points you currently have. Additionally, declaring your Opponent during your Turn no longer requires your Bonus Action.

{{pageNumber,auto}}

\page


#### Engagements
The Engagements available to the Duellist Branch are listed in alphabetical order.
:
**Analysis (0 Points)** As an Action, you study a hostile Creature you can see, observing their movements. Once during your next Turn, if you gain any Points, you gain 1 additional Point.
:
**Balestra (2 Point)** You prepare to make your next Melee Attack before the end of your next Turn a powerful lunging strike (no Action required). The reach of that Melee Attack is increased by 10 feet; after you make the Attack, you move 10 feet towards the Creature, finishing the movement as close to them as you can. If the Creature was more than 10 feet away from you when you made the Attack, the Damage of that Attack is not decreased by Damage Reduction.



\column

**Derobement (1 Point)** When you make a Melee Attack against a Creature, you may attempt to confound and misdirect any retaliation they might offer (no Action required); the Damage of that Attack is halved, but if it hits, that Creature has Disadvantage on Attack Rolls against you until the start of your next Turn.
:
**Displacement (1 Point)** When you make a Melee Attack against a Creature, you can use your weapon to make space for yourself (no Action required); the Damage of that Attack is halved, but if it hits, you can can move up to 10 feet directly away from the Creature you attacked. This movement does not provoke Opportunity Attacks from that Creature.
:
**En Garde (1 Point)** When you expend your Reaction for your Lightning Reflexes or Reposte Press the Attack Option, you may use this Engagement (no Action required) to returning to a guarding stance, refunding use of your Reaction.
:
**Feint (1 Point)** As a Bonus Action, you mislead a Creature you can see on your next strike. The next Melee Attack you make against that Creature before the end of your next Turn has Advantage; that Creature must be able to see you to gain this benefit.
:
**Fleche (2 Points)** As a Bonus Action, you can make your next Melee Attack this Turn a Fleche; a precise and lethal strike to a weak point. That Attack deals additional Damage equal to your Guard Level. 
:
**Redoublement (1 Point)** When you miss a Creature with a Melee Attack, you can use the momentum of the attack to try again on a different angle; you can reroll that Attack Roll as a Reaction. However, if you do, the Damage of that Attack is halved.
:
**Score (1 Points).** When you make a Melee Attack against a Creature, you may attempt to score their flesh (no Action required); the Damage of that Attack is halved, but if it hits, it causes that Creature to suffer the Bleeding Condition.

{{imageMaskEdge1,--offset:1%,--rotation:90
<img 
  src='https://arknights.wiki.gg/images/1/18/Franka.png' 
  style='position:absolute;top:335px;right:-70px;width:1000px;clip-path:rect(auto auto 47% auto);' />
}}

{{imageMaskEdge1,--offset:20%,--rotation:0
<img 
  src='https://arknights.wiki.gg/images/1/18/Franka.png' 
  style='position:absolute;top:335px;right:-70px;width:1000px;clip-path:rect(auto auto 47% auto);' />
