{{pageNumber,auto}}

\page
### Instructor
An Instructor does not desire to be the centre of attention; they understand that victory can only be achieved through excellence not of a single person, but of a team. Instructors excel at helping that team excel, punctuating orders with punishing blows against the enemy.

#### 1st Level: Squad Leader
To you, coordinating the battle is just as important as fighting in it.
:
If you have taken the Attack Action during your Turn, you may take the Help Action as a Bonus Action. Additionally, when you use the Help Action to aid an ally in attacking a Creature, that Creature can be within 30 feet of you, rather than within 5 feet of you, so long as the Creature you are helping can see or hear you.

#### 1st Level: Instruction
You are talented in leading or training fighters of any stripe. You learn Instructions that are fuelled by special dice called Instruction Dice, detailed below under Instruction Options.  
<br>
**Instructions:** When you use the Help Action, you may also issue one Instruction to the same Creature you helped. Each Instruction expends 1 Instruction Die.
:
**Instruction Dice:** You have four Instruction Dice, which are d6s; you regain all expended Instruction Dice when you finish a Short or Long Rest. 
:
You gain an additional Instruction Die when you reach Guard Levels 7 (five dice total) and 14 (six dice total).

<img 
  src='https://arknights.wiki.gg/images/c/c1/Whislash.png' 
  style='position:absolute;top:620px;right:500px;width:150px' />

\column

#### 4th Level: Teach from Experience
Your Instruction is not purely theoretical; you have lived it. You gain the following benefits:

**From Experience.** At the beginning of your Turn during Combat, you may choose one of your Instructions; you gain the benefit of that Instruction. This does not count as issuing an Instruction.

**Press the Attack: Cooperative Combat (1 Aggression)** You can use this Press the Attack Action when you issue an Instruction to a Creature (no Action required); you also gain the benefit of that Instruction. Once you have benefitted from an Instruction in this way, you may not do so again until the beginning of your next Turn.

#### 10th Level: Kingmaker
Once per Turn, when you issue an Instruction, you may choose a second allied Creature within 30 feet of you and issue the same Instruction to them. This additional Instruction does not expend an Instruction Die; however, your Instruction Die is always a d6 for this second Instruction.

#### 13th Level: Superior Instruction
You lead from the front, and have become a renowned leader. You gain the following benefits:

**Improved Instruction Dice.** Your Instruction Dice become d10s. 

**Renowned Instructor.** When you roll Initiative and have less than half of your maximum Instruction Dice remaining, you regain Instruction Dice until you have half (rounding up) remaining. 

#### 17th Level: Once More Unto The Breach
Sometimes, one final push is all it takes; one moment of resolve. You can provide that moment. 
:
As an Action, you may use the Help Action against all allied Creatures within 60 feet that can see or hear you. You may issue an Instruction to each Creature you help; these Instructions do not consume Instruction Dice, but you cannot use your Kingmaker Feature to issue additional Instructions from these Instructions.
:
Once you have used this Feature, you cannot do so again until you have finished a Long Rest.

{{pageNumber,auto}}
\page
#### Instruction Options
The Instructions available to the Instructor Branch are listed in alphabetical order.
:
**Defend Yourself!** <br>
The AC of that Creature is increased by an amount equal to half the result of one roll of your Instruction Die until the end of their next Turn.
:
**Focus!** <br>
That Creature gains a Bonus to the next Skill Check it makes before the beginning of your next Turn equal to one roll of your Instruction Die.
:
**Get out of there!** <br>
That Creature may immediately move a distance equal to three rolls of your Instruction Die, rounded to the nearest 5. This movement does not provoke Opportunity Attacks from Creatures they were within the Reach of when they began this movement. 
:
**Go!** <br>
That Creature may immediately move a distance equal to half of their Speed + three rolls of your Instruction Die, rounded to the nearest 5.
:
**Hang in there!** <br>
When that Creature rolls a Death Saving Throw before the end of their next Turn, they gain a bonus to that Saving Throw equal to one roll of your Instruction Die. If the result of that Saving Throw is greater than 20, they gain the effect of rolling a 20 on the d20.
:
**Keep fighting!** <br>
The Creature gains Temporary Hit Points equal to one roll of your Instruction Die.
:
**Now's your chance!** <br>
That Creature gains a bonus to the next Attack Roll it makes before the end of its next Turn equal to one roll of your Instruction Die.
:
**Snap out of it!** <br>
If that Creature is currently under an effect requiring them to make a Saving Throw at the beginning or end of their Turn, they can immediately attempt that Saving Throw with a Bonus equal to one roll of your Instruction Die.
:
**Steel Yourself!** <br>
That Creature gains a Bonus to the next Saving Throw it makes within the next Minute equal to one roll of your Instruction Die.
:
**Strike!** <br>
That Creature may immediately expend its Reaction to make an Attack or cast a Cantrip with a Casting Time of an Action; that Attack or Cantrip deals additional Damage equal to one roll of your Instruction Die.


<img 
  src='https://arknights.wiki.gg/images/3/3f/Dobermann.png' 
  style='position:absolute;top:0px;right:0px;width:400px' />

{{pageNumber,auto}}

\page

### Liberator
Ideological grievance, repressed anger, longing, revenge; these are the weapons of a Liberator. To be a Liberator is to understand the value of considered action; to rage and scream accomplishes naught. The right moment, and the right time, can change the world.

#### 1st Level: Essence of the Liberator
You take action only when the moment is right. Until then, you watch and wait, providing what succor you can to those who rally to you. You gain the following benefits:
:
**Liberty.** You have a special resource called Liberty. Liberty is expended to fuel your other Features, and empower your Operator Skills. Your maximum Liberty is 6.
:
You can generate Liberty in the following ways. You can only generate Liberty during Combat.
- You gain 1 Liberty at the end of any of your Turns that you did not:
  - Activate an Operator Skill, or have an active Operator Skill.
  - Cast an Art.
  - Make an Attack Roll.
- You gain 1 Liberty when you use your Guide the Masses Feature as an Action.

{{align-text:left
**Guide the Masses.** As an Action while in Combat, you can choose an allied Creature you can see within 20 feet, and grant them one of the following bonuses (your choice) until the beginning of your next Turn; these effects are not cumulative. 
- **Advocate.** The next time that Creature gains Hit Points or Temporary Hit Points, they gain an additional amount equal to your twice Proficiency Bonus or twice your current Liberty, whichever is higher.
- **Empower.** That Creature gains Damage Reduction against the next Damage they take equal to your Proficiency Bonus or your current Liberty, whichever is higher.
- **Uplift.** That Creature's next successful Attack deals additional Damage equal to your Proficiency Bonus or your current Liberty, whichever is higher.
}}

{{align-text:left
Starting at 7th Level, if you have used this Feature as an Action during a Turn, you may also use it as a Bonus Action during that Turn, but you must choose a different Creature for that Bonus Action.
}}

\column

#### 1st Level: Righteous Outburst
All it takes to change history is for the right person to take action at the right time. Make sure that you do. You gain the following benefits:

**Flash of Anger.** When you hit a Creature with a Melee Weapon Attack, you can expend any amount (min. 1) of your Liberty to cause that Attack to deal additional 1d6 Damage for each Liberty expended; this additional Damage cannot be increased by Critical Hits. If a Creature is reduced to 0 Hit Points as a result of this Attack, 1 Liberty is refunded.

**The Right Moment.** When you activate an Operator Skill, all of your current Liberty is consumed. The amount of Liberty you consume in this way grants you the following benefits for the duration of that Skill:
- Operator Skills that force a Creature to make a Saving Throw during your Turn, and deal Damage, deal 1d6 additional Damage for every Liberty you consumed; this only applies to Damage dealt as a result of making that Saving Throw.
- When you make a Melee Weapon Attack, it deals 1d6 additional Damage for every 2 Liberty you consumed.

**Unique Operator Skills.** You have access to Operator Skills unique to this Branch, detailed below. When you gain a Basic, Advanced, or Elite Operator Skill, you may instead choose one from the corresponding Operator Skill Table for this Branch.

#### 4th Level: Rising Tide
The tide is rising, rising, ever rising.
:
Your Skills are considered to have the Automatic, Offensive, and Defensive Charging Method.


{{imageMaskCorner27,--offsetX:46%,--offsetY:-60%,--rotation:0
   <img 
  src='https://arknights.wiki.gg/images/Leizi_the_Thunderbringer_Elite_2.png?d9f7cb' 
  style='position:absolute;top:380px;right:-380px;width:1000px' />
}}

{{imageMaskEdge1,--offset:31%,--rotation:0
   <img 
  src='https://arknights.wiki.gg/images/Leizi_the_Thunderbringer_Elite_2.png?d9f7cb' 
  style='position:absolute;top:380px;right:-380px;width:1000px' />
}}

{{pageNumber,auto}}

\page


#### 10th Level: Praxis
You practice what you preach, seeking out those who oppose your vision and striking them down. You gain the following benefits:

**Far Reaching Guidance.** The range of your Guide the Masses Feature is increased to 40 feet.

**Improved Liberty.** The additional Damage granted by your Flash of Anger and The Right Moment Features is increased to a d10.

**Press the Attack: Face Me (2 Aggression)** You can use this Press the Attack Feature as a Bonus Action; you can cast the Compelled Duel Art using this Feature as part of that Bonus Action, ignoring its Somatic Components. If you do so, you use your Martial DC in place of your Spellcasting DC for that Art.

#### 13th Level: Smouldering Anger
You gain the following benefits:

**Cannot be Denied.** The additional Damage from your Flash of Anger Feature is now Force Damage. If you have an Operator Skill that deals an Arts Damage Type, you can cause your Flash of Anger Feature to deal that Damage Type instead of Force when you use it.

**Caught in the Crossfire.** When you use your Flash of Anger Feature, you can choose a different Creature within 10 feet of the Creature you attacked; that Creature also takes the additional Damage dealt by your Flash of Anger Feature to the original Creature.

#### 17th Level: Révolution
You have become an ideological avatar, the distilled essence of all you believe in. You have held your tongue for long enough.
:
As an Action during your Turn, you can release your Liberty in a singular powerful burst; all of your Liberty is expended. All hostile Creatures within 20 feet must make a Dexterity Saving Throw against your Martial DC or take 1d12 Force Damage for every Liberty expended in this way. You then gain the following benefits for the next 10 minutes.

**Champion of the Worthy.** Once on each of your Turns, if you have not used it that Turn you may use your Guide the Masses Feature without expending an Action. If you do so, you generate Liberty as if you had done so as an Action, and you may not do use that Feature as an Action that Turn.

**Shield of Belief.** At the beginning of each of your Turns, you gain Temporary Hit Points equal to your Guard Level.

**Time to Act.** Whenever you gain or are refunded Liberty, you gain one more.
:
Once you have used this Feature, you cannot do so again until you complete a Long Rest.

{{imageMaskEdge1,--offset:2%,--rotation:270
   <img 
  src='https://arknights.wiki.gg/images/d/df/M%C5%82ynar_Skin_1.png' 
  style='position:absolute;top:-150px;right:-430px;width:1300px' />
}}

\page
### Lord
A Lord seeks to impose their will; this is as true as it ever was. Lords employ a variety of techniques to ensure none are safe from their jurisdiction, no matter the distance.

#### 1st Level: Authoritative
A Lord doesn't think of themselves at being better than everyone else; they know that they are. They are destined to lead, by virtue of the fact that none can stop them.  You gain the following benefits:

**Overruled.** Whenever you are afflicted by an effect that requires you to make an Intelligence, Wisdom, or Charisma Saving Throw at the beginning or end of your Turn to end the effect, you may make that Saving Throw at the beginning and end of your Turn.

**Position of Power:** As a result of your actions, you can enter a special state known as having Authority. While you have Authority, your other Branch Features have additional effects, and at the beginning of each of your Turns, you can gain 1d8 Temporary Hit Points (no Action required).
:
You can gain Authority in the following ways:
- When an Elite or Leader Creature you have damaged since the beginning of your last Turn is reduced to 0 Hit Points.
- You reduce 2 or more Creatures to 0 Hit Points in the same Turn.
- You reduce the Hit Points of a Creature who is not missing any Hit Points to 0 in a single instance of damage.

{{align-text:left
You can lose Authority in the following ways:
- You are reduced to 0 Hit Points.
- Your Turn ends while you are afflicted by an effect that requires you to roll a Saving Throw during your Turn to end the effect. If that Saving Throw is rolled at the end of your Turn, you roll to determine if that effect ends before you see if you lose your Authority.
- You are Prone at the end of your Turn.
- You roll Initiative.
}}

**Press the Attack: Manifest Destiny (3 Aggression)** You can use this Press the Attack Option when you would lose Authority (no Action required); you do not lose Authority.

\column

#### 1st Level: Manifest Will
You can channel your will into strange Arts that turn even dull blades into lethal weapons. You gain the following benefits:

**Manifestation.** During your Turn, if it is not currently Manifested, you may Manifest your Will for 1 minute (no Action required). While your Will is Manifested: 
- Melee weapons wielded by you gain the Range (30/-) Property. 
- When you make a Ranged Attack using these weapons, you may use your Strength Modifier instead of your Dexterity for the Attack and Damage Rolls.
- Your Weapon Attacks deal additional Force Damage equal to your Charisma Modifier.

{{align-text:left
You may use this Feature a number of times equal to your Proficiency Bonus, and regain all expended uses when you complete a Long Rest.
}}

:
&nbsp;**Will Attunement.** You may change the Damage Type dealt by your Manifestation Feature by conducting a 10 minute long ritual, as if you were casting a Ritual Art. At the conclusion of the Ritual, you may choose a Damage Type that one of your Operator Skills can deal; this becomes the new Damage Type for your Manifestation Feature.
:
*Authority:* If you have Authority, your Ranged and Melee Weapon Attacks while your will is Manifested mark Creatures hit until the end of your next Turn with a Ranged or Melee Mark respectively. If you hit a marked Creature with a Weapon Attack that does not match the mark, that mark is expended, and that Attack deals 1d6 additional Damage of the same Type dealt by your Manifestation Feature.

{{imageMaskEdge1,--offset:0%,--rotation:270
  <img 
  src='https://arknights.wiki.gg/images/7/70/Lappland_Elite_2.png' 
  style='position:absolute;top:560px;right:-150px;width:700px;clip-path:rect(12% auto auto auto);' />
}}

{{imageMaskEdge1,--offset:33%,--rotation:0
  <img 
  src='https://arknights.wiki.gg/images/7/70/Lappland_Elite_2.png' 
  style='position:absolute;top:560px;right:-150px;width:700px;clip-path:rect(12% auto auto auto);' />
}}


{{pageNumber,auto}}

\page

#### 4th Level: King's Domain
It's a ruler's right to choose their hunting grounds. When you use your Manifestation Feature, you may choose one of the following effects to activate for the duration of that Manifestation:

- **Castle Doctrine.** Your will manifests into a 15-foot-radius sphere centred on the location you were when you used your Manifestation Feature. When a hostile Creature enters this area while you are in it, you may (as a Reaction) move up to half of your Speed towards them and make a Melee Weapon Attack against them. If the Creature is not an Elite or Leader Creature, and you hit them with the Attack, your Reaction is refunded.
:
*Authority:* If you hit a Creature with a Melee Attack made as a result of this Feature, you reduce their Speed by half until the end of their Turn.

- **Pursuit of Power.** Once per Turn, when you hit a Creature with a Weapon Attack, you may move up to your Movement Speed without provoking Opportunity Attacks; your Jump Distance is doubled when making this Movement.
:
*Authority:* This Feature is no longer limited to once per Turn.

\column

#### 10th Level: By Lordly Decree
Your belief in yourself is so potent that you truly can carry out miracles. You gain the following benefits: 

**Make it So.** When you make a d20 roll to which you would add an Ability Score Bonus, you can expend a use of your Manifestation Feature to  instead add your Charisma Bonus. If you are not Proficient in the roll, you are considered to be Proficient in it. 
:
*Authority:* If you are already Proficient in the roll, you instead are treated as having Expertise in that roll. This allows you to temporarily have Expertise in rolls that cannot normally have it, such as Saving Throws and Attack Rolls. 

**Press the Attack: A Leader Leads (1+ Authority)** You can use this Press the Attack Option when you gain Authority (no Action required); for each Aggression you expend, choose one allied Creature that can see or hear you. That Creature gains a Special Inspiration that expires after 1 minute. 

{{imageMaskEdge1,--offset:-2%,--rotation:0
<img 
  src='https://arknights.wiki.gg/images/Thorns_Skin_2.png?262877' 
  style='position:absolute;top:310px;right:-100px;width:1200px;clip-path:rect(17% auto auto auto);' />
}}

{{imageMaskEdge1,--offset:1%,--rotation:270

  src='https://arknights.wiki.gg/images/Thorns_Skin_2.png?262877' 
  style='position:absolute;top:310px;right:-100px;width:1200px;clip-path:rect(7% auto auto auto);' />
}}


{{pageNumber,auto}}

\page

#### 13th Level: Force of Will
Your strange arts have found new, explosive expression. 
:
You gain one of the following benefits based on the Damage Type of your Manifestation Feature. When you change the Damage Type of your Manifestation Feature, the benefit you gain from this Feature also changes. You can only use this Feature once per Turn.


| Damage Type       | Effect| 
|:-----------------:|:-----:|
| Fire, Psychic, Force, Thunder | When you hit a Creature with a Weapon Attack, you can cause your Arts to surge through them with explosive effect. Creatures within 10 feet of that Creature (except yourself) must make a Dexterity Saving Throw against your Martial DC. On a failed Save, they take 3d6 Damage of the same Type dealt by your Manifestation Feature; on a successful Save, they take half as much Damage.|
| Bludgeoning, Piercing, Slashing, Radiant | As an Action, you can fire a slash of Arts energy in a 5-foot-wide, 50-foot-long line originating from you. Creatures within the area must make a Dexterity Saving Throw against your Martial DC. On a failed Save, they take 8d6 Damage of the same Type dealt by your Manifestation Feature; on a successful Save, they take half as much Damage.|
| Acid, Cold, Lightning, Necrotic, Poison | When you hit a Creature with a Melee Weapon Attack, you can channel your will into the blow, dealing a mortal blow. That Creature must make a Constitution Saving Throw against your Martial DC, taking 6d6 Damage of the same Type dealt by your Manifestation Feature on a failed Save, or half as much Damage on a successful Save.

{{align-text:left
Irrespective of which benefit you gain, you can only use this Feature a number of times equal to your Proficiency Bonus, and regain all expended uses when you complete a Long Rest.
}}

\column

#### 17th Level: Divine Right
Does your authority come from divine providence? No, of course not; but if it appears to, then it is the same thing. The Authority benefits of your other Features are improved:
- **Manifestation.** When you deal additional Damage as a result of a Ranged or Melee Mark, you can cause all other Creatures of your choice within 10 feet of the original Creature to also suffer that Damage.
- **Castle Doctrine.** When you hit a Creature with a Melee Attack made as a result of Castle Doctrine, they must make a Wisdom Saving Throw against your Martial DC. On a failed Save, you reduce their Speed to 0 until the end of next Turn. On a successful Save, their Speed is halved until the end of their Turn.
- **Pursuit of Power.** When you are moving as a result of this Feature, hostile Creatures that you move within 5 feet of take Damage equal to your Charisma Modifier of the same type as your Manifestation Feature. A Creature can only take this Damage once per Turn.
- **Make it So.** If you fail the roll after using this Feature, you may reroll it, but must use the new result.

<img 
  src='https://arknights.wiki.gg/images/SilverAsh_Elite_2.png?7d325c' 
  style='position:absolute;top:310px;right:-230px;width:1000px;clip-path:rect(17% auto auto auto);' />

{{pageNumber,auto}}

\page
### Musha
Fuelled by battle, Musha stand alone against the tide. They cannot, will not, rely on others to support them, trusting only in strength of arms and strength of will to see them through the day.<br>

#### 1st Level: Tenacity
The closer to death you are, the harder you fight. You gain the following benefits:

**Nothing to Lose.** If you have equal to or less than 50% of your maximum Hit Points remaining, you can make a Melee Weapon Attack as a Bonus Action. If you have equal to or less than 10% of your maximum Hit Points remaining, you can instead make two Melee Weapon Attacks as part of this Bonus Action. 

**Soloblade.** When you hit a Creature with a Melee Attack, you can recover 2d6 Hit Points. This amount increases to 2d8 at 7th Level, and 2d10 at 14th Level.

**Stand Alone.** When another Creature would restore Hit Points to you, you instead gain Temporary Hit Points equal to the Hit Points you would have recovered.

\column

#### 1st Level: Enduring Resolve
To live and fight so close to the edge is to spit in the eye of death. To fall, is to fail. You gain the following benefits:

**Additional Saving Throw Proficiency.** You gain Proficiency in Wisdom Saving Throws.

**Not Yet.** When you would be reduced to 0 Hit Points but not killed outright by a hostile Action, you can make a DC10 Wisdom Saving Throw. If you succeed, you survive with Hit Points equal to twice your Proficiency Bonus instead. If you had less Hit Points than this value before the Damage, you recover Hit Points until you have that value. Each time you use this Feature, the DC of the Saving Throw increases by 5. When you finish a Long Rest, the DC resets to 10.



<img 
  src='https://arknights.wiki.gg/images/f/f6/Hellagur_Skin_1.png' 
  style='position:absolute;top:300px;right:-420px;width:1500px' />




{{pageNumber,auto}}

\page

#### 4th Level: Blood Price
Blood and life is a currency, to be spent in pursuit of one’s goals. Musha are just more willing to spend big. When you use any option from this Feature, you lose 2d8 Hit Points; this cannot be reduced in any way, and you may not expend Temporary Hit Points in this manner. Additionally, you cannot gain Hit Points through your Soloblade Feature for any Attack you make as part of an Option from this Feature.

**Center Oneself.** Someone willing to trade away their own life cannot be swayed by lesser woes. With a steadying breath, you force away such impediments. As an Action, you can end one of the following Conditions on yourself if the duration on that Condition is less than 24 hours: Charmed, Dazed, Frightened, Paralyzed, Stunned. If the duration is longer than a minute, you instead do not suffer from that Condition for the next minute. You can use this Action even if an effect would prevent you from taking Actions.

**Dig Deep.** You pull power from deep within, strengthening your here-and-now with a loan from tomorrow. As an Action, you can gain Skill Points equal to the Hit Points you lose to use this Action.

**Iai.** In a single motion, you draw your weapon and strike your enemy. As an Action, draw a Melee Weapon you are carrying that you have enough available hands to make Attacks with. Then, you move up to half your Speed in a straight line; you can move through the space of other Creatures during this movement, and do not provoke Opportunity Attacks. Make a Melee Attack using the weapon you drew against a Creature you passed through as if they were within your Reach; this Attack is made at Advantage, and deals additional Damage equal to half of the Hit Points you expended to use this Action.

**Ichimonji.** A deadly blow, delivered from a high stance, sends foes reeling. As an Action, make a Melee Weapon Attack against a Creature within your Reach.  On a hit, the Attack deals additional Damage equal to the Hit Points you expended to use this Action, and the Creature is knocked off balance; until the end of the Turn, you have Advantage on Melee Attacks against that Creature.


#### 10th Level: Warrior Spirit
Kill or be killed, win or be forgotten. This is the code of the Musha; you gain the following benefits:

**Go Forth.** When a Creature makes an Attack against you, you may use your Reaction to move up to your Speed towards that Creature.

**Press the Attack: Retaliator (2 Aggression).** You may use this Press the Attack Option (no Action required) after you have used your Go Forth Feature, if the triggering Creature is within your Reach after the movement; make a Melee Attack against the triggering Creature. Alternatively, you may use your Iai Feature, but must Attack the triggering Creature as part of it.
 
#### 13th Level: Found Alongside Death
As death approaches, your reflexes sharpen; the reaper will have to work hard to claim you. You gain the following benefits:

**Greater Blood Price.** When you use your Blood Price Feature, you may choose to lose twice as many Hit Points instead.

**Living on the Edge.** If you have equal to or less than 50% of your maximum Hit Points remaining, your AC is increased by a value equal to half of your Proficiency Bonus, rounding up; you additionally recieve a Bonus on Saving Throws against effects you can see equal to this value. If you have equal to or less than 10% of your maximum Hit Points remaining, your AC is instead increased by a value equal to your Proficiency Bonus; you additionally recieve a Bonus on Saving Throws against effects you can see equal to this value. 

 
#### 17th Level: To Laugh at Death
An undying body, an unyielding soul.
:
The DC of your Not Yet Feature is now reset to 10 whenever your Hit Points are equal to your Maximum Hit Points. Additionally, your Not Yet Feature now functions against effects that would kill you outright as long as your body is not destroyed.


<img 
  src='https://arknights.wiki.gg/images/Akafuyu.png?b7d3c2' 
  style='position:absolute;top:465px;right:-100px;width:600px' />



{{pageNumber,auto}}

{{wide
### Reaper
Where the eponymous Reapers walk, death is soon to follow. Reapers forgo grace and technique for raw might, building from a single death knell to a roaring crescendo of violence as the battle drags on.

#### 1st Level: Reaper's Ruin
You are empowered by grinding conflict; as the battle continues, and your body count grows, your furor intensifies, represented by your Ruin.
:
You gain 1 Ruin whenever you score a Critical Hit, or reduce a Creature to 0 Hit Points. You lose 1 Ruin at the end of your Turn if you did not deal Damage to a hostile Creature. You lose all Ruin if you are reduced to 0 Hit Points.
:
You gain a number of benefits based on your current Ruin, as shown in the "Effects" column of the Reaper Table; these effects are cumulative, and so you gain all previous effects in addition to the current one.
:
However, as your Ruin climbs, it becomes more difficult to heal you, not least of all because of the difficulty of identifying who's blood is who's. When another Creature attempts to restore Hit Points to you, the amount of Hit Points they restore is reduced by the value shown in the "Healing Recieved" column of the Reaper Table, corresponding to your current level of Ruin.
:
**Push the Attack: Lingering Violence (1 Aggression)** You can use this Press the Attack Option when your Ruin would decay by 1 as a result of you not damaging a hostile Creature (no Action required); it does not decay.


}}


{{wide
##### Reaper Table
| Ruin | Effect | Reap Critical Range (Level 4+) | Curtainfall Critical Range (Level 4+) | Healing Recieved |
|:----:|:-----:|:----:|:-----:|:----:|
|0| -| +10  | +0 | 0 |
|
|1| -| +9 | +1 | -2 |
|
|2| When you roll a 1 on a result for a Damage die, you may reroll that result but must use the new result. | +8 | +2 | -4 |
|
|3|- | +7 | +3 | -6
|
|4| Your movement does not provoke Opportunity Attacks from non-Elite or Leader Creatures. | +6 | +4 |-8
|
|5|- | +5 | +5 | -10
|
|6| You can move through the space of hostile Creatures, though you may not finish a movement in the same space as another; they are considered Difficult Terrain. | +4 | +6 | -12
|
|7|- | +3 | +7 | -14
|
|8| When you roll to determine Damage, you may make that roll an additional time and choose either total. | +2 | +8 | -16
|
|9| - | +1 | +9 | -18
|
|10| If one of your Attacks, or your Reap Feature reduces a Creature to less Hit Points than a value 5 + your Guard Level, it dies. | + 0 | +10 | -20

}}

<img 
  src='https://arknights.wiki.gg/images/Entelechia_Elite_2.png?43525d' 
  style='position:absolute;top:430px;right:-0px;width:1000px' />


{{pageNumber,auto}}

\page

#### 1st Level: Death's Design
Your canvas is the battlefield, your paint death and destruction, and you are an adept artist indeed. You gain two options you can perform in place of the Attack Action.

**Reap.** While you are wielding a Melee Weapon, you can swing it in a 15-foot cleaving cone originating from you; all Creatures within that area must make on a Dexterity Saving Throw against your Martial DC. Roll the Damage of the weapon you used for the swing, as if you had hit with an Attack; any bonus that would increase or modify the Damage of that Attack are applied, such as the Deadly or Crush (X) Properties. On a failed Save, Creatures take half of the rolled Damage; on a successful Save, they take no Damage. 

**Curtainfall.** While you are wielding a Melee Weapon, you can make an Attack against a Creature within your Reach. If that Attack hits, that Attack deals 1d6 additional Damage to that Creature only for each Ruin you have; this additional Damage is not multiplied by a Critical Hit. After you have dealt Damage in this way, all of your Ruin is expended.


#### 4th Level: Crescendo of Violence
Like a orchestral masterpiece, you start slow and build up to the apex of your violent art. You gain the following benefits:

**Critical Death's Design.** Both of your Death's Design options can now Critically Hit. When you use Reap, roll a d20 as if you were making an Attack with the weapon used for Reap; if that Attack would result in a Critical Hit, Reap is considered to have Critically Hit all Creatures within the area. For Curtainfall, a Critical Hit on the Attack Roll causes Curtainfall to Critically Hit also. Neither option has its Damage multiplied on a Critical Hit, instead gaining the following bonuses on a Critical Hit
- **Reap.** Any non-Elite, non-Leader Creatures hit by that Reap are considered to be Vulnerable to its Damage.
- **Curtainfall.** The Damage dealt by this Attack ignores Resistance to Physical Damage.

{{align-text:left
**Critical Scaling.** The Critical Range of your Reap and Curtainfall Actions is increased based on your current level of Ruin, as shown in the Reaper Table.

}}

{{note
##### Reaper and Hordes
Reaper is a Branch that cares about killing hostile Creatures perhaps more than any other. As such, the reccomendation for Reaper interacting with Swarm is that the max Hit Points of the Creature that comprises the Swarm is considered the Threshold for earning "kills". If they deal more than this Threshold in a single instance of Damage, they have "killed" 1 Creature; if that instance is double that Threshold, they have "killed" 2 Creatures, and so on.
}}



\column

#### 10th Level: Fear the Reaper
Even for your allies, watching you work can be a perturbing sight. For your enemies, to know that they are next can be too much to bear. 
:
As a Bonus Action, you can mark a hostile Creature you can see with your Reaper's Mark for 1 hour. When a Creature with your Reaper's Mark sees or hears you gain Ruin, they take 1d6 Psychic Damage; Creatures that are immune to the Frightened Condition do not take this Damage. Additionally, you can always sense the direction to Creature with your Reaper's Mark, and if that Creature is moving, you know the direction of its movement. Finally, you can see a Creature with your Deathmark even if it has the Invisible Condition.
:
Once you have used this Feature, you cannot do so again until you complete a Long Rest.

#### 13th Level: Reaper Prime
When you use your Reap Action, you can choose the area it affects; a 15-foot cone, or a 10-foot-radius Sphere centered on you. Additionally, when you roll to determine the Damage of your Reap Feature, it is no longer halved.

#### 17th Level: Memento Mori
When a Creature with your Reaper's Mark takes Psychic Damage from your Mark, you can choose one of the following effects (no Action required)
- They are considered to be Vulnerable to that instance of Damage, and it ignores any Damage Reduction they have against it.
- Other hostile Creatures within 10 feet of the Creature with your Reaper's Mark also take 1d6 Psychic Damage.

<img 
  src='https://arknights.wiki.gg/images/a/a5/La_Pluma.png' 
  style='position:absolute;top:575px;right:0px;width:475px' />

{{pageNumber,auto}}

\page
{{pageNumber,auto}}

### Weaponmaster
A Weaponmaster is as much at home with a simple bludgeon as an artisanal blade. In their hands, any martial armament becomes a rapid dealer of death, cutting like winter wind.

#### 1st Level: Art of War
In your hands, all but the heaviest weapons flow like wind, and cut like water. You gain the following benefits:

**Additional Fighting Styles.** You gain a Fighting Style available to the Guard or Marksman Class. Whenever you complete a Long Rest, you may exchange this Fighting Style for a different one.

**Additional Weapon Proficiencies.** You are Proficient in all Light Ranged and Heavy Ranged Weapons.

**Martial Mastery.** Your Proficiency with all kinds of weapons provides you with a versatility in their use unavailable to the layman. Weapons that you are wielding, and are Proficient with, gain additional benefits according to the Martial Mastery Table.  

##### Martial Mastery
| Mastery Property  | Affected Weapons | Additional Effect |
|:-----------------:|:----------------:|:-----------------:|
| Can Opener        | Crossbow, Hammer, Heavy Crossbow, Heavy Hammer, Rifle | When you hit a Creature that has Physical DR, that Attack deals an additional 1d6 Damage. | 
| Cornering         | Bow, Greatbow, Machine Gun, Nailgun | When you hit a Creature, that Creature's Speed is reduced by 5 feet until the end of their next Turn. 
| Drive             | Spear, Polearm, Whip        | When you hit a Creature, you can move that Creature up to 10 feet in a direction of your choice; this movement does not provoke Opportunity Attacks.
| Executioner       | Knife, Shotgun   | When you hit a Creature that has the Incapacitated or Prone Condition, that Attack deals an additional 2d6 Damage. |
| Flourish          | Sword, Greatsword, Heavy Throwing Disc, Throwing Disc | When you make an Attack with this Weapon and miss, you may roll 1d6 and add the total to your result, potentially turning that miss into hit. Once you have hit as a result of this Feature, you may not do so again until the start of your next Turn. 
| Lethality         | Axe, Greataxe, Hand Crossbow, Handgun | When you hit a Creature that does not have any Physical DR, that attack deals an additional 1d6 Damage. |

\column

#### 1st Level: Dualstrike

As comfortable with one weapon as with two, you move with a kinaesthetic grace that onlookers can only hope to imitate. You gain the following benefits:

**Fast Hands.** You can Interact with Object an additional time on each of your Turns, but you can only use this to draw or stow weapons.

**Steelwind.** When you take the Attack Action while wielding weapons you are capable of Two-Weapon Fighting with, you can make the additional Attack from Two-Weapon Fighting as part of that Action, instead of as a Bonus Action.





{{imageMaskEdge4,--offset:5%,--rotation:270
 <img 
  src='https://arknights.wiki.gg/images/f/fe/Irene_Elite_2.png' 
  style='position:absolute;top:215px;right:-310px;width:1000px' />
}}


\page
{{pageNumber,auto}}

{{wide

#### 4th Level: Judgement Cut
When you hit a Creature with a Melee Attack, you can mark them for Judgement Cut as a Bonus Action. Judgement Cut ends at the end of your second Turn after activating this Feature, or until when you end it early during your Turn by stowing a Weapon. For the duration, keep track of the total Weapon Damage you deal to that Creature; when the Mark ends or is ended by you, that Creature takes Slashing Damage equal to half of the total Weapon Damage they received from you in that time.
:
Once you have used this Feature, you may not do so again until you complete a Short or Long Rest.

#### 10th Level: A Master's Weapon
You are familiar with almost all weaponry, but some just feel *right* in your hands. As a Long Rest Action, you can spend time training and familiarising yourself with every foible of a weapon in your possession. At the end of that Long Rest, that Weapon is considered to be your Signature Weapon. You can only have one Signature Weapon at a time; performing this Action on a second weapon removes the Signature Weapon status from the first. Your Signature Weapon gains the following benefits when wielded by you;
- **Every Part of the Weapon.** As a Bonus Action, you can change the Additional Effect on that Weapon granted by your Martial Mastery Feature to another Additional Effect until the end of the Turn.
- **In Trained Hands.** You have a +1 bonus to Attack and Damage Rolls with that Weapon.

#### 13th Level: Untouchable
It is a fool's errand to even attempt to harm you by conventional means.
:
When a Creature within your Reach makes an Attack Roll against you, you may, as a Reaction, make a Melee Attack against them using a weapon you are wielding. If your Attack deals Damage to that Creature, their Attack automatically misses. 
:
You may use this Feature twice, and regain all expended uses when you complete a Long Rest.

#### 17th Level: Song of Steel
The cacophony of war and battle flows through you; every sinew feels its vibrations, and sings its song. All you need to do is unleash it.
:
You may use this Feature as a Bonus Action; for the next minute, your Weapon Attacks have +1 Critical Range, and on a Critical Hit, you ignore any Damage Resistance that Creature has to Physical Damage. Additionally, Attacks with your Signature Weapon are made at Advantage for the duration.
:
Once you have used this Feature, you may not do so again until you complete a Long Rest.

}}

{{imageMaskEdge1,--offset:12%,--rotation:0
 <img 
  src='https://arknights.wiki.gg/images/8/8c/Degenbrecher_Elite_2.png?20231205044332' 
  style='position:absolute;top:300px;right:-210px;width:1300px' />
}}


\page

## Marksman
A Sankta grins as she leaps down from a rooftop, landing with a roll. Her weapon is already up before her feet are stable; Reunion irregulars pause in their tracks at her sudden appearance. She blows a strand of shaggy red hair away from her face, reaching into the etched Originium circuits of her patron firearm with her mind; a moment's thought, and with a lion's roar, bullets course into the stunned thugs to lethal effect.
:
A Lupo treks through barren, Catastrophe-ravaged wastes. Calm, alert eyes scan the terrain for any sign of life; nimble feet move whisper quiet through the rock and sand. An unknown sound catches upon her canine ears, freezing her to the spot- a burdenbeast, horrifically mutated by Originium, lowing angrily at a free-standing tree. She follows the Creature's gaze, spotting a young Sarkaz girl within the barren branches, horror and terror written plain across her face. With a flick, her crossbow is up, her sights trained. Before the bolt has even hit home, her hands flash to her pouch for her spare rebreather; there might be hope for the child yet.
:
{{toExcludeH3
### Hunters, Assassins, Heroes
}}
In many ways, all that ties most Marksmen together is their choice of weapon. The width and breadth of those under the Marksman's umbrella cannot be overstated. To be a Marksman is to have a proclivity towards ranged combat, and ultimately says little about character or nature in the same way other professions might.
:
Marksmen are exceptionally common; every war ever fought has involved ranged combat, every rural settlement has a hunter, or at least drive off threats. Thus, to be a Marksman of note is to rise above the dross, to be a name worth knowing. Everyone needs a Marksman; why not be the best?

{{tocExcludeH3 
### Class Features
}}

As a Marksman, you gain the following Class Features:

#### Hit Points
**Hit Dice:** 1d8 per Marksman Level<br>
**Hit Points at 1st Level:** 8 + your Constitution Modifier<br>
**Hit Points at Higher Levels:** 1d8 (or 5) + your Constitution Modifier per Marksman Level after 1st

#### Proficiencies
**Armor:** Light Armour, Medium Armour<br>
**Weapons:** Light Melee, Light Ranged, Heavy Ranged, Light Shields<br>
**Tools:** A Tool Kit/Vehicle Proficiency of your Choice<br>
**Saving Throws:** Dexterity + one of your choice from the following: Wisdom, Charisma <br>
**Skills:** Three from: Acrobatics, Athletics, Perception, Sleight of Hand, Stealth, Wilderness

\column

#### Equipment
You start with the following Equipment, in addition to any Equipment granted by your Background. 

- **A)** 1x Light Ranged Weapon OR **B)** 1x Heavy Ranged Weapon OR **C)** 1x Grenade Launcher
- **A)** 1x Light Melee Weapon OR **B)** 1x Light Shield
- **A)** Protective Clothing OR **B)** Underarmour
- 1x Tool Kit you are Proficient with

OR

- 1300 LMD

| Level | Proficiency Bonus | Features |    
|:-----:|:-----------------:|:--------:|
|  1st  |  +2  | Elite 0- Fighting Style, High Ground, Marksman Branch, Target Lock     |
|  2nd  |  +2  | Basic Operator Skill  |
|  3rd  |  +2  | Basic Talent  |
|  4th  |  +2  | Ability Score Improvement, Branch Feature  |
|  5th  |  +3  | Extra Attack |
|  6th  |  +3  | Ability Score Improvement  |
|  7th  |  +3  | Elite 1- Advanced Operator Skill     |
|  8th  |  +3  | Ability Score Improvement, Basic Talent Module   |
|  9th  |  +4  | Advanced Talent     |
| 10th  |  +4  | Ability Score Improvement, Branch Feature   |
| 11th  |  +4  | Enhanced Target Lock      |
| 12th  |  +4  | Ability Score Improvement   |
| 13th  |  +5  | Advanced Talent Module      |
| 14th  |  +5  | Elite 2- Elite Operator Skill |
| 15th  |  +5  | Branch Feature |

| 17th  |  +6  | Unerring  |

| 19th  |  +6  | Ability Score Improvement      |
| 20th  |  +6  | Steel Rain      |

\page
{{pageNumber,auto}}

#### 1st Level: Marksman Branch
You choose a Marksman Branch which most closely fits the style you envision for yourself. Choose one from the following list; further details on these Branches can be found at the end of the Class Description. The Branch you choose grants you Features at Level 1, 4, 10, and 15.
- **Anti-Air:** With a focus on rapid attacks, Anti-Air Marksmen are well equipped to engage nearly any target; but especially aerial ones.
- **Artilleryman:** Wielding heavy explosive launchers, Artillerymen are slow to strike. When they do, however, swathes fall before them like chaff.
- **Besieger:** Besiegers specialise in engaging heavy targets at long range, penetrating through hefty defences with measured, considered shots. No armour is proof from a Besieger. 
- **Deadeye:** A Deadeye's sights inevitably fall on the most dangerous foes within the ranks of their enemies, slowly delivering shots with unerring accuracy and massive damage. 
- **Flinger:** Using highly specialised Arts, Flingers do not use conventional Ranged Weapons at all. In their hands, anything can be a ranged weapon.
- **Heavyshot:** Heavyshot Marksmen specialise in single, decisive blows at the cost of other strengths. They customise their weapons to better facilitate their lethality, creating monstrous weapons that can only be wielded by them. 
- **Hunter:** A Hunter uses a special system to empower their weapons, lasting for a certain number of shots. When their charge is expended, they retreat and recharge, waiting for another moment to strike at their prey.
- **Spreadshot:** Spreadshot Marksmen, as their name would imply, spread their love around. Wielding shotguns, nailguns, or even more regular weapons, they are most alive against clustered groups of enemies.

<img 
  src='https://arknights.wiki.gg/images/May.png?49e31e' 
  style='position:absolute;top:687px;right:320px;width:500px;;clip-path:rect(auto auto 47% auto);' />

\column

#### 1st Level: High Ground
You are at your best when raining hate onto foes beneath you. You have Advantage on Attack Rolls against Creatures 10 or more feet below you.

#### 1st Level: Fighting Style
You adopt a particular style of fighting as your speciality. Choose one of the following options (You can't take a Fighting Style option more than once, even if you later get to choose again).

##### Braced for Action
Once per Turn, when you make a Damage Roll for a Weapon with the Brace Keyword, you may reroll one of those dice.

##### Close and Personal
You do not have Disadvantage on Ranged Attack Rolls when there is a hostile Creature within 5 feet of you.

##### Cone of Fire
Whenever you make an Attack using the Spread Quality, you may deal the additional Damage even if the Attack misses. 

##### Deadly Fighter
The first time each Turn you make an Attack Roll using a Weapon with the Deadly Property, the Critical Range of that Attack is increased by 1.

##### Defensive
When you are hit by an Attack while you have a Melee Weapon or Shield equipped, you may use your Reaction to increase your AC by 1d4 for the duration of that Attack, possibly causing that Attack to miss.

##### Ranger
You gain a +2 Bonus to Attack Rolls you make with Ranged or Thrown Weapons.

##### Smooth Operator
When you make an Attack as a Bonus Action using the Rapid Fire Property, you may add your Ability Modifier to the Damage Roll of the additional Attack(s). 

##### Two-Weapon Fighting
When you engage in Two-Weapon Fighting, you can add your Ability Modifier to the Damage of the additional Attack(s), and you may draw or stow two one-handed weapons when you would normally be able to draw or stow only one.



\page
{{pageNumber,auto}}

#### 1st Level: Target Lock
The benefit of ranged combat is the freedom to place your shots where they are most needed. During your Turn, you can select a hostile Creature you can see to be your Target (no Action required) for the next minute. You have a +1 bonus to Attack Rolls against your Target.
:
Once you have used this Feature, you cannot use it again until you have finished a Short Rest.

#### 2nd Level: Basic Operator Skill
You learn a Basic Operator Skill from the Basic Marksman Skills table, or from any other Basic Skill table available to you.

#### 3rd Level: Basic Talent
You gain a Basic Talent of your choice from the Basic Marksman Talent table. When you reach 8th Level, you may either pick a different Basic Talent to gain, or upgrade your Basic Talent, gaining its Module Effect.

#### 4th Level: Ability Score Improvement
When you reach 4th level, and again at 6th, 8th, 10th, 12th, 16th, and 19th level, you can increase one Ability Score of your choice by 2, or you can increase two Ability Scores of your choice by 1. As normal, you can’t increase an Ability Score above 20 using this feature.

#### 5th Level: Extra Attack
You can attack twice instead of once whenever you take the Attack Action during your Turn. 

#### 7th Level: Advanced Operator Skill
You learn an Advanced Operator Skill from the Advanced Marksman Skills table, or from any other Advanced Skill table available to you.

#### 9th Level: Advanced Talent
You gain an Advanced Talent of your choice from the Advanced Marksman Talent table. When you reach 13th Level, you may either pick a different Advanced Talent to gain, or upgrade your Advanced Talent, gaining its Module Effect.

#### 12th Level: Enhanced Target Lock
Your target escaping you is unthinkable. You gain the following benefits:

**Master Marksman.** The bonus granted by your Target Lock Feature is increased to +2.

**Target Acquisition.** You regain use of your Target Lock Feature when you roll Initative.

\column

#### 14th Level: Elite Operator Skill
You learn an Elite Operator Skill from the Elite Marksman Skills table, or from any other Elite Skill table available to you.

#### 17th Level: Unerring
When you miss with an Attack, you may choose to hit instead (your result is treated as the minimum required to hit for any effects that require it). Once you have done so, you may not do so again until you have completed a Short or Long Rest.

#### 20th Level: Steel Rain
Rain death upon them.
:
Once per Short Rest, you may take the Attack Action as a Bonus Action.

  style='position:absolute;top:340px;right:100px;width:220px' />

|:-------------:|:-----:|:------------:|
| One With Shadow	| When a Creature makes an Attack against you, and doesn't have Advantage on the Attack Roll, you can use your Reaction to give that Attack Roll Disadvantage. You can use this Feature a number of times equal to your Proficiency Bonus, and regain all expended uses when you complete a Long Rest.	| You can now use this Feature even when the Attack has Advantage.
| Opportunist	| Attacks you make against Creatures with reduced Speed deal additional Damage equal to your Proficiency Bonus.	| You may also deal this additional Damage against Creatures that have any detrimental Status Condition.
| Overwhelming Strike	| When you roll a 20 on the d20 for an Attack Roll, you can deal additional Damage to the target equal to half of the Ability Score used for that Attack. The Damage Type is the same as one present on that Attack (your Choice, if there are multiple).	| The additional Damage becomes equal to the Ability Score used for that Attack.
| Part-Time Inventor	| You gain the Equipment Modification Feature of the Inventor Specialist. However, your maximum number of Inventions is 1, and you cannot apply any Modification marked as Dangerous.	| Your maximum number of Inventions becomes 2.
| Reliable Talent	| When you make a Check using a Skill you are Proficient in, treat any result on the d20 of 8 or less as an 8.	| You cannot have Disadvantage on any Check using a Skill or Tool you are Proficient with.
| Sniper's Intuition	| When a Creature hits you with an Attack, you can expend your Reaction to halve the Damage you take from that Attack. Once you have used this Feature, you cannot do so again until you have finished a Short Rest.	| You can now use this Feature twice, and regain all expended uses when you complete a Short or Long Rest.
| Tactical Mind	| When you roll a d20, you may reroll that die and add 1d10 to the result of the roll as a Reaction. Once you have used this Feature, you must finish a Long Rest before you can use it again.	| You now regain use of this Feature when you complete a Short Rest.
| Weighted Shots	| The first time you deal Damage to a Creature with an Attack, that Creature's Movement Speed is reduced by 15 feet until the end of their Turn. It is considered to be the first time you hit a Creature if you have rolled Initiative since the last time you hit them with an Attack.	| Their Speed is reduced by 30 feet instead.

}}

{{imageMaskEdge1,--offset:5%,--rotation:0
<img 
  src='https://arknights.wiki.gg/images/2/20/GA-Actus_Fidei.png?8d8158' 
  style='position:absolute;top:550px;right:-100px;width:1000px' />
}}

\page
{{pageNumber,auto}}

### Anti-Air
Some, upon hearing the name, expect Anti-Air Marksmen to be narrow specialists. In truth, they could not be more wrong; it is a title they bear because their versatility and sheer weight of fire allows them to engage those difficult targets as efficiently as any other target. Whether attacking ground or air, the attacks of an Anti-Air Marksman are rapid and punishing.

#### 1st Level: Weight of Fire
Quantity has a quality all of its own. 
:
If you take the Attack Action during your Turn and have Advantage on a Ranged Attack Roll you make, you can forgo the Advantage for that Roll to repeat that Ranged Attack against the same Creature as part of the same action. You may only use this Feature once per Turn.

#### 1st Level: Air Tracking
You are already well versed in tracking fast moving aerial targets. You gain the following benefits:

**Pigeon Shooting.** Your Ranged Attacks against Creatures that are not in contact with the ground, or moved 60 or more feet during their last Turn, are made at Advantage. 

**Shifting Focus.** If your Target is reduced to 0 Hit Points, you can use a Bonus Action during your Turn to choose a new hostile Creature to be your Target for the remainder of the duration.

{{imageMaskCorner29,--offsetX:-53%,--offsetY:-60%,--rotation:0
<img 
  src='https://arknights.wiki.gg/images/Meteor_Elite_2.png?f157c4' 
  style='position:absolute;top:400px;right:150px;width:800px' />
}}




\column

#### 4th Level: Versatile Approach
You know a litany of techniques and options for dealing with a variety of threats. This is represented by your Options, detailed below. When you meet the conditions outlined by each Option, you can perform that Option. You can only use one Option on each Attack. 
:
You can use your Options a number of times equal to your Proficiency Bonus. You recover all expended uses whenever you finish a Short or Long Rest.
:
- **Area Denial.** You can use this Option as a Bonus Action while you are wielding a Ranged Weapon; choose a point you can see within the long range of that Weapon. 
:
At the beginning of your next Turn, a rain of projectiles falls down from that point in a 10-foot-radius cylinder, where the height reaches up to the point you chose. All Creatures within this cylinder must make a Dexterity Saving Throw against your Martial DC, taking 3d6 Piercing Damage on a failed Save, or half as much on a successful Save. Flying Creatures have Disadvantage on this Saving Throw, and if they fail, their Flying Speed is halved until the end of their next Turn. Creatures with an Intelligence Score of 10 or more can see where this area will be.

- **Crowd Control.** You can use this Option when you make a Ranged Weapon Attack (no Action required); you repeat that Ranged Attack against a different Creature within 15 feet of the original Creature, but that attack deals half Damage. 

- **Stop their Approach.** You can use this Option when you hit a Creature with a Ranged Weapon Attack (no Action required); that Creature must succeed on a Wisdom Saving Throw against your Martial DC or have their Speed reduced by 20 feet until the end of their next Turn. This effect is cumulative.

- **Shoot to Kill.** You can use this Option when you hit a Creature with a Ranged Weapon Attack (no Action required); that Creature takes 1d4 Piercing Damage for each time you have dealt Damage to them since your last Turn, except Damage dealt by this Option. 


#### 10th Level: Unapproachable
When a hostile Creature enters the normal range of a Ranged Weapon you are wielding, or moves while within 30 feet of you, you may make a Ranged Attack against them with that Weapon as a Reaction. If the Creature is your Target, this does not expend your Reaction. However, once you have attacked a Creature in this way, you may not attack that Creature in this way again until the beginning of your next Turn.
:
After you make this Attack, you may move up to half of your Speed; this movement does not provoke Opportunity Attacks. 

\page
{{pageNumber,auto}}

\column

#### 15th Level: Always Another Option
The art of being a generalist is the art of having many tools on which to rely; the more effective, the better. You gain the following benefits: 
:
**Option Recharge.** If you roll Initiative and have less than your maximum uses of your Versatile Approach Feature remaining, you recover 1 use.
:
**Upgraded Options.** The Options of your Versatile Approach Feature are improved in the following ways:
- **Area Denial.** The cylinder created lingers for an additional round; Creatures that enter the area or end their Turn within it must make the Saving Throw as if they were within the area when it appeared. Additionally, when you use this Option, you may nominate any number of Creatures that can hear you to be immune to its effects.
- **Crowd Control.** You may repeat the Attack an additional time; however, it must be against a different Creature than the other two, and still deals half Damage.
- **Stop their Approach.** Creatures that succeed on this Saving Throw still have their Speed reduced by 10 feet.
- **Shoot to Kill.** The Damage of this Option is increased to 1d6 per instance of Damage you have dealt to that Creature since your last Turn.

#### 18th Level: The Last Option
Sometimes, when the chips are down, it is not sufficienct to carefully weigh ones options and act prudently. Sometimes, it is better to do anything rather than nothing. That sometimes is now. 
:
You may activate this Feature during your Turn (no Action required). For the next minute, you gain the following benefits:

- Once on each of your Turns, you may use one of your Options without expending a use of your Versatile Approach Feature. 
- When you take the Attack Action, you may make one additional Ranged Weapon Attack as part of that Action.


{{align-text:left
Once you have used this Feature, you may not do so again until you have finished a Long Rest.
}}

{{imageMaskEdge1,--offset:1%,--rotation:90
<img 
  src='https://arknights.wiki.gg/images/GreyThroat_Skin_1.png?f9a06a' 
  style='position:absolute;top:-100px;right:-150px;width:1500px' />
}}

{{imageMaskEdge1,--offset:29%,--rotation:0
<img 
  src='https://arknights.wiki.gg/images/GreyThroat_Skin_1.png?f9a06a' 
  style='position:absolute;top:-100px;right:-150px;width:1500px' />

### Artilleryman
Artillerymen are proof that if you're good enough at anything, you can make a career from it. They are experts in the art of destruction and demolition, with a penchant for explosives. In any conflict, they are in high demand; one just has to pray they are more threat to the enemy than to you.

#### 1st Level: Tools of the Trade
An Artilleryman needs their tools. Without them, they are ordinary people; with them, they are a force of nature. You gain the following benefits:

**Additional Weapon Proficiencies.** You gain Proficiency with the Grenade Launcher and Rocket Launcher. 

**Controlled Demolition.** The DC of your Traps and Splash (X) Property is increased against your Target by an equal amount to the bonus it grants you on Attack Rolls against that Creature.

**Explosive Expertise.** Whenever you deal Thunder Damage as a result of a Weapon with the Splash (X) Property, you deal an additional die of Damage. This is increased to two additional dice at 7th Level, and three at 14th Level.

#### 1st Level: Delayed Detonation
Explosives aren't all about the heat of the moment.
:
Once per Short Rest, when you would use a weapon with the Splash (X) Property during an Attack, you can instead choose to fire a specially fused munition at a grounded point you can see within the range of that weapon, where it burrows into the ground and becomes invisible. As a Reaction to a Creature moving, you can detonate that munition; resolve an Attack with the weapon you used on the point you chose, as per the Splash (X) Property. If you do not manually detonate it, it detonates automatically after 10 minutes unless you disarm it as an Action while within 5 feet.

#### 4th Level: Secondary Detonation
Whenever you deal Thunder Damage as a result of a Weapon with the Splash (X) Property, you can cause a Secondary Detonation (no Action required). Choose a point you can see that is within 15 feet of the centre of the original effect, and resolve that Attack or Trap again centred on that location. However, the Damage of this Secondary Detonation is halved, and if a Creature succeeds on the Saving Throw, they take no Damage. These Secondary Detonations cannot generate additional Secondary Detonations.
:
You may use this Feature a number of times equal to your Proficiency Bonus, and recover all expended uses upon finishing a Long Rest.


\column

#### 10th Level: Bigger Booms
Bigger really is better, sometimes. You gain the following benefits:

**Enhanced Payload.** When you take the Attack Action, you can double the value of X for any Weapon with the Splash (X) Property wielded by you for that Action. 

**Sharpened Shrapnel.** Your Martial DC is increased by 2 for the purpose of the Splash (X) Property

<img 
 src='https://arknights.wiki.gg/images/Catapult.png?6e7633' 
  style='position:absolute;top:230px;right:0px;width:420px' />

\page
{{pageNumber,auto}}

\column

#### 15th Level: Force of Nature
No fortification is proof against your cataclysmic explosions. You gain the following benefits:

**Scatter Them.** Once per Short Rest, when you deal Thunder Damage as a result of a Weapon with the Splash (X) Property you can cause all Creatures that take that Damage to be blown away (no Action required); Creatures are flung 30 feet away from the centre of the blast. If they collide with a solid surface as a result of this movement, they take 4d8 Bludgeoning Damage.

**Siege Attacker.** When you deal Thunder Damage to a Vehicle or Structure, that Damage is doubled. 

#### 18th Level: Explosive Dawn
You are the single most dangerous signal on the battlefield- bar none. You gain the following benefits:

**God of Destruction.** Grenade Launchers and Rocket Launchers are no longer considered to have the Loading Property when wielded by you. 

**Last Maniac Standing.** You are Resistant to Thunder Damage, and Immune to Thunder Damage that is dealt by you. 

{{imageMaskEdge1,--offset:2%,--rotation:90
<img 
 src='https://arknights.wiki.gg/images/Fiammetta_Skin_1.png?bab1a1' 
  style='position:absolute;top:50px;right:-200px;width:1500px;clip-path:rect(auto 60% auto auto);' />

### Besieger
Besieger Marksmen are slow and deliberate. They take their time getting to the perfect position, where they know there will be no interruptions. Then, they entrench themselves in that position; and may the divine help the foe in their sights then. No armour is proof against a Besieger, and the larger you are, the more of you there is to pierce.

#### 1st Level: Siege Mode
At the beginning of your Turn you may declare that you will enter Siege Mode (no Action required) until the end of that Turn. While in Siege Mode, your Speed is reduced to 0 (and cannot be increased), and you have Disadvantage on Ranged Weapon Attacks against Creatures within 20ft of you. However, you gain the following benefits:

**Additional Damage.** Your Attacks deal 1d8 additional Damage.

**Crushing Shots.** While they are Equipped by you, Ranged Weapons gain the Crush (X) Property, where X is equal to twice your Proficiency Bonus. If it already has the Crush (X) Property, add the values of X together and use that value for X.

**Ignore Brace.** You ignore the effects of the Brace Property.

**Long Range Shooting.** You suffer no penalty for Attacking at Long Range.

#### 1st Level: Large Target
Your preferred prey are the hulking brutes that love to throw their weight around. You gain the following benefits:

**Between the Scales.** If your Target is a Large or larger Creature, you have Advantage on Weapon Attacks against them. 

**Hide Piercer.** Your Weapon Attacks against Creatures that are Large or larger deal an additional die of Damage.


#### 4th Level: Deliberate, Efficient
If you slow your pace, you can place your shots to best pierce the defences of your prey. 
:
When you take the Attack Action while in Siege Mode, you can choose to only make one Attack as part of that Action. If you do, that Attack deals 1d8 additional Damage, and ignores Resistances and Damage Reduction to Physical Damage.

\column

#### 10th Level: Siege Bolt
A shot to pierce the heavens.
:
As an Action, choose a Creature or Object you can see within 100 feet of you; that Creature must make a Dexterity Saving Throw (objects automatically fail) against your Martial DC. On a Successful Save, they suffer 5d8 Piercing Damage immediately, and no further effects. On a failed Save, they take 5d8 Piercing Damage immediately, and at the end of each of their next two turns. They may reattempt the Saving Throw at the beginning of their Turn, ending the effect early on a Success. Damage dealt by this Feature ignores Resistances and Damage Reduction to Piercing Damage.
:
Once you have used this Feature, you may not do so again until you have finished a Long Rest.

#### 15th Level: Goliath's Bane
Just as David did, your shots can stop giants in their tracks. Once per Turn, when you hit a Creature with a Ranged Weapon Attack while in Siege Mode, you can force that Creature (no Action required) to make a Constitution Saving Throw against your Martial DC. On a failed Save, they are Stunned until the end of their next Turn.
:
You may use this Feature a number of times equal to your Proficiency Bonus, and regain all expended uses upon finishing a Long Rest.

#### 18th Level: Godpiercer
No armour, forged or natural, can ward your strikes.
:
Your Siege Mode and Deliberate, Efficient Features no longer grant additional Damage. Instead, when they would have provided additional Damage, they instead cause Creatures hit by your Attacks to lose 8 Hit Points.

{{imageMaskEdge1,--offset:25%,--rotation:-20
<img 
  src='https://arknights.wiki.gg/images/8/82/Typhon_Elite_2.png?d0ed28&20230926223545' 
  style='position:absolute;top:550px;right:-230px;width:1000px;;transform: rotate(25deg);' />
}}

### Deadeye
"One Shot, One Kill" is the mantra of Deadeye Marksmen. They concentrate their considerable talent into making the perfect single shot, rather than multiple less effective shots. Quantity is no substitute for quality, or so they say.

#### 1st Level: Deadshot
You may enter Deadshot State as a Bonus Action; Deadshot State ends at the end of your Turn, or after you make an Attack. While in Deadshot State, your Ranged Attacks gain the following benefits:

**Long Range Shooting.** You suffer no penalty for Attacking at Long Range.

**Make it Count.** When you roll to determine Damage dealt, treat any results of 1 or 2 as 3.

**Unerring Accuracy.** Your Proficiency Bonus is doubled for that Attack Roll.

#### 1st Level: Target Shooting
To you, the emphasis in "High Value Target" is the word "Target." You gain the following benefits:

**Lethal.** Your Attacks against your Target have +1 Critical Range. If that Creature is also an Elite or Leader, your Critical Range is increased by a further +1.

**Target of Opportunity.** As a Short Rest Action, you can nominate a Creature, either by name or by stating at least two distinguishing features of that Creature. If you do so, that Creature is considered to be your Target until you next complete a Short or Long Rest. Marking a Creature as your Target in this way does not prevent you from marking a different Creature as your Target. 

#### 4th Level: Vital Strike
As an Action, while you are in Deadshot State, you make a Ranged Attack using a weapon you are wielding. If it hits, it deals 2d6 additional Piercing Damage.
:
This additional Damage is increased to 3d6 at 7th Level, and 4d6 at 14th Level.


#### 10th Level: Anti-Sniper Training
Keep low, and keep your profile small. That's how you survive when the bolts and bullets start flying. You gain the following benefits:

**Head Down.** After you enter Deadshot State, you gain the benefit of Half Cover until the beginning of your next Turn. If you would gain the benefit of Half Cover from a different source, you instead gain the benefit of Three-Quarters Cover while you are benefitting from that additional source.
:::
&nbsp; **Small Profile.** You gain Proficiency in the Stealth Skill. If you already have Proficiency in this Skill, you have Advantage on Checks using this Skill so long as you have the benefit of at least Three-Quarters Cover.

\column

#### 15th Level: Improved Deadshot
You now additionally gain the following benefits while in Deadshot State:

**Deathblow.** If your Attack reduces a Creature to fewer Hit Points than your Marksman Level, they are immediately reduced to 0 Hit Points. For Elite and Leader Creatures, this threshold is twice your Marksman Level instead.

**Piercing Shots.** Creatures do not receive the benefit of Half or Three-Quarters Cover against your Attacks.

#### 18th Level: Ultimate Deadshot
You never miss a target. Your name will enter the history books of sharpshooters.
:
You now additionally gain the following benefits while in Deadshot State:

**Killchain.** If you reduce a Creature to 0 Hit Points with a Ranged Attack, your next usage of your Deadshot Feature may be performed without expending your Bonus Action.

**Nevermiss.** When you make a Ranged Attack Roll, if the result on the d20 is less than the Ability Modifier used for the Attack + your Proficiency Bonus (not doubled by Unerring Accuracy), it instead becomes that value.

<img 
  src='https://arknights.wiki.gg/images/7/70/Ambriel_Elite_2.png?a1ea4e&20230926230638' 
  style='position:absolute;top:325px;right:-410px;width:900px;;transform: scaleX(-1);' />

\page
{{pageNumber,auto}}

### Flinger
For many, Arts are an end rather than a means. Flinger Marksmen believe in the opposite; they are a tool to be utilized, not an end unto themselves. Using their honed Arts, they control the field; and strike like a missile.

{{note
##### Equipment and Fighting Style Exchange
When you choose this Branch, you may exchange 1x Ranged Weapon from your Starting Equipment for 1x Heavy Melee Weapon.
:
Additionally, When you choose this Branch, you may exchange the Fighting Style you gained from the Marksman Class for one available to the Guard Class.
}}




\column


#### 1st Level: Manipulation Arts
The Arts you use to manoeuvre your weapons can have other applications. You can cast Arts; the information below details how you use those rules as a Flinger.
:
***Arts Points.*** The Flinger Arts Casting table shows how many Arts Points you have to cast your Level 1+ Arts. You regain all expended Arts Points when you finish a Long Rest. When you cast an Art, if that Art only affects either you (such as Mage Armour, or Heroism cast on yourself) or your Target (in the case of Arts that affect an area, they are the only Creature in that area), that Art costs 1 fewer Art Point, to a minimum of 0.
:
***Maximum Arts Level.*** The Flinger Arts Casting table shows the maximum Level you can cast an Art at, based on your Marksman Level. If an Art’s Level is above your current Maximum Art Level, you cannot cast it, even if it is Prepared.
:
***Spellcasting Ability.*** When you gain this Feature, choose either Intelligence, Wisdom, or Charisma; that is your Spellcasting Ability.
:
***Flinger Arts Pools.*** When you gain this Feature, choose two of the Standard Arts Pools available to the Caster Class; you learn all of the levelled Arts of 4th Level or less from those Pools. These Arts are always Prepared.
