### Infuser
"Healing Hands, Evil Heart"- that is how some describe those who are Infuser Specialists. There is perhaps some truth to this. Their chemistry aids their allies, but is never without consequence; heaven forbid that you are their foe, lest you fall prey to one of their lethal toxins. 

{{note
##### Infuser DC
Some Infuser Features do not use your Martial DC, but instead an Infuser DC. Your Infuser DC is calculated as 20 - Your Intelligence Modifier - Your Proficiency Bonus.
}}

#### 1st Level: Principle of Double Effect
You are but a simple chemist, dispensing medication to those in need. If it happens to have some side effects, are you really to blame? You gain the following benefits:

**Additional Tool Proficiencies.** You are Proficient with the Pharmacology Kit. 

**Alchemical Infusions.** As a Bonus Action, you can apply one of your chemical Infusions to a willing Creature you can see within 5 feet of you that is not a Construct or Undead for 1 minute. Infusions available for you to apply are in the Infusions table below. A Creature can only have one Infusion applied to them at a time; applying a second Infusion removes the effect of any other Infusions on that Creature. You may use this Feature a number of times equal to twice your Proficiency Bonus, and recover all expended uses when you complete a Long Rest.
:
To gain a benefit from an Infusion, that Creature must Leverage it. Each Infusion describes the scenarios under which it can be Leveraged. When a Creature chooses to Leverage an Infusion that has been applied to it by you, it must immediately make a Constitution Saving Throw against your Infuser DC. On a successful Save, they gain the benefit of that Infusion; on a failed Save, they gain the benefit of that Infusion, but they are additionally Dazed until the beginning of their next Turn, and cannot Leverage any Infusions while they have this Condition. An Infusion is not consumed when it is Leveraged.



\column

#### 5th Level: New Concoctions
Not everyone can breach the boundaries you can. When you use the Toxicology Proficient Use of the Pharmacology Kit, you can create two additional types of Toxin, detailed below.

{{note
##### Additional Toxins
The following are the additional Toxins you can Create with a Pharmacology Kit. Both have a Weight of 1 lb. and a Cost of 140 LMD, but cannot be purchased.

#### Neurological Inhibitor
As an Action, you can apply this to a Weapon you are carrying, or poison food/drink within 5ft of you. Each Weapon or other object can only contain one Toxin at a time; applying a new Toxin overwrites the effect of the old toxin.
:
When applied to a Weapon, the effect lasts for ten minutes.The first time each Turn you hit a Creature with that Weapon, that Creature must pass a DC15 Constitution Saving Throw or be driven Berserk until the end of their next Turn. While Berserk, they regard all other Creatures as actively hostile, and must attack the Creature closest to themselves.
:
If a Creature consumes food/drink that has been poisoned using this, they must make a DC15 Constitution Saving Throw. On a failed save, they are driven Berserk for 1 hour, and regard all other Creatures as actively hostile. On a successful Save, they are instead driven Berserk for 1 minute.

#### Bungarotoxin
As an Action, you can apply this to a Weapon you are carrying, or poison food/drink within 5ft of you. Each Weapon or other object can only contain one Toxin at a time; applying a new Toxin overwrites the effect of the old toxin.
:
When applied to a Weapon, the effect lasts for ten minutes or until after you have hit a Creature with the affected weapon. When you hit a Creature with that Weapon, that Creature must make a DC15 Constitution Saving Throw. On a failed Save, they take 2d10 Poison Damage and are Paralyzed. On a succesful Save, they take 2d10 Poison Damage and are not Paralyzed. A Creature Paralyzed in this way can reattempt this Saving Throw at the end of each of their Turns, ending the effect on a success.
:
If a Creature consumes food/drink that has been poisoned using this, they must make a DC15 Constitution Saving Throw. On a failed Save, they are Paralyzed and Unconscious for 24 hours. On a successful Save, they are instead Paralyzed for 1 hour.

}}

\page
{{pageNumber,auto}}


#### 5th Level: Double-Blind Trial
As a Bonus Action while you are wielding a Weapon that does not have a Toxin applied to it, and have access to a Pharmacology Kit, you can expend 1 Prowess and 1 use of your Alchemical Infusions Feature to roll 1d6 and apply the corresponding Toxin to that Weapon; this does not require you to physically possess the Toxin.

| Result | Toxin |
|:--------------:|:-----:|
| 1 | Neurologicial Inhibitor
| 2 | Necrotoxin
| 3 | Haemotoxin
| 4 | Cytotoxin
| 5 | Neurotoxin
| 6 | Bungarotoxin

:
**Gaining Prowess as an Infuser.** As an Infuser, you gain 1 Prowess when a Creature that either has the Poisoned Condition, or is affected by a Toxin created by you, is reduced to 0 Hit Points. Additionally, you gain 1 Prowess when the effect of one of your Infusions causes a Creature to succeed on an Attack Roll, Skill Check, or Saving Throw it would otherwise have failed; you can only gain Prowess in this way once per Round. 

#### 11th Level: Stimpack
You gain the following benefits:

**Delivery Service.** The range at which you can apply Infusions is increased to 30 feet.

**Questionable Rejuvenation.** When you apply an Infusion to a Creature, and when a Creature succeeds on a Saving Throw against one of your Infusions, that Creature gains 1d10 Temporary Hit Points; if that Infusion is Procoagulant, these Temporary Hit Points are instead added to the Temporary Hit Points granted by that Infusion.



\column

#### 15th Level: Excessive Dosing
Your toxins reach new heights of lethality, much to your joy. You gain the following benefits:

**Succumb.** The DC of your Toxins is increased by your Proficiency Bonus.

**Toxic Shock.** Creatures that fail a Saving Throw against your Toxins are now additionally Poisoned for the same duration as that Toxin's Conditions. If the Toxin does not apply a Condition upon failing the Saving Throw (such as Haemotoxin), they are instead Poisoned until the end of their next Turn. 

#### 20th Level: To Defy Science
Your Alchemical Concoctions have developed so far as to appear as magic.
:
As an Action, you can choose a Creature you can see within 30 feet; that Creature cannot lose Hit Points for the next minute, as if they were under the effect of Invulnerability.
:
Once you have used this Feature, you may not do so again until you have completed a Long Rest.

<img 
  src='https://arknights.wiki.gg/images/Folinic.png?af9cb4' 
  style='position:absolute;top:450px;right:20px;width:500px;clip-path:rect(auto auto 88% auto);' />

\page
{{pageNumber,auto}}

#### Alchemical Infusions
These options are presented in alphabetical order.

##### Adrenaline Trigger
**Leverage Condition:** As a Reaction when the Creature fails a Strength Skill Check, Saving Throw, or misses a Melee Attack.
:
The Creature rolls 1d6 and adds the result to their total result, possibly changing a failure into a Success.

##### Flight Reflex Stimulant
**Leverage Condition:** As a Reaction when the Creature fails any Saving Throw 
:
The Creature adds your Intelligence Bonus to the result of that Saving Throw, possibly changing a failure into a Success.

##### Focus Heightener
**Leverage Condition:** As a Reaction when the Creature fails a Dexterity Skill Check, Saving Throw, or misses a Ranged Attack.
:
The Creature rolls 1d6 and adds the result to their total result, possibly changing a failure into a Success.

##### Hesitation Suppressor 
**Leverage Condition:** As a Reaction when the Creature forces a hostile Creature to make a Saving Throw.
:
The Creature rolls 1d4; the DC of that Saving Throw is increased by the amount rolled.

##### Muscle Strengthener
**Leverage Condition:** As a Reaction when the Creature hits a Creature with a Melee Attack. 
:
The Damage of that Melee Attack is increased by a value equal to your Intelligence Bonus.

##### Procoagulant
**Leverage Condition:** As a Bonus Action
:
The Creature rolls one of their Hit Dice (this does not expend that Hit Dice) and adds their Constitution Modifier; they gain that many Temporary Hit Points. Additionally, if they have the Bleeding Condition, it is removed. 

##### Respiration Stimulant
**Leverage Condition:** As a Bonus Action
:
The Creature's Speed is increased by 10 feet until the end of the Turn. 

\column

##### Sensation Nulling 
**Leverage Condition:** As a Reaction when the Creature takes Damage
:
The Creature rolls 1d6; they gain Damage Reduction against all Damage equal to the result until the end of that Turn.

{{wide

### Inventor
A tinge of insanity combined with the spark of genius leads a person to become an Inventor Specialist. They can create incredible things from seeming scraps; with just a *small* chance to blow up in your face.

{{note
##### Inventor DC
Some Inventor Features do not use your Martial DC, but instead an Inventor DC. Your Inventor DC is calculated as 8 + Your Intelligence Modifier + Your Proficiency Bonus.
}}

#### 1st Level: Equipment Modification
You can modify non-magical weapons and armour with special Modifications. 

**Inventions.** You have a number of Inventions equal to twice your Proficiency Bonus that you expend to modify equipment. Each Modification you apply consumes 1 Invention; this Invention is refunded when that Modification is removed.

**Modifying Equipment.** Over the course of 10 minutes, you can modify or remove Modifications from a non-magical Weapon or piece of Armour that you can work on for the duration. If you do so, you may apply any number of valid Modifications, listed below, to that equipment. A piece of equipment cannot have duplicate Modifications. If you cannot maintain a modification for whatever reason, such as the equipment being lost or stolen, the Modification is automatically removed after a week.

**Activating Modifications.** Each Modification describes the Action cost to active that Modification. 

**Instability.** Any piece of equipment with Modifications on it has an Instability Value; this begins at 3, and increases by 1 for each Modification after the first placed on it. When a Creature activates a Modification, they roll a number of d6's equal to that equipment's Instability; if any of those dice show the same result as another die, that equipment suffers a breakdown; the wielder or wearer loses 1d8 Hit Points, plus an additional 1d8 for every Instability above 3 that equipment has. This loss of Hit Points cannot reduce you below 1 Hit Point.
 

}}

{{imageMaskEdge1,--offset:-4%,--rotation:0
<img 
  src='https://arknights.wiki.gg/images/4/42/Exusiai_the_New_Covenant_Elite_2.png?c8c1b2&20250502140259' 
  style='position:absolute;top:280px;right:-50px;width:900px;clip-path:rect(20% auto auto auto);' />
}}




\page
{{pageNumber,auto}}

#### 5th Level: Strange Contraption
As an Action, you choose a point you can see within 30 feet and throw a Strange Contraption to that location. You choose the effect of each Strange Contraption from the Strange Contraption table below; once you have chosen an option from the table, you cannot choose that option again until you have completed a Long Rest. 

<img 
  src='https://arknights.wiki.gg/images/0/0a/Closure_Act_2.png?7c5fe9&20230927010702' 
  style='position:absolute;top:200px;right:200px;width:800px' />

\column

##### Strange Contraptions
| Strange Contraption | Effect                              |
|:------------------- |:-----------------------------------:|
| 12 Million Lumen Flashlight | All Creatures within 15 feet of the chosen point must succeed on a Dexterity Saving Throw against your Inventor DC or be Blinded until the end of their next Turn. |
| Fun Toy             | A Tiny Construct Mobile Camera is created at the location for 10 minutes. It has 18 AC, 10 Hit Points, a walking speed of 20ft, and 60ft of Darkvision. The Mobile Camera does not move, and takes no Actions or Reactions unless you use your Action to cause it to move while you are within 100 feet of it. You can use your Bonus Action to see and hear what the Mobile Camera does until the beginning of your next Turn. |
| Musbeast Remover    | A 10-foot radius Sphere appears centred on the chosen location for 2 Rounds. When the area appears, all Creatures within it that are not protected by Full Cover must make a Dexterity Saving Throw against your Inventor DC. On a failed Save, they take 4d8 Piercing Damage. On a successful Save, they take half as much Damage. Creatures must also make this Saving Throw if they enter the area during their Turn.
| Needlefly Fumigator | A 20-foot radius Sphere appears centred on the chosen location for 1 minute; this area spreads around corners. When a Creature enters the area during their Turn, or begins their Turn there, they must make a Constitution Saving Throw against your Inventor DC. On a failed Save, they take 2d8 Poison Damage and gain the Poisoned Condition until the beginning of their next Turn. On a successful Save, they take half as much Damage, and suffer no other effects.
| Over-tuned Speaker   | The Contraption begins making a sound of your choice, loud enough to be heard from 100 feet away, for up to 1 minute.
| Portable Firestarter | All Creatures within 10 feet of the chosen location must make a Dexterity Saving Throw against your Inventor DC. On a failed Save, they take 2d8 Fire Damage and begin Burning. On a successful Save, they take half as much Damage and do not burn. Objects within the area that are not being worn or carried also begin Burning. 
| Universal Keycard   | Choose an object within 5 feet of the chosen location that is mostly electronic and is locked or restricted in some way, such as a computer with a password or an electronic coded lock; that lock or restriction is removed, but leaves a trace that you did so. The DM has final discretion on what objects this functions on. 
| Unstable Contraption | All Creatures within 10 feet of the chosen location must make a Dexterity Saving Throw against your Inventor DC. On a failed Save, they take 5d8 Thunder Damage; on a successful Save, they take half as much Damage.

\page
{{pageNumber,auto}}

#### 5th Level: Mobile Modding
You can expend 1 Prowess as an Action to choose a willing Creature within 5 feet of you; you may immediately apply one Modification of your choice to a piece of equipment they are wielding or wearing. This Modification does not cost an Invention, and expires after a minute. 
:
**Gaining Prowess as an Inventor.** As an Inventor, you gain 1 Prowess whenever a Creature who has activated one of your Modification this Turn reduces a Creature to 0 Hit Points. You can only gain Prowess in this way once per Turn.

#### 11th Level: Risk Assessment
Your creations are marginally more safe, at least when operated by someone with competency. You gain the following benefits: 

**Speciality Modding.** The Instability of Weapons you have modified that match your Weapon Specialisation Feature choice is reduced by 1. 

**Trained Operator.** The Instability of equipment you have modified that you are wielding or wearing is reduced by 1. 

#### 15th Level: Stranger Contraptions
The effect of your Strange Contraptions are improved.
:
##### Stranger Contraptions
| Stranger Contraption | Effect                              |
|:------------------- |:-----------------------------------:|
| 12 Million Lumen Flashlight | Creatures that succeed on the Saving Throw are Dazed until the end of their next Turn. |
| Fun Toy             | The Mobile Camera has the Invisible Condition. |
| Musbeast Remover    | Creatures that fail the Saving Throw suffer the Bleeding Condition.
| Needlefly Fumigator | When you use this Strange Contraption, you may choose any number of Creatures to be immune to its effects until the end of their next Turn.
| Over-tuned Speaker   | You can now control the volume of the sound produced, and may also pick a particular direction to direct the sound.
| Portable Firestarter | When a Creature or Object takes Damage from the Burning Condition that was gained through this Strange Contraption, the Fire Damage taken is increased by your Intelligence Bonus.
| Universal Keycard   | No longer leaves a trace.
| Unstable Contraption | Damage increased to 8d8.

\column

#### 20th Level: The Big One
At this point, no-one truly knows what you can pull from your arsenal, but everyone knows the cleanup will not be pretty. 
:
As an Action, choose from one of the following options:

**FLASH** Choose up to four locations within 100 feet; all Creatures within 10 feet of one of those points must make a Dexterity Saving Throw against your Inventor DC, taking 8d6 Thunder Damage and suffering the Burning Condition on a failed Save, or half as much on a successful save. If a Creature would be within multiple affected areas, they only make the Saving Throw once. 

**SHARD** You release a blast of shrapnel in a 50 foot cone originating from you. All Creatures within the area must make a Dexterity Saving Throw against your Inventor DC, taking 10d6 Slashing Damage and suffering the Bleeding Condition on a failed Save. On a successful Save, they take half as much Damage, and suffer no other effects. Creatures do not gain the benefit of cover against this effect.

**QUASAR** You project a line 100 feet long and 10 feet wide in a direction of your choice; all Creatures within that line must make a Dexterity Saving Throw against your Inventor DC, taking 14d6 Radiant Damage on a failed Save, or half as much on a successful Save.
:
Once you have used this Feature, you may not do so again until you have completed a Long Rest.

\page
{{pageNumber,auto}}

#### <u>Inventor Modifications</u>
These Modifications are presented by category, and then by alphabetical order.

{{note
##### Dangerous Modifications
Some Modifications are noted as being Dangerous in their name; Dangerous Modifications increase Instability by 1 more than usual. In addition, when equipment suffers a breakdown as a result of activating a Dangerous Modification, that equipment is damaged in addition to the loss of Hit Points. If it is a weapon, it cannot be used; if it is armour, it provides no benefits. It returns to functionality when it is repaired, such as by using a Maintenance Kit.
}}

#### Melee Weapon Modifications
:
##### Boom Module
**Item:** A weapon that deals Bludgeoning Damage
:
An explosive payload is built into the weapon, triggered by the striking head. Once per Turn when the wielder hits a Creature with an Attack using this Weapon, they may activate this Modification (no Action required); that Attack deals one additional die of Thunder Damage. In addition, that Creature must succeed on a Strength Saving Throw against your Inventor DC or be knocked back 20 feet and knocked Prone. 

##### Burning Edge
**Item:** Melee Weapon
:
Flames coruscate along the edge of the blade on command. Once per Turn when the wielder takes the Attack Action, they may activate this Modification. Until the end of the Turn, the Damage of Attacks made using that Weapon are converted to Fire Damage. 

##### Collapsible Weapon
**Item:** Melee Weapon
:
This weapon has been modified so as to be able to compress down into a state easy for concealment. The wielder may activate this Modification as an Action; the weapon folds down into a form roughly half of its previous size. Additionally, it folds into an appropriate disguise for its new size; a polearm may become a cane, for example. If the weapon is used to Attack in this form, it is treated as being an improvised melee weapon; the wielder may return the weapon to its previous form as a Bonus Action.

\column

##### Flexible Weapon
**Item:** Melee Weapon
:
Some part of this weapon has been made possible to bend and extend like a whip. Once per Turn when the wielder makes a Melee Attack using this Weapon, they may activate this Modification (no Action required); That Attack ignores the benefit of any shield wielded by the target Creature. In addition, the wielder may then immediately use their Bonus Action to repeat that Attack against a different Creature within 10 feet of the original target, keeping the benefit of this Modification being active.

##### Laser Cutter (Dangerous)
**Item:** Melee Weapon
:
This weapon possesses a specialised Casting Unit that converts Arts from the wielder into a slicing blade of light. When the wielder takes the Attack Action, they may forgo one Attack to activate this Modification. If they do, all Creatures in a 15-foot Cone originating from the wielder must make a Dexterity Saving Throw against your Inventor DC. On a failed Save, they take 3d8 Radiant Damage. On a successful Save, they take half as much Damage.
:
At 7th Level, the Damage of this Modification is increased to 4d8. At 14th Level, it is increased to 5d8.

##### Returner
**Item:** Melee Weapon
:
This weapon is attached to its wielder by string or wire. When the wielder takes the Attack Action, they may activate this Modification (no Action required); until the end of the Turn, that Weapon has the Returning Property. Alternatively, if they are disarmed of or drop the Weapon, they can use a Bonus Action to return it to their hand as long as they are within a distance equal to its normal range.

##### Rotary Blade
**Item:** Melee Weapon that deals Slashing or Piercing Damage
:
A terrifying rotary component is attached to the weapon, tearing through flesh with sickening ease. Once per Turn when the wielder hits a Creature with a Melee Attack using this Weapon, they may activate this Modification (no Action required); that Attack deals one additional die of Slashing Damage. The hit Creature must also succeed on a Dexterity Saving Throw against your Inventor DC or suffer the Bleeding Condition.


\page
{{pageNumber,auto}}

##### Shock Engine
**Item:** Melee Weapon
:
Once per Turn when the wielder hits a Creature with a Melee Attack using this Weapon, they may activate this Modification (no Action required); the Damage of that Attack is converted to Lightning Damage. That Creature must also succeed on a Dexterity Saving Throw against your Inventor DC or suffer the Dazed Condition until the end of their next Turn.

##### Telescopic Weapon 
**Item:** Melee Weapon
:
The haft or handle of this weapon has been made extendable, allowing for a much greater reach. Once per Turn when the wielder takes the Attack Action, they may active this Modification (no Action required). Until the end of the Turn, that Weapon gains the Reach Property. If it already has that Property, its reach is instead increased to 15 feet. 

##### Trick Weapon 
**Item:** Melee Weapon
:
This weapon can be quickly and temporarily transformed into another weapon as a Bonus Action. When this Modification is applied, choose another Light Melee or Heavy Melee Weapon.  As a Bonus Action during the wielder's Turn, they may cause this Weapon to transform into the chosen Weapon for a minute; a Creature Proficient with the original Weapon is considered Proficient with the transformed Weapon. 

\column

#### Ranged Weapon Modifications
:
##### Arts Cannon (Dangerous)
**Item:** Ranged Weapon with the Two-Handed Property
:
This weapon has been fitted with an experimental grenade launcher/casting unit hybrid allowing it to pelt an unstable burst of Originium Arts in a chosen direction. The wielder may activate this Modification as an Action; choose a point they can see within 60 feet. All Creatures within a 10-foot radius sphere centred on that point must make a Dexterity Saving Throw against your Inventor DC; on a failed Save, they take 3d8 Force Damage. On a successful Save, they take half as much Damage.
:
At 7th Level, the Damage of this Modification is increased to 4d8. At 14th Level, it is increased to 5d8.

##### Collapsible Weapon
**Item:** Ranged Weapon
:
This weapon has been modified so as to be able to compress down into a state easy for concealment. The wielder may activate this Modification as an Action; the weapon folds down into a form roughly half of its previous size. Additionally, it folds into an appropriate disguise for its new size; a rifle may become a briefcase, for example. If the weapon is used to Attack in this form, it is treated as being an improvised melee weapon; the wielder may return the weapon to its previous form as a Bonus Action.

##### Explosive Shots
**Item:** Light or Heavy Ranged Weapon
:
This weapon has been modified to fire special explosive ammunition. When the wielder makes an Attack using this weapon, they can activate this Modification (no Action required); after the Attack, roll the Damage of that Attack even if the Attack misses; all other Creatures within 10 feet of the target Creature must succeed on a Dexterity Saving Throw against your Inventor DC, or take Thunder Damage equal to half of the amount rolled.



<img 
  src='https://arknights.wiki.gg/images/3/34/Spuria.png?20230927024609' 
  style='position:absolute;top:600px;right:400px;width:400px' />

\page
{{pageNumber,auto}}

##### Electrobolts
**Item:** A Ranged Weapon that does not have the Splash (X) or Firearm Properties
:
This weapon can transfer shocking electricity through its bolts. When the wielder hits a Creature with a Ranged Attack using this Weapon, they may activate this Modification as a Bonus Action. The Damage of that Ranged Attack is converted to Lightning Damage, and the target Creature must suceed on a Constitution Saving Throw against your Inventor DC; on a failed Save, that Creature gains the Nervous Impairment Condition until the beginning of your next Turn.

##### Flame Gout
**Item:** Ranged Weapon with the Two-Handed Property
:
This weapon is fitted with a flame-projector. The wielder may activate this Modification as an Action to project a 15-foot cone of fire in a direction of their choice. All Creatures within that area must make a Dexterity Saving Throw against your Inventor DC; on a failed Save, they take 1d8 Fire Damage and suffer the Burning Condition. On a successful Save, they take half as much Damage, and suffer no other effects.
:
At 7th Level, the Damage of this Modification is increased to 2d8. At 14th Level, it is increased to 3d8.

##### Homing Shots
**Item:** Light or Heavy Ranged Weapon
:
This weapon is outfitted with special ammunition that tracks its target. When the wielder makes a Ranged Attack using this Weapon and miss, they may activate this Modification as a Reaction to immediately reroll that Attack; they must take the new result, and they cannot reroll an Attack more than once. 

##### Melee Attachment
**Item:** Ranged Weapon
:
This weapon has an attachment designed for melee. The wielder may activate this Modification as a Bonus Action during their Turn, or as part of an Attack of Opportunity. After activation, they may make Melee Attacks using that Weapon until the end of that Turn. They may use their Dexterity Modifier instead of Strength for the Attack and Damage Rolls of this Weapon, and those Melee Attacks deal 1d8 + their Attack Modifier in Piercing Damage.



\column

##### Safety Removal
**Item:** A Ranged Weapon with the Firearm Property
:
The safety protocols for this weapon have been "adjusted". When the wielder takes the Attack Action while wielding this weapon, they may activate this Modification (no Action required). They may make one additional Attack with this weapon as part of that Attack Action, but all Attacks they make as part of that Action are made at Disadvantage. 

#### Armour Modifications
:
##### Adrenal Overloader (Dangerous)
**Item:** Any Armour
:
This armour is fitted with an onboard intravenous injector of stimulants designed to push a person onwards when it is most necessary. The wearer can activate this Modification during their Turn (no Action required); that Creature can take an additional Action that Turn. A Creature can only activate this Modification once per Short Rest, and only once per Turn.

##### Artsfield Generator
**Item:** Medium or Heavy Armour
:
This armour is fitted with a special Arts Unit that allows the wearer to create a protective barrier. When a Creature within 5 feet of you is hit by an Attack, you may activate this Modification as a Reaction; that Creature gains Resistance against one of the Damage Types present on that Attack (your choice if there are multiple).

##### Camouflage Cloak
**Item:** Light Armour or Clothing
:
This armour is fitted with a specially created cloak that bends the light around it, rendering the wearer particularly difficult to see. The wearer may activate this Modification as an Action; for a minute, they have Advantage on Dexterity (Stealth) Checks, and Wisdom (Perception) Checks to see them are made at Disadvantage. 

##### Exoskeleton
**Item:** Any Armour
:
This armour is fitted with a mechanical exoskeleton, reinforcing the wearer's strength when necessary. The wearer may activate this Modification as a Bonus Action; until the beginning of their next Turn, they are treated as being one size larger for determining what they can push, pull, or carry, their jump height and length is doubled, they have Advantage on any Strength (Athletics) Check, and they are not slowed by carrying another Creature of the same size or smaller.





\page
{{pageNumber,auto}}

{{wide

##### Jetboots
**Item:** Any Armour
:
This Armour is fitted with small propulsions devices that allow for enhanced movement speed at the cost of some manoeuvrability. The wearer may activate this Modification as a Bonus Action; as part of that Bonus Action, they may immediately take the Dash Action, but can only move in a single straight line as part of that Dash Action.

##### Life Support
**Item:** Any Armour
:
This armour contains a system that constantly monitors the vitals of its wearer, and attempts to keep them alive. When the wearer of this armour is below half of their maximum Hit Points, they may activate this Modification as a Bonus Action to immediately expend and roll one of their Hit Dice, recovering Hit Points equal to the amount rolled + their Constitution Modifier. 

##### Powered Gauntlet
**Item:** Any Armour
:
The gauntlets of this armour are fitted with power packs that allow them to deal a dangerous shock. When the wearer hits with an Unarmed Strike, they may activate this Modification (no Action required); that Attack deals an additional die of Lightning Damage.

##### Refocuser
**Item:** Any Armour or Clothing
:
This armour contains a device designed to monitor the wearer's brain activity and deliver a safe but steadying jolt when their consciousness flickers. When the wearer of this armour fails a Constitution Saving Throw to maintain Concentration on an Art, they may activate this Modification as a Reaction to reroll that Saving Throw with Advantage.

##### Shock Absorbers
**Item:** Any Armour
:
This armour is fitted with devices that allow the wearer to better regulate incoming kinetic impacts. When the wearer makes a Strength or Dexterity Saving Throw, they can activate this Modification as a Reaction to add your Intelligence Modifier to the result of the roll. 

##### Wingsuit
**Item:** Light Armour or Clothing
:
This armour is fitted with lightweight webbing that allows for temporary gliding. When the wearer of this armour would fall or is falling a distance great enough for them to take falling Damage, they can activate this Modification as a Reaction; they gain a Flying Speed of 30 feet until they touch the floor, but they cannot ascend using this Flying Speed, and must descend at least 10 feet each Turn. 

}}

\page
{{pageNumber,auto}}

### Skyranger
The very idea of a Skyranger specialist will draw strange looks from many people on Terra. The technology required for aircraft is not commonplace; the technology required for personal flight seems the realm of fiction. To a Skyranger however, the difference between a person and a drone is not that great if you refuse to care for your own safety.

#### 1st Level: Take Flight
The field of avionics in Terra is stumbling and childish; like Icarus, you have burst beyond the bounds of safety. Only time will tell if you follow his example.

**Flight Suit.** When you choose this Branch, a Flight Suit is added to your starting equipment; replacing a Flight Suit costs 650 LMD and 24 hours of crafting time. 
:
A Flight Suit is Light Armour with an AC of 12 + your Dexterity Modifier, 0 DR, and a weight of 25lb.
:
While any Creature with Light Armour Proficiency can wear a Flight Suit, only Creatures with the Take Flight Feature can use it to fly.

**Fuel.** You have a special resource called Fuel that you use to power your flight and some of your other Features; the maximum amount of Fuel you have increases with your Specialist Level, as shown in the Skyranger Features Table. You regain all expended Fuel when you finish a Long Rest.

**Take Off.** As a Bonus Action while you are wearing your Flight Suit, you can expend 1 Fuel to grant yourself a Flying Speed equal to your Walking Speed for 1 minute. 

##### Skyranger Features
| Level | Maximum Fuel | Strafe Damage |
|:-----:|:------------:|:-------------:|
| 1     | 2            | -
| 2     | 2            | -
| 3     | 3            | -
| 4     | 3            | -
| 5     | 3            | 5d8
| 6     | 4            | 5d8
| 7     | 4            | 5d8
| 8     | 4            | 6d8
| 9     | 5            | 6d8
| 10    | 5            | 6d8
| 11    | 5            | 7d8
| 12    | 6            | 7d8
| 13    | 6            | 7d8
| 14    | 6            | 8d8
| 15    | 7            | 8d8
| 16    | 7            | 8d8
| 17    | 7            | 9d8
| 18    | 8            | 9d8
| 19    | 8            | 9d8
| 20    | 8            | 10d8


\column

#### 5th Level: Air Doctrine
When you complete a Short or Long Rest, you choose from one of the following Doctrines; Air Superiority, Bombardment, or Close Air Support. Some of your Branch Features functions corresponds to your current Doctrine, described in those Features. 
:
At this Level, your choice of Doctrine grants you the following benefits:

**Air Superiority (AA Missile).** As an Action while you are wearing your Flight Suit, you can expend 1 Fuel to choose a Creature you can see within 100 feet. That Creature must make a Dexterity Saving Throw against your Martial DC, taking Thunder Damage equal to your Strafe Damage on a failed Save, or half as much on a successful Save. If that Creature is not touching the ground, you do not roll, and instead use the highest number possible for each die.

**Bombardment (Bombing Run).** As an Action while you are wearing your Flight Suit, you can expend 1 Fuel to choose a grounded point you can see within 30 feet and launch a bomb at it; if you are currently Flying, this distance is extended to 120 feet. All Creatures within 15 feet of the chosen point must make a Dexterity Saving Throw against your Martial DC, taking Thunder Damage equal to your Strafe Damage on a failed Save, or half as much on a successful Save. 

**Close Air Support (Swooping Dive).** When you take the Attack Action while you are wearing your Flight Suit, you can expend 1 Fuel to move up to your Speed as part of that Action. Additionally, when you make an Attack against a Creature within 10 feet of you as part of that Action, that Attack is made at Advantage, and deals additional Thunder Damage on hit equal to your Strafe Damage; a Creature can only take this additional Damage once per Turn.

#### 5th Level: Jink
Your prowess as a Skyranger grows, and you can express it in new ways. You gain the following benefits:

**Evasive Manoeuvres.** As a Reaction when you would be hit by an Attack while wearing your Flight Suit, you can expend 1 Prowess to increase your AC by an amount equal to your Proficiency Bonus until the beginning of your next Turn, potentially turning that hit into a miss. Additionally, if the triggering Attack still hits you, you only take half Damage.

**Gaining Prowess as a Skyranger.** You can now gain Prowess in the following ways, once per Turn:
- While you are Flying, a Creature on the ground that you have dealt Damage since the beginning of your last Turn is reduced to 0 Hit Points. 
- A Creature that is not touching the ground that you have dealt Damage since the beginning of your last Turn is reduced to 0 Hit Points. 

\page
{{pageNumber,auto}}

#### 11th Level: Advanced Doctrine
You gain additional benefits based on your current Doctrine:

**Air Superiority.** When you reduce a Creature that is not touching the ground to 0 Hit Points, you can choose a grounded point you can see; this point can be no further away than twice the distance between that Creature and the ground directly below them. The Creature then crashes there, and all Creatures within 10 feet of the chosen point must succeed on a Dexterity Saving Throw against your Martial DC or take Thunder Damage equal to half of your Strafe Damage. 

**Bombardment.** When you use your Bombing Run Feature, you can remove 3d8 Damage from your Strafe Damage from that Bombing Run (before rolling), to modify that Bombing Run in one of the following ways:
- **Firebomb.** The Damage is converted to Fire Damage, and Creatures that fail the Saving Throw suffer the Burning Condition.
- **Gas Strike.** The Damage is converted to Poison Damage, and Creatures that fail the Saving Throw are then Poisoned until the end of their next Turn. Additionally, that area is considered to be Difficult Terrain for any Creatures that breathe until the end of your next Turn.

**Close Air Support.** Creatures you hit with an Attack as part of your Swooping Dive have Disadvantage on Attacks against you until the beginning of your next Turn. Additionally, when you deal your Strafe Damage to a Creature, that Creature must succeed on a Wisdom Saving Throw against your Martial DC or become Frightened of you until the end of your next Turn. 

#### 15th Level: Afterburners
When you use your Evasive Manoeuvrers Feature, you can move up to your Speed as part of that Reaction. Additionally, once per Turn while you have increased Armour Class as a result of your Evasive Manoeuvrers Feature, when you move within 10 feet of a Creature you can force that Creature to succeed on a Dexterity Saving Throw against your Martial DC or take Fire Damage equal to your Strafe Damage.

\column

#### 20th Level: Skyscreamer
The difference between you and Icarus is that Icarus did not believe hard enough. 
:
Once per Long Rest, while you are wearing your Flight Suit, you can transform it into its ultimate Combat Mode for 1 minute. While in Combat Mode, you gain the following benefits:
:
**Multirole:** You are considered to be in all three Doctrines at once. Additionally, you may use any of the options of your Air Doctrine Feature without expending Fuel. 
:
**Special Weapons.** You gain access to the following Special Weapons for the duration; they do not need to be equipped for you to use them. 
:
**Name:** Core Cannon<br>
**Weapon Category:** Light Ranged<br>
**Damage:** 3d8 Force<br>
**Properties:** Range (100/300), Rapid Fire, Light<br>
**Weight:** -<br>
**Cost:** -<br>
:
**Name:** Core Saber<br>
**Weapon Category:** Light Melee<br>
**Damage:** 3d8 Force <br>
**Properties:** Deadly, Finesse, Reach, Light<br>
**Weight:** -<br>
**Cost:** -<br>

### Dollkeeper
Dollkeepers are an eccentric bunch, even amongst the already eccentric Specialists. Whether their Doll is a complex mechanical construct, an inanimate structure animated by Arts, or something even more poorly understood, they have a reputation for being somewhat strange, even disconcerting. Where a Dollkeeper is required however, almost no-one can fill their place. 

#### 1st Level: Living Doll
You have created a Doll, a construct that is friendly to you and your companions. Visually, the Doll appears to be you but cannot speak; a Creature must succeed on a DC18 Perception Check to see that it is fake, unless there is an obvious clue that something is amiss. In addition, you can see and hear whatever your Doll can see and hear. The Doll is capable of wearing clothes, armour, and wielding weapons; it is Proficient with all Weapons and Armour that you are. 
:
**The Doll in Combat.** In Combat, the Doll acts during your Turn, and can use Reactions on its own. However, it will not move or take Actions unless you use your Bonus Action to command it to move; as part of that Bonus Action, you can also command it to take an Action in its Stat Block. To command the Doll in this way, you must be within 100 feet of it. 
:
**Restoring or Replacing the Doll.** As a Construct, the Doll cannot regain Hit Points in most ways (See Page 9). Whenever you roll Hit Dice to restore missing Hit Points, your Doll also recovers Hit Points equal to the amount rolled + your Constitution Modifier if it has at least 1 Hit Point, even if you do not regain Hit Points.

{{align-text:left
If the Doll is reduced to 0 Hit Points, it is destroyed. Replacing a Doll requires a Ritual performed as a Long Rest Activity if you retain the Doll's components. If they are not retrieved, replacing the components requires 500 LMD. If you already have a Doll when you create one, the old Doll immediately perishes.
}}

\column

#### 5th Level: Pull the Strings
You gain the following benefits:

**Doll's Dance.** When you use an Operator Skill with Instant Duration, you may use it as if  in your Doll's space.

**Look in the Mirror.** Your Doll gains the benefit of your Weapon Specialisation, Weapon Adept, Weapon Expertise, and Weapon Mastery Features.

**Marionette.** When you would be reduced to 0 Hit Points, you can choose to swap places with your Doll if it is within 100 feet of you; if you do, your Doll takes the Damage you would have taken in your place. Once you have used this Feature, you may not do so again until you have completed a Long Rest.

#### 5th Level: Musical Chairs
When you use your Bonus Action to instruct your Doll, you may also expend 1 Prowess to swap places with your Doll as part of that Bonus Action.
:
**Gaining Prowess as a Dollkeeper.** If you reduce a Creature to 0 Hit Points, you gain 1 Prowess if that Creature had taken Damage from your Doll since the beginning of your last Turn. You also gain 1 Prowess when your Doll reduces a Creature to 0 Hit Points; you can only gain this Prowess once per Turn.

#### 11th Level: Masquerade
Your Dolls are more advanced; uncannily so. You gain the following benefits:

**Doll Multiattack.** When your Doll takes the Strike Action, it can make one additional Attack as part of it. 

**Mistaken Identity**. When a Creature would make an Attack against you or your Doll, and the other Creature is within range of that Attack, you can force that Creature to make a DC18 Perception Check. If they fail the Check, you may choose whether that Attack targets you or your Doll. If they succeed on the Check, they become immune to this Feature for 1 hour. 
You can use this Feature a number of times equal to your Proficiency Bonus, and regain all expended uses whenever you finish a Short or Long Rest.

**Talking Heads.** Your Doll can now speak using your voice. It will only say what it is ordered to say; if it is within 100 feet of you, you can communicate telepathically what you wish for it to say.

#### 15th Level: Living Doll
Arts used in the Creation of your Doll have been used to reinforce you. You gain the following benefits:

**Reciprocity.** When either you or your Doll recovers Hit Points except by Resting, the other can gain Temporary Hit Points equal to the Hit Points recovered. 

**Share Arts.** When an Art that does not restore Hit Points or deal Damage is cast targeting you or your Doll, you can choose for the other to also be affected by that Art.

\column

#### 20th Level: Artistry is Violence
Your control over your Doll is legendary. You gain the following benefits:

**Doll Multiattack.** When your Doll takes the Strike Action, it can now make two additional Attacks as part of that Action.

**Doll Raid.** When you take the Attack Action, you can also cause your Doll to take the Strike Action (no Action required). Once you have used this Feature, you may not do so again until you have completed a Long Rest.

### Trapmaster
To battle face to face is one thing, but to maim and kill your foe without ever looking them in the eye is another entirely. Thus, Trapmasters tend to fall into one of three archetypes; the insane, the vindictive, and the stone cold.

#### 1st Level: Environmental Warfare
The environment is the greatest weapon of a Trapmaster; if it is not dangerous yet, it will be. You gain the following benefits:

**Place Trap.** Over the course of ten seconds (or two rounds), you can plant a Trap in an unoccupied space on the ground within 5 feet of you; unless stated, a Trap can only be triggered once. The Traps available for you to place are found below. 

There is no limit to the amount of Traps you can have planted at once. You can use this Feature a number of times equal to your twice your Proficiency Bonus, and regain all expended uses when you complete a Long Rest.

**Devil's Own Luck.** The environment is full of danger if you know where to look. As a Bonus Action, you can suggest an visible object in the environment to function as a Trap of your choice (except Timed Explosive), and the trigger condition for that "trap". This trigger condition should be an action you actively perform, not when the Creature enters a defined space like ordinary traps. If the GM agrees with your suggestion, that object functions in the way you suggested; once it has been triggered, this "trap" is expended as other Traps are. Once you have successfully suggested a Trap in this way, you may not do so again until you complete a Short or Long Rest.  

{{note
##### Example: Devil's Own Luck
Player A, a Trapmaster, sees a barrel that could possibly contain fuel. As a Bonus Action, they use the Devil's Own Luck Feature to suggest to their GM that it could explode as if it was an Explosive Trap if they shot it with their handgun. Their GM agrees, and so Player A holds their Action to shoot the barrel as an enemy runs past it.
:
Player B sees some taut cabling in a wall. As a Bonus Action, they use the Devil's Own Luck Feature to suggest to their GM that it could lash out like a Snare Trap when they cut it with their knife. Their GM agrees, and they hold their Action to do that. A Creature comes running up to to Player B. They use their Held Action to cut the cabling, and the hostile Creature is caught in the "snare". 
}}

**Skill Expertise.** You gain Proficiency in the Sleight of Hand Skill. If you already have Proficiency, you instead gain Expertise. 


\column

{{note
##### Trap DC
Many Features in this Branch call for your Trap DC. The Trap DC of a Creature is equal to 8 + Dexterity Modifier or Intelligence Modifier (Creature's Choice) + Proficiency Bonus.
}}

#### 5th Level: Panic Inducer
Your demeanour and trickery is well crafted to undermine the confidence of your enemies; or instill them with false confidence. You gain the following benefits:

**Irritating Distraction.** If you make an Attack against a Creature, that Creature has Disadvantage on Saving Throws against your Traps and Warning Point Feature until the beginning of your next Turn.

**Jester's Grace.** You do not trigger Traps that you or your allies have placed when you move into the triggering area. Additionally, you have Advantage on any Saving Throw you make against Traps placed by you.

**Warning Point.** When a Creature would trigger one of your Traps, you can instead cause them to instead have to succeed on an Intelligence Saving Throw against your Trap DC or have their Speed reduced to 0 until the beginning of their next Turn; they are not aware of the Trap, but feel as if they are in danger if they move. When you have delayed the activation of one of your Traps in this way, you may cause that Trap to trigger as a Reaction. 

<img 
  src='https://arknights.wiki.gg/images/Robin.png?8dad75' 
  style='position:absolute;top:480px;right:-50px;width:550px' />

\page
{{pageNumber,auto}}

#### 5th Level: Here's One I Made Earlier
You can expend 1 Prowess to immediately set a Trap as an Action rather than its normal time. Additionally, the range at which you can set up that Trap is increased to 60 feet; it is not obvious to others where you have set this new trap. 

**Gaining Prowess as a Trapmaster.** As a Trapmaster, you gain 1 Prowess at the beginning of your Turn if two or more Creatures have failed a Saving Throw against you or your Traps since your last Turn. Additionally, you gain 1 Prowess when a Creature is reduced to 0 Hit Points as a result of one of your Traps.

#### 11th Level: Bait the Trap
You gain the following benefits:

**Come and Get Me.** When you hit a Creature with an Attack, you can force them to make a Wisdom Saving Throw against your Trap DC. On a failed Save, during their next Turn they must attempt to move towards you if possible; they will not walk into easily visible harm, such as falling from a great height, as part of this movement. On a successful Save, nothing happens. You can use this Feature a number of times equal to your Proficiency Bonus, and regain all expended uses when you complete a Short or Long Rest.

#### 15th Level: Agent of Chaos
After a Creature takes Damage as a result of failing a Saving Throw against one of your Traps, it loses any Resistances or Damage Reduction it has against the Damage of that Trap until the beginning of their next Turn. 

\column

#### 20th Level: House of Horrors
To approach you is to die. To flee from you is to die. And the entire time, you'll be there; laughing.
:
You gain the following benefits:

**Trap DC Increase.** Your Trap DC is permanently increased by 5. 

**Party Favours.** Once per Long Rest as an Action, you can immediately set six traps, ignoring the time required. Additionally, the range at which you can set up those Traps is increased to 100 feet, and does not have to be in a location you can see, though you must be aware of it; it is not obvious to others where you have set these Traps.

  
{{imageMaskCorner21,--offsetX:54%,--offsetY:-26%,--rotation:-5
<img 
  src='https://arknights.wiki.gg/images/thumb/6/64/W_Elite_2.png/1024px-W_Elite_2.png?20bcc7'

}}

#### Traps
The following Traps are presented in alphabetical order.
:
**Set Up:** The area you set up that Trap in, and how the Trap is triggered.
:
**On Trigger:** What happens when the Trap is triggered.

{{note
##### Detecting and Disarming Traps
Detecting a Trap requires a successful Perception Check as an Action against the Trap DC of the Creature who set it, or the Creature's Passive Perception being greater than the Trap DC.
:
Disarming a Trap requires a successful Sleight of Hand Check as an Action against the Trap DC of the Creature who set it, while within 5 feet of the Trap. Failing the Check by 5 or more results in the trap being immediately triggered.
}}

##### Alarm Trap
**Set Up:** :: You set an Alarm Trap in a line up to 10 foot long and 1 foot wide. When a Creature enters the area, they trigger the Trap. This Trap can be triggered multiple times.

**On Trigger:** :: A loud sound is produced, capable of being heard clearly up to 300 feet away.

##### Explosive Trap
**Set Up:** :: You set an Explosive Trap in a 5-foot square on the ground. When a Creature enters the area, the Trap is triggered.

**On Trigger:** :: The trap explodes violently. All Creatures within a 15-foot-radius Sphere centered on the Trap must make a Dexterity Saving Throw against your Trap DC. On a failed Save, they take 4d8 Thunder Damage. On a successful Save, they take half as much Damage. 

:: The Damage of this Trap is increased to 6d8 at 7th Level, and 8d8 at 14th Level.

##### Gas Trap
**Set Up:** :: You set an Gas Trap in a 5-foot square on the ground. When a Creature enters the area, the Trap is triggered.

**On Trigger:** :: A rush of toxic gas fills the air in a 15-foot-radius sphere centred on the Trap for 1 minute; if a Creature is in the sphere when it appears, or enters it for the first time during a Turn, they must make a Constitution Saving Throw against your Trap DC. On a failed Save, they take 2d8 Poison Damage and are Poisoned. On a successful Save, they take half as much Damage and are not Poisoned. 

:: The Damage of this Trap is increased to 3d8 at 7th Level, and 4d8 at 14th Level.

{{imageMaskEdge1,--offset:1%,--rotation:270
<img 
  src='https://arknights.wiki.gg/images/thumb/8/8b/Dorothy.png/800px-Dorothy.png' 
  style='position:absolute;top:30px;right:-300px;width:1000px;clip-path:rect(auto auto auto 28%);' />
}}


\page
{{pageNumber,auto}}

{{wide

##### Incendiary Trap
**Set Up:** :: You set an Gas Trap in a 5-foot square on the ground. When a Creature enters the area, the Trap is triggered.

**On Trigger:** :: A plume of fire and flammable liquid erupts in a 15-foot-radius sphere centred on the Trap; all Creatures within this area must make a Dexterity Saving Throw against your Trap DC. On a failed Save, they take 2d8 Fire Damage and begin Burning. On a successful Save, they take half as much Damage, and do not suffer the Burning Condition. 

:: The Damage of this Trap is increased to 3d8 at 7th Level, and 4d8 at 14th Level.

##### Paddle Trap
**Set Up:** :: You set a Paddle Trap in a line up to 10 foot long and 1 foot wide. When a Creature enters the area, they trigger the Trap.

**On Trigger:** :: A blunt object is launched at the triggering Creature. That Creature must make a Dexterity Saving Throw against your Trap DC. On a failed Save, they take 4d8 Bludgeoning Damage and are Dazed for 1 minute. On a successful Save, they take half as much Damage, and are not Dazed. If this damage reduces them to 0 Hit Points, they are knocked Unconscious instead of killed.

:: The Damage of this Trap is increased to 6d8 at 7th Level, and 8d8 at 14th Level.

##### Snare Trap
**Set Up:** :: You set a Snare Trap in a line up to 10 foot long and 1 foot wide. When a Creature enters the area, they trigger the Trap.

**On Trigger:** :: A coiling rope or similar object attempts to tie the Creature up. The triggering Creature must succeed on a Dexterity Saving Throw against your Trap DC or become Restrained by it. As an Action during their Turn, they can make a Athletics or Acrobatics Check against your Trap DC to end the Condition. Alternatively, a different Creature within 5 feet of them can free them by cutting them free with a bladed weapon, or succeeding on a Sleight of Hand Check against your Trap DC as an Action.

##### Spike Trap
**Set Up:** :: You set an Spike Trap in a line up to 10 foot long and 1 foot wide. When a Creature enters the area, they trigger the Trap. This Trap can be triggered multiple times.

**On Trigger:** :: The triggering Creature must succeed on a Dexterity Saving Throw against your Spell Save DC or take 1d8 Piercing Damage, and begin Bleeding.

:: From 7th Level, Creatures who fail this Saving Throw also have their Speed reduced by 10 feet until they are no longer missing any Hit Points.

##### Timed Explosive
**Set Up:** :: You plant a bomb. When you do so, you nominate a length of time until the bomb explodes; no greater than one hour, and no less than 10 seconds (or two Rounds). 
**On Trigger:** :: When the time has elapsed, the bomb explodes, dealing 6d10 Thunder Damage to everything within a 20-foot-radius sphere centred on the bomb; this explosion goes around corners. Vehicles and Structures take double Damage from this explosion.

}}


\page
{{pageNumber,auto}}

## Supporter
In Leithanien, beautiful music flows from the cello of a raven-haired Sankta. Her audience are utterly enraptured, hanging on her every note. A slight smile cracks her porcelain features; it is time to drive them to violence. 
:
In the Rim Billiton outback, a Savra works feverishly under the killer glare of the sun. The cooling system of her truck has failed; without it, the Infected girl within, unable to regulate her own body temperature, will die. She grimaces, cursing as she wipes the sweat from her brow, but she does not stop. 
:
In Trimounts, a Liberi's Arts flash violently, stopping another onslaught from a Department of Defence soldier. Unregulated scientific advancement has led to unprecedented tragedy, and she will allow it not one further step without ethics' yoke; for everyone harmed, for her friends, she will make her stand.  
::::
{{classTable,frame,decoration,

| 19th  |  +6  | Ability Score Improvement |  d12
| 20th  |  +6  | Will Into Being | d12
}}

\column

{{tocExcludeH3 
### Facilitators and Fixers
}}
The role of a Supporter is rarely to take centre stage, though they do not shy away from it. Supporters, both moral and immoral, exist to facilitate the actions of their comrades; to allow them to reach new heights, and in so doing take the Supporter with them. They function best at the core of a coordinated team effort, delivering aid where it is needed, when it is needed. 
:
The work of a Supporter is often an unsung role, but the absence of one is a chasmic gap.  

### Class Features

As a Supporter, you gain the following Class Features:

#### Hit Points
**Hit Dice:** 1d6 per Supporter Level<br>
**Hit Points at 1st Level:** 6 + your Constitution Modifier<br>
**Hit Points at Higher Levels:** 1d6 (or 4) + your Constitution Modifier per Supporter Level after 1st

#### Proficiencies
**Armor:** Light Armour, Light Shields<br>
**Weapons:** Light Melee<br>
**Tools:** A Tool Kit/Vehicle Proficiency of your Choice<br>
**Saving Throws:** Choose two from the following: Constitution, Charisma, Wisdom <br>
**Skills:** Three from the following: Arts, Lore, Tech, Insight, Deception, Persuasion

#### Equipment
You start with the following Equipment, in addition to any Equipment granted by your Background. 

- A) 1x Light Melee Weapon
- A) 1x Light Melee Weapon or B) 1x Light Shield
- Protective Clothing
- Two Tool Kits you are Proficient with

OR

- 1300 LMD


\page
{{pageNumber,auto}}

#### 1st Level: Supporter Branch
Choose a Supporter Branch which most closely fits the style you envision for yourself. Choose one from the following list; further details on these Branches can be found at the end of the Class Description. The Branch you choose grants you Features at Level 1, 4, 10, 13, and 17.
- **Artificer:** Artificer Supporters specialise in the construction of useful aids for their allies, from mechanical turrets and drones to portable cover and more, and defend what they have made with weighty blows.  
- **Bard:** To a Bard, Arts and Performance are irrevocably linked. When the muse strikes them, they compose new pieces entwined with Originium Arts, releasing the stored power as they perform.
- **Hexer:** Hexer Supporters aid their allies by ensuring that their foes are so thoroughly deflated that they pose no further threat; their potent hexes are transferred as they cast Arts.
- **Ritualist:** For a Ritualist Supporter, everything is about the bigger picture. The here and now only matters in how it can change the future, as they strive to fulfil their rituals to earn long term, or even permanent, benefits for themselves and others. 
- **Summoner:** Creating constructs from their Arts and ambient material, Summoner Supporters fill the holes in their allies' ranks, guarding their backs on multiple angles at once.

#### 1st Level: Provide Aid
Your calling is to help others achieve the peak of their capability. This help is represented by your Aid Die, which is a d8.
:
**Providing Aid.** As a Bonus Action, you can Provide Aid to another Creature within 60 feet who can see or hear you. This consumes one of your Aid Dice, and grants it to that Creature. A Creature can only have one Aid Die at a time.
:
Once within the next ten minutes, when that Creature makes an Attack Roll, Skill Check, Saving Throw (except Death Saving Throws), Damage Roll, or Healing Roll, they may expend and roll that Aid Dice, adding the number rolled to the total. 
:
**Number of Uses.** The amount of Aid Dice you possess is equal to either your Intelligence, Wisdom, or Charisma Modifier (choose when you gain this Feature). You regain all expended uses when you finish a Long Rest.
:
**Aid Die Size.** Your Aid Die is a d8. This changes when you reach certain Supporter Levels, as shown in the Aid Die column of the Supporter Features table. 

\column

#### 2nd Level: Basic Operator Skill
You learn a Basic Operator Skill from the Basic Supporter Skills table, or from any other Basic Skill table available to you.

#### 3rd Level: Basic Talent
You gain a Basic Talent of your choice from the Basic Supporter Talents table. When you reach 8th Level, you may either pick a different Basic Talent to gain, or upgrade your Basic Talent, gaining its Module Effect.

#### 4th Level: Ability Score Improvement
When you reach 4th level, and again at 6th, 8th, 10th, 12th, 16th, and 19th level, you can increase one Ability Score of your choice by 2, or you can increase two Ability Scores of your choice by 1. As normal, you can’t increase an Ability Score above 20 using this feature.

#### 5th Level: Advanced Aid
When a Creature expends your Aid Die, they gain an additional effect based on whether the die is benefitting a Attack Roll, Skill Check, Saving Throw, Damage Roll, or Healing Roll, as detailed in the Advanced Aid table:

##### Advanced Aid
| Benefitting Roll  | Additional Effect |
|:------------------|:-----------------:|
| Attack Roll       | The Critical Range of that Attack is increased by 1. |
| Skill Check       | The Creature rolls your Aid Die an additional time, and choose which result they wish to use. 
| Saving Throw      | The Creature can gain Temporary Hit Points equal to the amount rolled on your Aid Die + your Proficiency Bonus |
| Damage Roll      | When a Creature rolls this Aid Die to increase the Damage they deal, they can choose a different Creature within 10 feet of a Creature they are going to Damage; that additional Creature takes Force Damage equal to the amount rolled on the Aid Die. |
| Healing Roll      | Creatures that recover Hit Points from this Roll can also gain Temporary Hit Points equal to the amount rolled on your Aid Die. |

#### 7th Level: Advanced Operator Skill
You learn an Advanced Operator Skill from the Advanced Supporter Skills table, or from any other Advanced Skill table available to you.

#### 9th Level: Advanced Talent
You gain an Advanced Talent of your choice from the Advanced Supporter Talents table. When you reach 13th Level, you may either pick a different Advanced Talent to gain, or upgrade your Advanced Talent, gaining its Module Effect.

\page
{{pageNumber,auto}}

#### 11th Level: Lighten the Load
Your efforts can lift entire armies up. When a Creature other than yourself within 60 feet of you rolls one of your Aid Die, you can use your Reaction to choose a different Creature that can hear you, except yourself, within 60 feet and grant them one of your Aid Die without expending any of your Aid Die. You cannot use this Feature on these supplementary Aid Dice. 

#### 14th Level: Elite Operator Skill
You learn an Elite Operator Skill from the Elite Supporter Skills table, or from any other Elite Skill table available to you.

#### 15th Level: Ever Reliable 
You are a steadfast pillar of the team, without whom they would surely crumble. You gain the following benefits:

**Aid Die Regeneration.** If your Turn begins and you have no Aid Dice remaining, you regain one Aid Die.

**Short Rest Refill.** Whenever you finish a Short Rest, you recover Aid Dice equal to half of your maximum Aid Dice, rounding up.

#### 18th Level: Self-Supportive
At the beginning of your Turn, you gain a special Aid Die; this Aid Die is granted automatically to you, and cannot be granted to another Creature or used by any of your Features. You can expend this Aid Die as if you were the target of your own Provide Aid Feature.

#### 20th Level: Will Into Being
Such is your aptitude and skill that what you say goes. 
:
When a Creature rolls one of your Aid Die, you may add your Proficiency Bonus to the result of that roll.
