You regain all expended Instruction Dice upon completing a Long Rest.	| You gain a third Instruction Dice, and your Instruction Dice become d8s.
| Mirror Move	| When a Creature you can see within 30 feet deals Arts Damage to you, you can expend your Reaction to deal 2d8 Force Damage to that Creature. You can use this Feature a number of times equal to your Proficiency Bonus, and regain all expended uses when you complete a Long Rest. |	The Force Damage is increased to 4d8.
| Painful Lesson	| When a Creature within 30 feet who has a detrimental Status Condition makes an Attack against you, you can expend your Reaction to cause that Creature to take 2d6 Force Damage.	| The Force Damage is increased to 4d6.
| Part-Time Inventor	| You gain the Equipment Modification Feature of the Inventor Specialist. However, your maximum number of Inventions is 1, and you cannot apply any Modification marked as Dangerous.	| Your maximum number of Inventions becomes 2.
| Reliable Talent	| When you make a Check using a Skill you are Proficient in, treat any result on the d20 of 8 or less as an 8.	| You cannot have Disadvantage on any Check using a Skill or Tool you are Proficient with.
| Shatterer	| When you deal Damage (except Cold Damage) to a Creature that has the Frozen Condition, that Creature loses the Frozen Condition and takes 3d8 Cold Damage. 	| When you remove the Frozen Condition from a Creature in this way, they gain the Dazed Condition until the end of their next Turn.


}}

\page
{{pageNumber,auto}}

### Artificer
For an Artificer Supporter, the essence of support is providing exactly what your team needs; and if you don't have it already, you will build it. They are combat engineers, constructing machines that can both save lives, and take them.

#### 1st Level: Tools of Creation and Destruction

You are familiar with the heavy tools of your trade, but you are no boorish brute. You gain the following benefits:

**Additional Weapon Proficiencies.** You gain Proficiency with Heavy Melee Weapons.

**As One.** When you take the Attack Action during your Turn, you may also cause each Automaton you created to take an Action of your choosing. If you do, that Automaton cannot take that same Action until the beginning of your next Turn.   

**Calculated Strikes.** When you make a Weapon Attack, you may use your Intelligence Modifier, instead of Strength or Dexterity, for the Attack and Damage Rolls.

<img 
  src='https://arknights.wiki.gg/images/Windflit.png?a9d15e' 
  style='position:absolute;top:420px;right:350px;width:600px;clip-path:rect(auto auto 99% auto);' />

\column


#### 1st Level: Master of Machines
You support your allies through the creation of specialised tools and aids. You gain the following benefits:

**Expert Touch.** You gain Proficiency in the Technology Skill; if you are already Proficient, you instead gain Expertise. Additionally, you gain Proficiency in Vehicles (Drones).

**Fort Builder.** As an Action, you can deploy a piece of portable cover up to 5 feet in length, 3 foot in height, and 1 foot in depth, to an unoccupied space within 5 feet of you; it is sufficient to provide Half Cover. You can use this Feature a number of times equal to your Proficiency Bonus, and regain all expended uses when you complete a Long Rest.
:
**Professional Engineer.** You have created a custom Automaton; choose one of Automaton Variants on Page 466/467. This Automaton gains benefits, as befitting a custom job:
- It has additional Maximum Hit Points equal to your Technology Modifier. 
- Its Proficiency Bonus is equal to yours.
- When it makes an Attack Roll, its bonus to hit is equal to your Intelligence Modifier + your Proficiency Bonus.

**Your Automaton in Combat.** In Combat, the Automaton acts during your Turn. It can use Reactions on its own, but it will not move or take Actions unless you use your Bonus Action to verbally command it to either move to a location of your choice, or take one Action in its Stat Block. To command your Automaton in this way, you must be within 100 feet of it.

**Restoring or Replacing your Automaton.** As a Construct, the Automaton cannot regain Hit Points in most ways (See Page 9).
:
If your Automaton is reduced to 0 Hit Points, it is destroyed. You can rebuild a destroyed Automaton as a Long Rest Activity if you retain the destroyed Automaton’s components. If they are not retrieved, replacing the components costs 500 LMD. The maximum number of Automatons you can have at once is one; if you create more than your maximum, you cannot upkeep or control the first anymore, and it will crumble to uselessness.

{{note
##### Equipment and Proficiency Exchange
When you choose this Branch, you may exchange any Tool Kit or Vehicle Proficiency you have for Proficiency with the Engineering Kit.
:
Additionally, when you choose this Branch, you may exchange one Toolkit from your Starting Equipment for an Engineering Kit, and 1x Light Melee Weapon or 1x Light Shield from your Starting Equipment for 1x Heavy Melee Weapon.
}}


\page
{{pageNumber,auto}}

#### 4th Level: Heavy Duty Upgrades
Refining of your craft has allowed you to produce upgraded versions of the typical Automatons. You gain the following benefits:

**Basic Personality Matrix.** You can instill a rudimentary sentience whenever you create a new custom Automaton, or as a Short Rest Activity with a pre-existing custom Automaton. It can speak, understands that it is artificial, and is unwaveringly loyal to you. When you instil sentience, roll a d6 on the following table to determine its personality:

##### Machine Personality
| Roll | Personality |
|:----:|:-----------:|
| 1    | Taciturn    |
| 2    | Caring      |
| 3    | Excitable   |
| 4    | Warlike     |
| 5    | Nervous     |
| 6    | Analytic    |

**Loaded for Bear.** When you create a new custom Automaton, or use your Fort Builder Feature, you may apply one of the Upgrades, detailed below, based on whether you create a Automaton or Portable Cover.
:
Additionally, as a Long Rest Activity, you can install one Upgrade onto each of your Automatons; the maximum number of Upgrades an Automaton can have is two.


#### 10th Level: Concussive Maintenance
As anyone who has worked with machines will tell you, sometimes they just need a good smack to get working.
:
When you take the Attack Action, if there is an Automaton within your Reach, you may sacrifice one of your Attacks to bash that Automaton (your choice if there are multiple) into shape. Expend and roll one of your Aid Die, and multiply the result by your Proficiency Bonus; that Automaton recovers that many Hit Points.

#### 13th Level: Rise of the Machines
A robot is no longer just that when it has been made by you. You gain the following benefits:

**Advanced Personality Matrix.** The personality matrix of your Automatons is upgraded. Their Intelligence, Wisdom, and Charisma Scores are increased to 10, each gains Proficiency in two Saving Throws of your choice, and they can understand complex instructions; when you use your Bonus Action to command an Automaton, you can cause it to both move and take an Action.

**Maximum Automaton Limit.** The maximum amount of Automatons you can have at once is increased to 2. When you use your Bonus Action to command your Automaton, you can command all of your Automatons simultaneously.

\column

#### 17th Level: Armoured Core
Dangerous does not define your machines; devastating does.
:
When you create an custom Automaton, or use your Fort Builder Feature, you may immediately apply two of the Upgrades detailed by your Heavy Duty Upgrades Feature to your creation, rather than just one. Additionally, the maximum number of Upgrades one of your Automatons can have is increased to 3.

<img 
  src='https://arknights.wiki.gg/images/Catherine.png?b626c2' 
  style='position:absolute;top:240px;right:0px;width:380px;clip-path:rect(auto auto 99% auto);' />

\page
{{pageNumber,auto}}

#### <u>Heavy Duty Upgrades</u>
The following Upgrades are presented firstly by target, and then by alphabetical order.

{{note
##### Artificer DC
Some of the Heavy Duty Upgrades call for your Artificer DC; this is calculated as 8 + your Intelligence Modifier + your Proficiency Bonus. Additionally, when you would use your Martial DC, you can use your Artificer DC instead.
}}

#### Automaton
:
##### AEGIS System
**Effect:** This Automaton possess a powerful Originium Arts inhibiting barrier; Creatures within a 10 foot radius sphere centred on this Automaton have Advantage on Saving Throws against Arts that originate from outside of this radius. 

##### Arbalest (Turret Only)
**Effect:** This Automaton is fitted with a second, explosive crossbow.
:
Once per Turn, as an Action the Automaton can launch an explosive bolt at a Creature it can see within 80 feet; the Automaton makes a Crossbow Attack against that Creature. On a hit, it deals Piercing Damage equal to 3 + one roll of your Aid Die instead of its normal Damage, and all Creatures within 5 feet of the original target must succeed on a Dexterity Saving against your Artificer DC or take Thunder Damage equal to one roll of your Aid Die.

##### Arts Detonator 
**Effect:** As a Reaction when this Automaton is hit by an Attack, it can release the energy stored within specialised power cells in an explosive manner; all other Creatures within 10 feet of the Automaton must make a Dexterity Saving Throw against your Artificer DC. On a failed Save, they take Force Damage equal to three rolls of your Aid Die and are knocked 10 feet away from the Automaton. On a successful Save, they take half as much Damage, and suffer no other effects.
:
Once this Automaton has used this Reaction, it may not do so again until both it and its Creator complete a Short or Long Rest at the same time. However, if the Automaton is reduced to 0 Hit Points, it may use this Reaction even if it has expended its use of it.


\column

##### Bombtail (Air Support Only)
**Effect:** This Automaton possesses a powerful explosive payload. As an Action, it can choose a visible point within 60 feet; each Creature in a 10-foot radius Sphere centred on that point must make a Dexterity Saving Throw against your Artificer DC. On a failed Save, they take Thunder Damage equal to five rolls of your Aid Die. On a successful Save, they take half as much Damage.
:
Once this Automaton has used this Action, it may not do so again until both it and its Creator complete a Long Rest at the same time.

##### CASTLE System
**Effect:** This Automaton is upgraded with a series of bladed weapons to engage foes. These weapons have the following profile: 
:
**Cutting Edge Blades.** *Melee Weapon Attack:* Bonus equals Proficiency Bonus + your Intelligence Modifier, Reach 5ft, one target, *Hit* = Strength Modifier + one roll of your Aid Dice in Slashing Damage.
:
Additionally, whenever this Automaton takes the Attack Action, it may Attack an additional time using this Weapon.

##### Electroshock Cables
**Effect:** This Automaton can fire wires that deliver a powerful electric shock on contact. As an Action, it can choose a Creature it can see within 15 feet; that Creature must succeed on a Dexterity Saving Throw against your Artificer DC or take Lightning Damage equal to three rolls of your Aid Die, and be Stunned until the end of their next Turn.
:
Once this Automaton has used this Action, it may not do so again until both it and its Creator complete a Short or Long Rest at the same time.

##### Flamethrower
**Effect:** As an Action, the Automaton releases a 20 foot long Cone of flame in a chosen direction. Every Creature within that area must make a Dexterity Saving Throw against your Artificer DC; on a failed Save, they take Fire Damage equal to three rolls of your Aid Die and are Burning. On a successful Save, they take half as much Damage, and suffer no further effects. 
:
Once this Automaton has used this Action, it may not do so again until both it and its Creator complete a Short or Long Rest at the same time.


\page
{{pageNumber,auto}}

##### Gas Projectors
**Effect:** As an Action, the Automaton begins emitting toxic gas in a 10 foot radius sphere that moves with the Automaton for the next minute. When a Creature enters the area for the first time during their Turn, or begins their Turn within the area, they must make a Constitution Saving Throw against your Artificer DC. On a failed Save, they take Poison Damage equal to two rolls of your Aid Die, and are Poisoned until the beginning of their next Turn. On a successful Save, they take half as much Damage, and are not Poisoned.
:
Once this Automaton has used this Action, it may not do so again until both it and its Creator complete a Long Rest at the same time.

##### HOIST System (Air Support Only)
**Effect:** This Automaton is fitted with more powerful engines and a system by which a Creature can hold onto it and be lifted. One Medium or smaller Humanoid Creature can grab onto the Automaton when they are within 5 feet of it, can see it, and have at least one empty hand; while they are holding onto it, that Creature's Speed is reduced to 0, and the Automaton's Speed is halved. However, when the Automaton moves, it also moves that Creature, including flying movement.

##### Medical Dispenser (Platform Only)
**Effect:**  This Automaton is upgraded with the capability to dispense restorative substances to nearby wounded. 
:
As an Action, this Automaton chooses a Creature it can see within 5 feet; that Creature recovers Hit Points equal to one roll of your Aid Die.
:
This Automaton may perform this Action a number of times equal to your Intelligence Modifier, and regains all expended uses when it and its Creator complete a Short Rest at the same time.


##### Mobility System (Turret Only)
**Effect:** This Automaton is fitted with legs, tracks, or wheels, and gains a Walking Speed of 15 feet.

##### "Raptor" Armour
**Effect:** This Automaton has a Damage Reduction of 4 against Physical Damage. If it already has Physical Damage Reduction, that amount is increased by 4.

##### Reconnaissance Suite
**Effect:** When this Automaton deals Weapon Damage to a Creature, that Creature does not gain the benefit of cover until the start of your next Turn. Additionally, the Automaton gains a bright spotlight, capable of shining a 30 foot cone of bright light, with a further 30 feet of dim light.

\column

##### Rotary Crossbow (Requires Crossbow)
**Effect:** Once per Turn, when this Automaton makes an Attack using a Crossbow, it may make an additional Attack with that Crossbow as part of that Action.

##### Search and Rescue Suite (Platform Only)
**Effect:** This Automaton possesses powerful "arms" that it uses to relocate people, willingly or otherwise.
:
The Automaton is treated as being two sizes larger for the purposes of lifting and carrying. Additionally, as an Action, it may target a Creature within 5 feet of itself and attempt to move them. If the Creature is unwilling, it must succeed on a Strength Saving Throw against your Artificer DC. On a failed Save, or if the Creature is willing, this Automaton may immediately move up to half its Speed, moving the Creature with it.

##### Smoke Launcher
**Effect:** The Automaton can produce obscuring smoke as an Action; the smoke cloud is a 20 foot radius Sphere centred on the Automaton, and lasts until the end of your next Turn. For the Duration, any Attack that would pass into or out of the smoke is made at Disadvantage. 
:
This Automaton may perform this Action a number of times equal to your Intelligence Modifier, and regains all expended uses when it and its Creator complete a Short Rest at the same time.

##### Tracer Shots (Requires Crossbow)

**Effect:** After this Automaton has dealt Damage to a Creature using its Crossbow, the next Ranged Attack made against that Creature before the beginning of your next Turn is made at Advantage. 

<img 
  src='https://arknights.wiki.gg/images/e/eb/Alanna.png?acf426&20250410210327' 
  style='position:absolute;top:650px;right:90px;width:275px' />

\page
{{pageNumber,auto}}

\column

#### Mobile Cover
:
##### Mounted Crossbow
**Effect:** This Mobile Cover has a Heavy Crossbow mounted upon it. A Creature within 5 feet of the Mobile Cover can use this Crossbow as if they had it equipped; a Creature is considered Proficient with this Crossbow as long as they have an Intelligence Score of 8 or higher. 

##### Reinforced
**Effect:** This Mobile Cover is considered to be Three-Quarters Cover against Ranged Attacks, and Half Cover against Melee Attacks. 

##### Stimulant Vaporiser
**Effect:** This Mobile Cover is fitted with a vaporiser that is filled with a heady mix of anaesthetic gases and combat stimulants. Creatures that begin their Turn within 5 feet of the Mobile Cover can gain Temporary Hit Points equal to one roll of your Aid Dice.
:
After one minute, the vaporiser is exhausted, and no longer has any effect.

##### Ultralight
**Effect:** An allied Creature within 5 feet of the Mobile Cover can use a Bonus Action to begin shoving the Mobile Cover. Until the end of their Turn, when they perform grounded movement, they also move the Mobile Cover as they go.

### Bard
For a Bard, Music and Arts are intrinsically linked; one is not meaningfully distinct from the other. Their voice can raise their allies to new heights, driving them ever onwards, or it can blast forth in a thunderous roar.

#### 1st Level: A Magical Performance
You learn to compose and perform special Performances that are entwined with Arts.
:
**Composing a Performance.** When you finish a Short or Long Rest, you may expend and roll any amount of your Aid Dice; the total value rolled is your Muse. You then expend this Muse to compose a number of Performances, up to your Charisma Modifier.
:
A Performance is made up of Arts; a Performance has a minimum length of three Arts. To compose a Performance, choose Arts from the Performance Arts table by spending the Muse required for each Art you wish to add to that Performance; you cannot have the same Art consecutively within a Performance. When you have finished Composing your Performances, you gain Temporary Hit Points equal to the amount of Muse remaining, and all remaining Muse is then lost. 
:
**Putting on a Performance.** As an Action during your Turn, while you have a Instrument you are Proficient with equipped, you may choose one of your Performances and begin Performing. If you do, you immediately cast the first Art in that Performance. Arts cast as part of a Performance do not cost Arts Points.
:
When you cast an Art for the first time during a Performance that requires Concentration, you can ignore that Concentration requirement. However, for the Duration of your Performance, you must Concentrate on it as if it was an Art. When your Performance ends, you can choose one Art to maintain your Concentration on; all other Arts end. 
:
On each of your subsequent Turns, you may spend an Action to cast the next Art in the Performance, corresponding to the usual Action Cost for that Art. When all Arts in the Performance have been cast, or you end your Turn without having cast an Art from the Performance, that Performance ends.
:
Your Spellcasting Ability for Arts you cast as part of a Performance is Charisma, and you use an Instrument that you are Proficient with as a Spellcasting Focus with the Casting Unit Property.
:
**No Encore.** When a Performance ends, that Performance cannot be performed again; it must be Composed again. When you complete a Long Rest, all Performances you have not Performed are also lost and must be recomposed, as their Muse has faded.

\column

**Rising Crescendo.** When you cast the second Art of your Performance, and for each Art after that you cast as part of the Performance, you gain the following benefits until your Performance ends:
- **Additional Healing.** When you restore Hit Points or grant Temporary Hit Points with an Art, the amount you restore/grant is increased by 1. 
- **Shield Power.** When you cast the Shield of Faith or Mage Armour Arts, you may target an additional Creature to gain the effects.
- **Spell Save DC Increase.** Your Spell Save DC increases by 1.
- **Spell Attack Bonus.** You gain a +1 Bonus to your Spell Attack Rolls. 


{{imageMaskEdge7,--offset:6%,--rotation:270
<img 
  src='https://arknights.wiki.gg/images/c/c5/Sora_Skin_2.png?946d75&20230927011948' 
  style='position:absolute;top:90px;right:-400px;width:1200px;clip-path:rect(18% auto auto auto);' />
}}


\page
{{pageNumber,auto}}

{{wide

#### 1st Level: Soul of the Bard
Music is a part of your very being. You gain the following benefits:

**Instrument Proficiency.** You gain Proficiency in three Instruments of your choice.

**Speak to the Soul.** When you or a Creature that can hear you rolls one of your Aid Die, you can replace the result of that die with your Performance Modifier. Once you have done so, you may not do so again until you complete a Short or Long Rest.

**Performance Expertise.** You gain Proficiency in the Performance Skill. If you already have Proficiency, you gain Expertise instead. 

#### 4th Level: Font of Inspiration
When you begin Performing, and at the beginning of each of your Turns while you are Performing, you gain a Special Aid Die. This Aid Die can only be used for your Provide Aid Feature, and is lost if you do not grant it to another Creature before the end of your Turn.

#### 10th Level: Time Signature Change
You can change the tempo of your Performances to better suit the moment, and the audience. While you are Performing, you can cast the next Art in your Performance as an Action or a Bonus Action; you can do both within the same Turn, allowing you to cast more than one Art a Turn.

#### 13th Level: Emergency Improv
As an Action during your Turn, while you have an Instrument you are Proficient with equipped, you can quickly try to come up with a Performance on the spot; make a DC17 Performance Check, as if you were using the Improvise a Tune Proficient Use of your Instrument. If you succeed, you may either:
- Immediately expend and roll one of your Aid Die, gaining Muse equal to the amount rolled, and Compose a Performance using that Muse. This Performance is lost if you do not begin Performing it within a minute of Composing it. 
- Immediately expend and roll one of your Aid Die, gaining Muse equal to the amount rolled, and insert an Art into your current Performance at any point with Muse cost equal to or less than Muse you gained.

{{align-text:left
Once you have used this Feature, you may not do so again until you have completed a Long Rest.
}}

#### 17th Level: Fortissimo
Your Performance can shake the soul, such is its dynamism and character.
:
Your Rising Crescendo Feature now increases at twice the rate; each Art you cast as part of a Performance after the first is considered to be two Arts for that Feature.

}}
::

### Hexer

<div align="right">
The support a Hexer provides is inherently malicious in nature; they leave their enemies in a crippled, writhing heap with their Arts, making them easy pickings. This vindictiveness is archetypal of a Hexer, but while such focused cruelty oft makes for poor allies, <br>
Hexers perform such mischief<br> 
in service to their comrades.
</div>

{{imageMaskEdge1,--offset:1%,--rotation:90
<img 
  src='https://arknights.wiki.gg/images/c/c5/Shamare_Skin_1.png?5907bb&20230926234759' 
  style='position:absolute;top:10px;right:30px;width:1300px' />
}}

{{imageMaskEdge1,--offset:32%,--rotation:0
<img 
  src='https://arknights.wiki.gg/images/c/c5/Shamare_Skin_1.png?5907bb&20230926234759' 
  style='position:absolute;top:10px;right:30px;width:1300px' />
}}
  
\column

#### 1st Level: Hexer's Arts
You can cast Arts; the information below details how you use those rules as a Hexer.
:
**Arts Points.** The Hexer Arts Casting table shows how many Arts Points you have to cast your Level 1+ Arts. You regain all expended Arts Points when you finish a Long Rest.
:
**Maximum Arts Level.** The Hexer Arts Casting table shows the maximum Level you can cast an Art at, based on your Supporter Level. If an Art's Level is above your current Maximum Art Level, you cannot cast it, even if it is Prepared.
:
**Spellcasting Ability.** When you gain this Feature, choose either Intelligence, Wisdom, or Charisma; that is your Spellcasting Ability.

{{classTable,frame,decoration
##### Hexer Arts Casting
| Level | Maximum Art Level | Arts Points |
|:-----:|:-----------------:|:-----------:|
| 1st   |         1         |      3      |
| 2nd   |         1         |      3      |
| 3rd   |         1         |      4      |
| 4th   |         1         |      4      |
| 5th   |         2         |      6      |
| 6th   |         2         |      6      |
| 7th   |         2         |      7      |
| 8th   |         2         |      7      |
| 9th   |         3         |      10      |
| 10th  |         3         |      10     |
| 11th  |         3         |      11      |
| 12th  |         3         |      11     |
| 13th  |         4         |      12      |
| 14th  |         4         |      12      |
| 15th  |         4         |      15     |
| 16th  |         4         |      15     |
| 17th  |         5         |      16     |
| 18th  |         5         |      16     |
| 19th  |         5         |      17     |
| 20th  |         5         |      17     |

}}

***Hexer Arts Pools.*** When you gain this Feature, choose one of the Standard Arts Pools available to the Caster Class; you learn all of the Arts of 5th Level or less from those Pools, in addition to any Cantrips from those Pools. These Arts are always Prepared. At 11th Level, you may choose an additional Standard Arts Pool.


\page
{{pageNumber,auto}}

#### 1st Level: Potent Hex
When you deal Damage to one or more Creatures with an Art, you can apply a Hex to one of those Creatures for 1 minute.  Choose one of the following Hexes:

**Abjuring Hex.** When a Creature affected by this Hex makes a Damage Roll, it must also roll a die of the same size as your Aid Die, and subtracts half of that result from their Damage Roll, to a minimum of 1. 

**Binding Hex.** All Creatures affected by this Hex have their Speed reduced by a value equal to two rolls of your Aid Die, rounded to the nearest five.

**Breaking Hex.** All Creatures affected by this Hex have any Damage Reduction they have reduced by a value equal to one roll of your Aid Die.

**Looping Hex.** When you cast an Art that requires Concentration on a Creature affected by this Hex, that Art doesn't count against the maximum number of Arts you can concentrate on simultaneously, as long as the Creature remains Hexed.
:
Additionally, when a Creature affected by any of your Hexes is hit by an Attack, you deal Force Damage to them equal to one roll of your Aid Die. Once they have taken this Damage, they cannot take it again until the beginning of your next Turn.
:
You can use this Feature a number of times equal to your Proficiency Bonus, and regain all expended uses when you complete a Long Rest. A Creature can only be affected by one instance of each Hex at a time.

#### 4th Level: Cursemark
You develop a way to dilute your Hexes over a wider area, with lessened effect.
:
As a Bonus Action, you can expend a use of your Potent Hex Feature to choose a point you can see within 100 feet, and one of the options available for your Potent Hex Feature. For the next minute, while a Creature is within 10 feet of the chosen point, that Creature suffers the effects of the chosen Hex. However, if you do so, your Aid Die becomes a d6 for the purposes of that Hex. 

#### 10th Level: Controlled Cruelty
You learn finer control over your antagonistic Arts, gaining the following benefits:

**Empathic Pain.** When you deal the additional Force Damage of your Potent Hex Feature, you may deal that Force Damage to a different Creature you can see within 30 feet of the Hexed Creature.

**Spare the Rod.** When you use your Cursemark Feature, you may choose any number of Creatures to be unaffected by the Hex you place.


\column


#### 13th Level: Gospel of Curses
You develop additional Hexes with which to cripple your foes. The following Hexes are added to your list of options for your Potent Hex Feature:

**Voicethief Hex.** A Creature affected by this Hex must succeed on an Intelligence Saving Throw against your Spell Save DC or be Silenced until the end of their next Turn. While they remain affected by this Hex, they must repeat this Saving Throw at the beginning of each of their Turns.

**Wasting Hex.** Choose one Ability Score; when a Creature affected by this Hex makes an Ability Check or Saving Throw based on that Ability Score, they suffer a penalty to the roll equal to one roll of your Aid Die.

#### 17th Level: The Ultimate Hex
When you deal Damage to a Creature with an Art, you can apply the Ultimate Hex to one of those Creatures for 1 minute. The Ultimate Hex combines the effects of all options of your Potent Hex Feature, in addition to the following effect:

**Rupturing Hex.** The first time each Turn a Creature affected by this Hex is hit by an Attack, instead of taking Force Damage equal to one roll of your Aid Die, all hostile Creatures within 5 feet of that Creature take Force Damage equal to one roll of your Aid Die. Additionally, when that Creature is reduced to 0 Hit Points, all Creatures of your choice within 15 feet of that Creature must make a Dexterity Saving Throw against your Spell Save DC; on a failed Save, they take Force Damage equal to four rolls of your Aid Die. On a successful Save, they take half as much Damage. 
:
The Hexes applied as part of your Ultimate Hex are not cumulative with your Potent Hex Feature. Once you have applied the Ultimate Hex, you may not do so again until you complete a Long Rest.

\page
{{pageNumber,auto}}

### Ritualist
A Ritualist's Arts are poorly understood, sometimes even by themselves. Meticulously chasing goals only known to them, they warp the world around them on behalf of their allies; sometimes permanently. 

{{imageMaskEdge1,--offset:2%,--rotation:90
<img 
  src='https://arknights.wiki.gg/images/Tragodia.png?c00289' 
  style='position:absolute;top:170px;right:100px;width:800px' />
}}




\column

#### 1st Level: Ritual Arts
You can cast Arts; the information below details how you use those rules as a Ritualist.
:
**Arts Points.** The Ritualist Arts Casting table shows how many Arts Points you have to cast your Level 1+ Arts. You regain all expended Arts Points when you finish a Long Rest.
:
**Maximum Arts Level.** The Ritualist Arts Casting table shows the maximum Level you can cast an Art at, based on your Supporter Level. If an Art's Level is above your current Maximum Art Level, you cannot cast it, even if it is Prepared.
:
**Spellcasting Ability.** When you gain this Feature, choose either Intelligence, Wisdom, or Charisma; that is your Spellcasting Ability.

{{classTable,frame,decoration
##### Ritualist Arts Casting
| Level |Arts Known| Maximum Art Level | Arts Points |
|:-----:|:--------:|:-----------------:|:-----------:|
| 1st   |    5     |          1        |      3      |
| 2nd   |    5     |          1        |      3      | 
| 3rd   |    6     |          1        |      4      |  
| 4th   |    6     |          1        |      4      |        
| 5th   |    7     |          2        |      6      |       
| 6th   |    7     |          2        |      6      |        
| 7th   |    8     |          2        |      7      |        
| 8th   |    8     |          2        |      7      |        
| 9th   |    9     |          3        |      10     |        
| 10th  |    9     |          3        |      10     |        
| 11th  |    10    |          3        |      11     |       
| 12th  |    10    |          3        |      11     |        
| 13th  |    11    |          4        |      12     |        
| 14th  |    12    |          4        |      12     |        
| 15th  |    12    |          4        |      15     |        
| 16th  |    13    |          4        |      15     |        
| 17th  |    13    |          5        |      16     |        
| 18th  |    14    |          5        |      16     |        
| 19th  |    14    |          5        |      17     |        
| 20th  |    15    |          5        |      17     |        

}}

***Ritualist Arts Pools.*** When you gain this Feature, choose a number of Arts from any of the Standard Arts Pools available to the Caster Class equal to the Arts Known value in the Ritualist Arts Casting Table; they do not have to be from the same Arts Pool. You learn those Arts, and they are always Prepared. When your Arts Known increases, you may select an additional Art; when you finish a Long Rest, you may exchange one of these Arts for another valid selection.



\page
{{pageNumber,auto}}

#### 1st Level: The Essence of Ritual
The essence of Ritual is pre-preparation. You gain the following benefits:

**Ritual Performance.** You have access to a number of Rituals. These Rituals are divided into two types; Ephemeral Rituals and Permanent Rituals.
- **Ephemeral Rituals.** When you activate an Ephemeral Ritual, you gain that benefit until you next complete a Long Rest, after which you must activate the Ritual again to regain the benefit. 
- **Permanent Rituals.** When you activate a Permanent Ritual, you gain that benefit *permanently*, unless stated otherwise.

{{align-text:left
All of your Ephemeral and Permanent Rituals can be found in their respective tables below.
You access these Rituals by meeting certain criteria, listed below; once you have met a Criteria, you cannot meet that Criteria again until you complete a Short or Long Rest. When you meet one of these Criteria, you earn 1 Essence, and can immediately activate 1 Ephemeral Ritual of your choice (no Action required). To activate a Permanent Ritual of your choice, you must expend the required amount of Essence for that Ritual by completing a 10 minute Ritual, as if you were casting a Ritual Art.
}}
::
{{note
##### Ritual Criteria
You earn 1 Essence when you meet one of the following Criteria; you can only meet each Criteria once per Short or Long Rest:

- An Elite or Leader Creature you have damaged since the beginning of your last Turn is reduced to 0 Hit Points.
- You score a Critical Hit.
- You deal 30 or more Damage in a single instance (including spread across mutiple Creatures)
- You succeed on a Skill Check by 5 or more.
- You cast a levelled Art on an allied Creature that does not restore Hit Points or deal Damage.
- A Creature succeeds on a roll as a result of your Aid Die.

}}

{{note
##### Ritualist starting at later levels
If you are starting at a higher level, the Ritualist is lessened by its reliance on its Rituals and the time it takes to complete them. Thus, if you are starting at 7th Level or above, it is recommended that you choose two Permanent Rituals for you to have already completed. If you are starting at 14th Level or above, it is recommended that you instead choose four Permanent Rituals to have already completed.
}}


\column

**Accoutrements of Ritual.** You can conduct a 10 minute long Ritual to convert one of the following items into a Ritual Tool: A Melee Weapon that deals Piercing or Slashing Damage, an Instrument you are Proficient with, a set of Clothing, an Arts Wand or Arts Staff, or a trinket such as a ring or pendant. The item functions as a normal item of its type, but gains special benefits when equipped by you, detailed below. You can only have one Ritual Tool at a time; if you convert a new Ritual Tool when you already have your maximum amount, the oldest Ritual Tool returns to being an ordinary item. 

- **Arts Wand/Arts Staff.** You learn the Eldritch Blast Art; it does not count against your Arts Known, but you must be wielding this Ritual Equipment to cast it. Additionally, once per Turn when you hit a hostile Creature with the Eldritch Blast Cantrip, you can choose a willing Creature you can see within 30 feet of you (no Action required), then expend and roll one of your Aid Dice; that Creature gains Temporary Hit Points equal the amount rolled. If the Attack that triggered this effect was a Critical Hit, your Aid Die is not expended.
- **Clothing.** While you are wearing this Clothing, and are not wearing any Armour, your base Armour Class is equal to 13 + your Spellcasting Ability Modifier. Additionally, at the beginning of your Turn, if you do not have any Temporary Hit Points, you gain Temporary Hit Points equal to your Proficiency Bonus.
- **Instrument.** This Instrument gains the Casting Unit and Conductive (1) Properties. Additionally, once per Long Rest, when you play this Instrument for at least a minute, you regain expended Arts Points equal to half of your maximum Arts Points.
- **Piercing/Slashing Weapon.** You may use your Spellcasting Ability Modifier, instead of Strength or Dexterity, for the Attack and Damage Rolls with that Weapon. Additionally, you are considered Proficient with that Ritual Tool. Finally, once per Long Rest, as a Bonus Action while you are wielding this Ritual Tool, you may lose 1d4 Hit Points. If you do, you gain a bonus to your Spell Save DC and Spell Attack Rolls equal to the Hit Points you lost until the end of the Turn.
- **Trinket.** When you fail a Saving Throw, you may reroll that Saving Throw, but must use the new result. After you succeed on a Saving Throw as a result of this reroll, you may not use this Feature again until you have completed a Short Rest.



\page
{{pageNumber,auto}}

{{wide

#### 4th Level: Taking Shortcuts
Your familiarity with Ritual allows you to take a few shortcuts here and there. You gain the following benefits:

**Continuing Ritual.** When you complete a Long Rest, you may choose one Ephemeral Rituals that you had active before the Long Rest to reactivate. 

**Instant Ritual.** As a Bonus Action, you may immediately ativate any currently non-active Ephemeral Ritual for 1 minute. Once you have used this Feature, you may not do so again until you complete a Long Rest.

#### 10th Level: Pre-Preparation of Rites
Ritual is something that requires preparation, and you are keenly aware of this. You gain the following benefits:

**Esoteric Arts.** You learn one of the following Ritual Arts of your choice; it is always Prepared, and does not count against your number of Arts Known, but must always be cast as a Ritual.
- Find Familiar
- Unseen Servant
- Augury

{{align-text:left
**Ritual Expert.** You can cast any Art you know as a Ritual. At the end of that Casting Time, you may choose one of the following options:
- You immediately cast that Art; if the Art would require Concentration, you ignore that requirement for the duration of that Art.
- You do not manifest that Art's effects, and instead store that cast. If you do, you may cast that Art as an Action once; this casting does not cost Arts Points. You can only have one Art stored this way at a time.  
}}

#### 13th Level: Relics of Ritual
Your tools are so enchanted, so steeped in Arts, that they almost have a life of their own. Almost. You gain the following benefits:

**Ritual Tool Maximum.** The maximum amount of Ritual Tools you can have at a time is increased to 2. 

**Ritual Relics.** Your Ritual Tools provide additional effects:

- **Arts Wand/Staff.** Your Spell Attack Rolls when casting the Eldritch Blast Cantrip have +1 Critical Range. 

- **Clothing.** When you succeed on a Saving Throw against an Art or Art-like Effect to take only half Damage, you instead take no Damage if you succeed on the Saving Throw, and only half Damage if you fail.

- **Instrument.** You can play your Instrument as an Action. If you do, until the end of your next Turn, your Speed is reduced to 0, and you gain a +5 bonus to your Spell Save DC and Spell Attack Rolls.

- **Piercing/Slashing Weapon.** When you hit a Creature with an Attack using this Ritual Tool, that Attack deals additional Damage equal to one roll of your Aid Die of one of the following Damage Types (your choice): Acid, Fire, Lightning, Necrotic.

- **Trinket.** You may use this Ritual Tool's reroll on any d20 roll. Additionally, you may now use this reroll to succeed an additional time per Short Rest.

#### 17th Level: Ritual of Ruin
Ritualism is about channelling large amounts of Arts to create long term, lasting effects; you have created the ultimate destructive expression of this.
:
You can conduct a stationary 10 minute Ritual as if you were concentrating on an Art; at the conclusion of that Ritual, you choose a point you can see. All Creatures and Objects within 100 feet of the chosen point must make a Dexterity Saving Throw against your Spell Save DC. On a failed Save, they take Damage equal to 10 rolls of your Aid Die + your Supporter Level as a spear of energy crashes down from on high; Objects and Structures take double Damage. The Damage Type of this Damage is either Fire, Lightning, Necrotic, or Acid; you choose which. On a successful Save, they take half as much Damage. 
:
Once you have used this Feature, you may not do so again until you have completed a Long Rest.

}}

{{pageNumber,auto}}

{{wide

\page

{{wide

### Summoner
There are precious few Summoners; the Arts they employ are exceptionally complex, and seemingly resist all academic study; every Summoner has a unique way of accessing their ability, and few are capable of explaining how it works. Their rarity only adds to their demand; the ability to draw a small fighting force out of the air is a trump card generals throughout history would have given an arm for, and for good reason. Breaking the frontline of a Summoner is an exercise in attrition, and even the greatest get tired.

{{note
##### A Note on Party Composition
Any class that summons in any game walks a fine line between overpowered and useless; this is no exception. It is recommended that only one Player in a group play a Summoner, both to lighten the load on the GM and to help preserve the balance of the game.
}}

#### 1st Level: Summoner's Arts
You can cast Arts; the information below details how you use those rules as a Summoner.
:
**Arts Points.** The Summoner Arts Casting table shows how many Arts Points you have to cast your Level 1+ Arts. You regain all expended Arts Points when you finish a Long Rest.
:
**Maximum Arts Level.** The Summoner Arts Casting table shows the maximum Level you can cast an Art at, based on your Supporter Level. If an Art's Level is above your current Maximum Art Level, you cannot cast it, even if it is Prepared.
:
**Spellcasting Ability.** When you gain this Feature, choose either Intelligence, Wisdom, or Charisma; that is your Spellcasting Ability.

{{classTable,frame,decoration,wide

| Level | Maximum Art Level | Arts Points | Sparks of Creation |
|:-----:|:-----------------:|:-----------:|:------------------:|
| 1st   |         1        |      3      |        3
| 2nd   |         1        |      3      |        3

| 20th  |         5        |      17     |        12

***Summoner Arts Pools.*** When you gain this Feature, choose one of the Standard Arts Pools available to the Caster Class; you learn all of the Arts of 5th Level or less from those Pools, in addition to any Cantrips from those Pools. These Arts are always Prepared. At 11th Level, you may choose an additional Standard Arts Pool.
}}

\page
{{pageNumber,auto}}

{{wide

#### 1st Level: Creature Creation
You can use a combination of your Arts and physical matter to create artificial creatures, and instil them with motion.
:
**Sparks of Creation.** You have a number of Sparks of Creation that you use to create your Summons that increase with your Supporter Level, as shown in the Summoner Features Table. You regain all expended Sparks of Creation when you complete a Long Rest.
:
**Art of Creation:** As an Action, you can expend one of your Sparks of Creation to create a Summon in an unoccupied location you can see within 10 feet of you. When you do so, you choose its Stat Block; **Protector Creation**, **Slayer Creation**, or **Sling Creation**. The physical appearance of the Summon is determined by you, but should be appropriate for the type of Summon it is. A Summon lasts for 1 minute, at which point it disintegrates. The maximum number of Summons you can have at a time is three.
:
**Summons in Combat.** Your Summons act immediately after your Turn, in an order determined by you. As a Bonus Action during your Turn, you may give each of your Summons a particular instruction, such as to use a certain Action, pursue a certain Creature, or protect a Creature or location; they will attempt to fulfil this instruction until it is completed, they are destroyed, or given a different instruction. If a Summon has no instructions, each type of Creation has "default behaviour" that it will follow:

**Protector Creation.** Remains as close to you as it can, and prioritises protecting you. 

**Slayer Creation.** Aggressively pursues the nearest hostile Creature, and attacks them until they are reduced to 0 Hit Points before selecting a new target.

**Sling Creation.** Attacks the nearest hostile Creature.

}}

:
#### 4th Level: Symbiosis
Your Summons are created with your Arts, and thus your Arts have the strongest effect on them. You gain the following benefits:

**Art Cost Reduction.** When you cast a non-damaging Art that targets one or more of your Summons, one of the Arts Points you used to cast that Art is refunded after it is cast.

**Non-Damaging Restructuring.** As a Bonus Action, you can choose one of your Summons that you can see; expend and roll one of your Aid Dice. That Summon regains Hit Points equal to twice the value rolled, and gains any remaining Hit Points as Temporary Hit Points instead.

#### 10th Level: Arts Conduit
The Arts infused in your Summons allow you to treat them as a kind of "relay station" for your Arts. You gain the following benefits:

**Arts Relay.** When you cast a non-damaging Art that targets only one of your Summons, you may choose a different Creature you can see within 30 feet of that Summon; that Creature gains the benefits of that Art.

**Group Aid.** When you grant an Aid Die to one of your Summons using your Provide Aid Feature, you may also grant an Aid Die to a different Creature you can see (except yourself) within 10 feet of that Summon; this additional Aid Die does not consume one of your Aid Dice. 

<img 
  src='https://arknights.wiki.gg/images/8/87/Deepcolor_Elite_2.png?83fa2a&20230926231013' 
  style='position:absolute;top:370px;right:10px;width:400px' />

\page
{{pageNumber,auto}}


{{wide
#### 13th Level: Greater Creature Creation
As an Action, you can expend 3 Sparks of Creation to create a Greater Summon in an unoccupied location you can see within 10 feet of you. When you do so, you choose its Stat Block; Greater Protector Creation, Greater Slayer Creation, or Greater Sling Creation; The physical appearance of the Summon is determined by you, but should be appropriate for the type of Summon it is. A Summon lasts for 1 minute, at which point it disintegrates.
:
Alternatively, if two of your non-Greater Creations of the same type are within 10 feet of each-other, you can combine them into the corresponding Greater Creation as an Action. 

}}

{{monster,frame,wide

*Huge Construct, Neutral*

***Arcane Durability.*** This Creatures has DR against all Damage equal to its Proficiency Bonus.
::
***Artificial Nature.*** This Creature cannot regain Hit Points except through its Summoner's Symbiosis Feature or its own Features.
:
***Bastion.*** This Creatures provides Half Cover for Creatures within 5 feet of it.
:
***Aura of Wrath.*** The first time each turn a hostile Creature within 10 feet of this Creature is hit by an Attack, that Attack deals additional Force Damage equal to one roll of your Aid Die.

### Actions
***Crushing Slam.*** *Melee Weapon Attack:* +Strength Modifier & Proficiency Bonus to hit, reach 5 ft., one target. *Hit:* 13 (2d8 + 4) Bludgeoning Damage.
:
A Creature hit by this Attack must succeed on a Strength Saving Throw against your Spell Save DC or be knocked away 10 feet and knocked Prone. 

### Reactions
***Intervene.*** When a Creature within this Creature's Reach would make an Attack against a different Creature, this Creature may immediately make a Slam Attack against them. If it hits, that Creature's Attacks are made at Disadvantage until the end of the Turn.
}}

___

***Aggressive.*** This Creature may take the Dash Action as a Bonus Action. If they do, they must move as close as possible towards a hostile Creature they can see or hear.
:
***Artificial Nature.*** This Creature cannot regain Hit Points except through its Summoner's Symbiosis Feature or its own Features.
:
***Evasion.*** When this Creature is subjected to an effect that allows them to make a Dexterity Saving Throw to take only half Damage, they instead take no Damage if they succeed on the Saving Throw and only half Damage if they fail. 
:
***Multiattack.***. When this Creature takes the Attack Action, it may make two Attacks instead of one.
:
***Reaper.*** When this Creature reduces a Creature to 0 Hit Points, it regains Hit Points equal to one roll of your Aid Die.

### Actions
***Bristling Blades.*** *Melee Weapon Attack:* + Dexterity Modifier & Proficiency Bonus to hit, reach 5 ft., one target. *Hit:* 14 (2d8 + 5) Slashing Damage.
:
This Attack has +1 Critical Range.

### Reactions
***Uncanny Dodge.*** When this Creature is hit by an Attack, it can halve the Damage it takes from that Attack.

\column

#### 17th Level: Master Summoner
Your unusual Arts are so refined as to appear effortless; a terrifying prospect for your foes. You gain the following benefits:

**Maximum Summon Slots.** The maximum amount of Summons you can have at one time is increased to four.

**Refactorisation.** When you use your Non-Damaging Restructuring Feature to restore Hit Points to one of your Summons, you can restore half as many Hit Points to a different Summon you can see within 30 feet of that original Summon.

**Simultaneous Summoning.** Once per Turn when you summon a non-Greater Creation, you can expend 1 Spark of Creation to summon an additional non-Greater Creation; the additional Summon does not have to be of the same Stat Block as the original.

## Vanguard
In Kazimierz, a Kuranta's spear sparks as it makes contact with the Aegir's greatsword. Her opponent's strength is truly inhuman, but she does not need to outmatch her- only outmanoeuvre her.
:
In Ursus, two paramilitary forces clash in the ruined streets of a nomadic city. The outcome of their battle could very well determine the survival of hundreds of thousands; amongst the chaos, a cadre of messengers, led by a man with a single red streak in his hair, guides the soon to be winners to victory. He raises his standard once more. 
:
In Kazdel, a Caprinae slinks into the shadows, blending into her umbral backdrop like a chameleon. In a few moments, the outcome of her handiwork will be discovered, and the message will be delivered. She will remain to observe the whole process; despite the dozens of people pouring into the murder scene, not a one will spot her. 

{{tocExcludeH3 
### Class Features
}}

As a Vanguard, you gain the following Class Features:

#### Hit Points
**Hit Dice:** 1d8 per Vanguard Level<br>
**Hit Points at 1st Level:** 8 + your Constitution Modifier<br>
**Hit Points at Higher Levels:** 1d8 (or 5) + your Constitution Modifier per Vanguard Level after 1st

#### Proficiencies
**Armor:** Light Armour, Light Shields<br>
**Weapons:** Light Melee<br>
**Tools:** Messenger Kit<br>
**Saving Throws:** Dexterity + one of your choice from the following: Strength, Charisma <br>
**Skills:** Three from: Acrobatics, Athletics, Perception, Persuasion, Stealth, Wilderness

#### Equipment
You start with the following Equipment, in addition to any Equipment granted by your Background. 

- 1x Light Melee Weapon
- **A)** 1x Light Melee Weapon OR **B)** 1x Light Shield
- **A)** Reinforced Outfit OR **B)** Light Combat Gear
- 1x Tool Kit that you are Proficient with

OR
- 1300 LMD

\column

### Leaders of the Pack
Vanguards lead the way; that is the essence of what it means to be a Vanguard. Some choose the direct approach, physically leading their allies down a path with their own martial prowess; others, the indirect approach, clearing the way through stealth and subterfuge. Others still lead their allies in a literal sense, doling out orders, strategies, and tactics to win the day. 
:
What ties all Vanguards together, despite their disparate diaspora, is that they are all team players. While they may function independently, they do so at the behest of their allies. A Vanguard with no allies swiftly finds an ignominious end. 

::::

{{classTable,frame,decoration
##### The Vanguard
| Level | Proficiency Bonus | Features |    
|:-----:|:-----------------:|:--------:|
|  1st  |  +2  | Elite 0- Vanguard Branch, Landstrider, Tip of the Spear  |
|  2nd  |  +2  | Basic Skill |

|  4th  |  +2  | Ability Score Improvement, Branch Feature |

|  6th  |  +3  | Ability Score Improvement |
|  7th  |  +3  | Elite 1- Advanced Skill       |
|  8th  |  +3  | Ability Score Improvement, Basic Talent Module |
|  9th  |  +4  | Advanced Talent |
| 10th  |  +4  | Ability Score Improvement, Branch Feature |
| 11th  |  +4  | Uncanny Dodge |
| 12th  |  +4  | Ability Score Improvement |
| 13th  |  +5  | Advanced Talent Module, Branch Feature |
| 14th  |  +5  | Elite 2- Elite Skill
| 15th  |  +5  | Directed Engagement
| 16th  |  +5  | Ability Score Improvement
| 17th  |  +6  | Branch Feature
| 18th  |  +6  | Lightspeed
| 19th  |  +6  | Ability Score Improvement
| 20th  |  +6  | Approaching Storm
}}


\page
{{pageNumber,auto}}

#### 1st Level: Vanguard Branch
You choose a Vanguard Branch which most closely fits the style you envision for yourself. Choose one from the following list; further details on these Branches can be found at the end of the Class Description. The Branch you choose grants you Features at Level 1, 4, 10, 13 and 17.
- **Agent:** Striking and fading, the Agent excels at appearing where the enemy does not expect to lethal effect.
- **Charger:** The straightforward solution is often the best; Chargers engage the enemy head on, relying on the force of their strike to overwhelm the enemy before they can fight back.
- **Pioneer:** Clad in heavier armour than most Vanguards, Pioneers enter the fray and stay there, empowering their allies around them as they do so.
- **Standard Bearer:** Standard Bearers inspire their allies to fight ever harder; the sight of their standard fluttering in the wind can keep even the most battered force fighting.
- **Tactician:** Evaluate the situation, plan a strategy, direct the pieces on the gameboard; this is the core of a Tactician.

#### 1st Level: Landstrider
Your training as a Vanguard grants you the following benefits:

**Speed Increase.** Your Walking Speed is increased by 10 feet.

**Nimble.** When you hit a Creature with an Attack, you do not provoke Opportunity Attacks from that Creature for the rest of the Turn.

#### 1st Level: Tip of the Spear
As a Vanguard, you lead and direct your allies, pushing them ever onwards, supporting them when they might otherwise fail. Each Vanguard Branch, and some Skills and Talents, will provide you with the opportunity to use this Feature to grant beneficial effects to an ally; each instance will specify the conditions under which you do so.
:
When you use this Feature, choose an allied Creature you can see within 60 feet; roll 1d6 and apply the corresponding effect to them until the start of your next Turn; these effects are cumulative.


| Roll | Effect                      |
|:----:|:---------------------------:|
| 1    | Additional Damage equal to your Proficiency Bonus on one Damage Roll of their choice.
| 2    | They gain 1d6 Temporary Hit Points; these Hit Points are additive with any Temporary Hit Points they already have.
| 3    | Speed increased by 10 feet.
| 4    | +2 Bonus to Attack Rolls.
| 5    | Critical Range increased by 1.
| 6    | Whenever they gain a Skill Point, they gain 1 more.


#### 2nd Level: Basic Operator Skill
You learn a Basic Operator Skill from the Basic Vanguard Skills table, or from any other Basic Skill table available to you.

#### 3rd Level: Basic Vanguard Talent
You gain a Basic Talent of your choice from the Basic Vanguard Talents table. When you reach 8th Level, you may either pick a different Basic Talent to gain, or upgrade your Basic Talent, gaining its Module Effect.

#### 3rd Level: Quick off the Mark
Whenever you roll Initative, you may add your Proficiency Bonus to the result of the roll. 

#### 4th Level: Ability Score Improvement
When you reach 4th level, and again at 6th, 8th, 10th, 12th, 16th, and 19th level, you can increase one Ability Score of your choice by 2, or you can increase two Ability Scores of your choice by 1. As normal, you can’t increase an Ability Score above 20 using this feature.

<img 
  src='https://arknights.wiki.gg/images/Yato_CF.png' 
  style='position:absolute;top:400px;right:-100px;width:600px' />

\page
{{pageNumber,auto}}


:::::::::::::
#### 5th Level: Extra Attack
You can make two Attacks instead of one when you take the Attack Action.

#### 7th Level: Advanced Operator Skill
You learn an Advanced Operator Skill from the Advanced Vanguard Skills table, or from any other Advanced Skill table available to you.

#### 9th Level: Advanced Talent
You gain an Advanced Talent of your choice from the Advanced Vanguard Talents table. When you reach 13th Level, you may either pick a different Advanced Talent to gain, or upgrade your Advanced Talent, gaining its Module Effect.

#### 11th Level: Uncanny Dodge
When a Creature that you can see hits you with an Attack, you can use your Reaction to halve that Attack's Damage against you, rounding down.

#### 14th Level: Elite Operator Skill
You learn an Elite Operator Skill from the Elite Vanguard Skills table, or from any other Elite Skill table available to you.

#### 15th Level: Directed Engagement
When you would use your Tip of the Spear Feature, you may expend your Reaction to allow you to choose what effects are applied with that Feature, rather than rolling, until the end of that Turn.

#### 18th Level: Lightspeed
Your speed and reactions are so legendary that your foe barely has a chance to register happenings before it is too late.
:
You can take two Turns during the first Round of a combat. The second Turn is taken at your Initiative Score -10.

#### 20th Level: Approaching Storm
Like a force of nature, you roll over the unready.
:
When you roll to determine Damage dealt during the first Round of a combat, you may add half of your Vanguard Level to the result rolled.

| Leafcutter	| 65	| 30	| Offensive	| Action	| Choose up to eight different Creatures you can see within 30 feet; you may make a Melee Attack against each of those Creatures as if they were in your Reach. Those Attacks deal double Damage, and ignore any Damage Resistances. 	| Instant
| Oath	| 80	| 40	| Defensive	| Action	| You may activate this Operator Skill even if you would be prevented from taking an Action. You may remove any Status Conditions or effects affecting you, and you gain Temporary Hit Points equal to your Wisdom Modifier multiplied by ten (minimum of 10). While you have this Temporary Hit Points, you take half Damage from all sources. These Temporary Hit Points are removed when the Skill ends.	| 4 Rounds
| Spirit Strikes	| 80	| 35	| Automatic	| Bonus Action	| As part of the Bonus Action to activate this Operator Skill, and as a Bonus Action on subsequent Turns for the duration, you may make a Melee Attack; this Attack has 5 feet of additional Reach, and has its Damage converted to Force Damage. Additionally, if the Attack hits a Creature that has less than half of its maximum Hit Points, that Attack deals Damage twice. 	| 5 Rounds
| Thousand Cuts	| 65	| 30	| Offensive	| Action	| For the duration, you manifest a storm of slashes in a 15-foot-radius Sphere that is always centred on you. When a Creature (except you) enters the area or begins its Turn there, it takes 3d10 Slashing Damage. You may extend the duration of this Skill by 1 Round by expending your Action, up to a maximum of 5 Rounds.	| 2 Rounds

### Agent
Half-spy, half-assassin, Agent Vanguards roam ahead of the main force to gather information and eliminate key targets, all while remaining completely unseen.

#### 1st Level: Strike from the Shadows
Your training has given you a speciality with a particular set of tactics. You gain the following benefits:

**Quicklaunch.** When you make an Attack Roll using a Weapon with the Thrown Property, you may Draw a different Weapon with the Thrown Property as part of that Attack. 

**Shadowstrike.** When you take the Attack Action while you have the Invisible Condition, you gain the following benefits until the end of the Turn:
- You may make 1 additional Attack as part of that Attack Action.
- When you hit with an Attack, you may use your Tip of the Spear Feature (no Action required). 

#### 1st Level: Secret Agent
The covert Arts are your bread and butter. You gain the following benefits: 

**Skill Expertise.** You gain Expertise in one Skill of your choice in which you have Proficiency. Stealth or Sleight of Hand are recommended. 

**Fade.** As a Bonus Action during your Turn, you may grant yourself the Invisible Condition until the end of your next Turn. The Condition ends early if you make an Attack Roll, or force a Creature to make a Saving Throw.
:
You may use this Feature a number of times equal to your Proficiency Bonus, and regain all expended uses when you finish a Long Rest. If you are in Darkness when you use this Feature, you do not expend a use of it.

#### 4th Level: Surgical Strike
You move through targets less like a ghost, and more like bloody-handed death. You gain the following benefits:

**Pay it Forward.** Once per Turn, when you hit a Creature with a Ranged Attack using a Weapon that has the Thrown Property, you may move up to half of your Speed.

**Second Strike.** When you hit a Creature with a Ranged Attack using a Weapon that has the Thrown Property, your next Melee Attack this Turn against that Creature deals additional Damage equal to your Agent Level. 

\column

#### 10th Level: Closing the Case
On any Turn that you have used your Shadowstrike Feature, you may activate this Feature as a Bonus Action; choose a Creature you can see within 30 feet. That Creature takes 1d8 Piercing Damage for each time this Turn you have:
- Hit that Creature with a Ranged Attack using a Weapon that has the Thrown Property.
- Hit that Creature with a Melee Weapon Attack.

{{align-text:left
This Damage is increased to 2d8 at Level 14. 
}}

#### 13th Level: Consummate Professional
A professional never blames his tools, and is never found without them. You gain the following benefits:

**Tool Proficiency.** You gain Proficiency in the Mechanism Kit.

**Make it Happen.** You are always considered to have access to a Mechanism Kit, even if you do not physically possess one. This Mechanism Kit has unlimited Charges.

#### 17th Level: Smoke and Mirrors
If you don't want to be caught, you won't be.
:
During your Turn (no Action required), or as a Reaction, you can create a 10 foot radius sphere of magical darkness centred on yourself until the beginning of your next Turn; you can see through this Darkness. You may use this Feature once per Short Rest; however, if you reduce a Creature to 0 Hit Points, you may use this Feature without expending your use of it.

<img 
  src='https://arknights.wiki.gg/images/e/ef/Puzzle.png?c6c9ea&20230926233037' 
  style='position:absolute;top:580px;right:10px;width:450px' />

\page
{{pageNumber,auto}}

### Charger
While many Vanguards choose to support and direct their allies indirectly, Chargers take the most direct approach; they blaze a trail, and all their allies need to do is follow their warpath.

#### 1st Level: Gathering Momentum
As the battle wears on, you build up pace for a spine-shattering blow, represented by your Momentum. Your maximum Momentum is 6; You lose all accumulated Momentum when you are no longer involved in a Combat. You can gain Momentum in the following ways:
- +1 Momentum after you roll Initiative.
- +1 Momentum when you hit a hostile Creature with a Melee Attack; you must have not Damaged them since the beginning of your last Turn AND have moved at least 15 feet in a straight line immediately before making the Attack.
- +1 Momentum when you take the Couch Action detailed below.

**Couch.** As an Action, you take a stance ready to rush towards your enemy. Your Speed is reduced to 0 until the beginning of your next Turn. During your next Turn, your Speed is increased by 10 feet, and your first Melee Attack Roll during that Turn has a +1 Bonus for each Momentum you had when you used this Action.

**Momentous Strike.** As an Action, you can expend all your Momentum to make a single potent strike into which you put your all. This Attack deals 1d6 additional Weapon Damage for each Momentum expended. This is increased to 1d8 at 7th Level, and 1d10 at 14th Level.

{{imageMaskEdge1,--offset:1%,--rotation:270
<img 
  src='https://arknights.wiki.gg/images/6/6e/Fang_the_Fire-Sharpened_Elite_2.png?8e42a9&20240502045422' 
  style='position:absolute;top:350px;right:-160px;width:1000px;clip-path:rect(14% auto auto auto);' />
}}

{{imageMaskEdge1,--offset:8%,--rotation:0
<img 
  src='https://arknights.wiki.gg/images/6/6e/Fang_the_Fire-Sharpened_Elite_2.png?8e42a9&20240502045422' 
  style='position:absolute;top:350px;right:-160px;width:1000px;clip-path:rect(31% auto auto auto);' />
}}






\column

#### 1st Level: Crash and Bash
You are not a typical Vanguard, and prefer a much more direct approach to the idea of "guiding allies".  You gain the following benefits:

**Additional Weapon Proficiencies.** You gain Proficiency with Heavy Melee Weapons.

**Mark for Elimination.** When you deal Damage to a Creature, you mark that Creature until the beginning of your next Turn. If a Marked Creature is reduced to 0 Hit Points, you can immediately use your Tip of the Spear Feature (no Action required).

#### 4th Level: Onto the Next
Those unlucky enough to recieve your charge are either staggered or dead. You gain the following benefits:

**Push on Through.** On your Turn, if you have reduced a Creature to 0 Hit Points, you can make a Melee Weapon Attack as a Bonus Action that Turn. 

**Unbalancing Charge.** When you apply your Mark as a result of an Attack immediately before which you moved 15 feet in a straight line, it gains an additional effect; Creatures have Advantage on Attack Rolls against that Marked Creature.

{{note
##### Equipment Exchange
When you choose this Branch, you may exchange 1x Light Melee Weapon or 1x Light Shield from your Starting Equipment for 1x Heavy Melee Weapon.
}}

\page
{{pageNumber,auto}}

\column


#### 10th Level: Deathblow
The essence of the Charger is to put everything into a single fatal strike. When you hit with a Momentous Strike for which you consumed 5 or more Momentum, that Attack ignores Resistances.

#### 13th Level: Renewing Momentum
Would you ask a landslide why it is increasing in momentum, or would you simply try to get out of the way?
:
When you reduce a Creature to 0 Hit Points, you gain 1 Momentum. You can only gain this Momentum once per Turn.
  
#### 17th Level: Geirskögul
When you hit with a Momentous Strike for which you consumed 5 or more Momentum, you can force that Creature to make a Strength Saving Throw against your Martial DC (no Action required); your Martial DC is increased by 1 for each Momentum consumed to make that Attack until this Feature has resolved. You can use this Feature even if the Creature is reduced to 0 Hit Points by your Momentous Strike, as long as the body remains; they automatically fail this Saving Throw.  
:
You drive that Creature back a distance equal to your Speed, pushing them the whole way and thus moving with them. If you would push them into another Creature, that Creature must succeed on a Dexterity Saving Throw against your Martial DC or have you crash the original Creature into them; allied Creatures automatically succeed on this Save. If you do not crash into a Creature, this movement ends when you have moved the total distance or if you crash into an object. When you crash into a Creature or Object, both the original Creature and the additional Creature/Object take Bludgeoning Damage equal to half of the Damage dealt by the triggering Momentous Strike.
:
Once you have used this Feature, you must complete a Long Rest before you can do so again.


<img 
  src='https://arknights.wiki.gg/images/7/75/Plume.png?278a37&20230927014207' 
  style='position:absolute;top:-150px;right:210px;width:800px' />


\page
{{pageNumber,auto}}

### Pioneer
Pioneer Vanguards are frontline fighters, through and through. Unlike other Vanguards, who skirt around the battle, Pioneers are typically first into the fray, and often the last out, using their skills to inflict heavy casualties. The role of a Pioneer is to establish a foothold in the battle, and it is a role they fill well. 

#### 1st Level: Heavy Duty
Your tactics favour getting stuck into the fight. You gain the following benefits:

**Additional Weapon Proficiencies.** You gain Proficiency with Heavy Melee Weapons.

**Additional Armour Proficiencies.** You gain Proficiency with Medium Armour.

**Hardened Scars.** Your Hit Point Maximum increases by 2. Whenever you gain a Level thereafter, your Hit Point maximum increases by an additional 2.

{{note
##### Equipment Exchange
When you choose this Branch, you may exchange 1x Light Melee Weapon or 1x Light Shield from your Starting Equipment for 1x Heavy Melee Weapon.
}}

{{imageMaskCorner23,--offsetX:-47%,--offsetY:-34%,--rotation:0
<img 
  src='https://arknights.wiki.gg/images/1/12/Vulpisfoglia_Elite_2.png?20241103015802' 
  style='position:absolute;top:00px;right:0px;width:1300px;clip-path:rect(31.5% 69.5% auto auto);' />
}}

{{imageMaskEdge1,--offset:21%,--rotation:0
<img 
  src='https://arknights.wiki.gg/images/1/12/Vulpisfoglia_Elite_2.png?20241103015802' 
  style='position:absolute;top:00px;right:0px;width:1300px;clip-path:rect(auto auto auto auto);' />
}}








\column

#### 1st Level: Auramancy
You project an Aura in a 15-foot-radius around yourself that moves with you; you may disable or enable this Aura as an Action. When you complete a Long Rest, all effects your Aura currently has end, and you may choose one of the following effects for your Aura to gain. Your Aura is disabled while you have the Incapacitated Condition.

**Aura of Clarity.** You and allied Creatures within your Aura are immune to the Charmed and Frightened Conditions; if an affected Creature has one of these Conditions, it is suspended while they are within your Aura.

**Aura of Liberation.** You and allied Creatures within your Aura are unaffected by Difficult Terrain. Additionally, the Speed of affected Creatures cannot be reduced except by Conditions.

**Aura of Protection.** Allies within your Aura gain a bonus to Saving Throws equal to your Proficiency Bonus.

**Aura of Punishment.** Hostile Creatures within your Aura take 1d4 Psychic Damage whenever they are hit by an Attack.

**Aura of Purity.** You and allied Creatures within your Aura are Resistant to Poison and Necrotic Damage, and have Advantage on Saving Throws to resist or end the Poisoned Condition. 

**Aura of Infirmity.** Hostile Creatures within your Aura suffer a penalty to their Saving Throws equal to your Proficiency Bonus.

**Aura of Violence.** You and allied Creatures within your Aura can gain a Bonus to the first Attack Roll they make each Turn equal to your Proficiency Bonus.

:
For every 20 Skill Points you expend, you may choose an additional one of these effects for your Aura to gain (you may not choose an effect that is already active), and can immediately use your Tip of the Spear Feature (no Action required). If a Creature is within multiple Auras from this Feature, it only benefits (or suffers) from one of those Auras; the Creature may choose which one it is affected by if it is a beneficial effect, or automatically suffers the worse effect if is a negative effect..

{{note
##### Example: Overlapping Auras
For example, if a Creature was within the range of two different Auras with **Aura of Punishment** active, and was hit by an Attack, it would only take 1d4 Psychic Damage, not 2d4.
:
Similarly, if a Creature was within the range of two different Auras with **Aura of Protection** active, it would only gain the benefit of one of those Auras, and would have to choose which Aura to benefit from.
}}

\page
{{pageNumber,auto}}

#### 4th Level: Moment of Power
Your strength comes in waves, spiking high before returning to normal. You gain the following benefits:

**Skill Haste.** During the Turn you activate an Operator Skill, and while you have an Active Operator Skill, you can make one additional Attack when you take the Attack Action.

**Tactical Burst.** Whenever you activate an Operator Skill, you generate you can immediately use your Tip of the Spear Feature (no Action required). For every 10 Skill Points you spent to activate that Skill, you can use Tip of the Spear an additional time.

\column

#### 10th Level: Cycle of Violence
Violence begets more violence; few understand this more keenly than you. You gain the following benefits:
:
**Additional Auras.** The following options are added to your Auramancy Feature:

**Aura of Pressure.** Hostile Creatures that begin their Turn within your Aura take 5 (1d8) Force Damage. 

**Aura of Shadows.** Attacks against you from Creatures within your Aura are made with Disadvantage.

**Aura of Vigilance.** You and allied Creatures within your Aura gain a bonus to Initiative Rolls equal to your Proficiency Bonus.
:
**Skill Recharge.** When your Operator Skill ends, you gain Skill Points equal to your Vanguard Level if you reduced a Creature to 0 Hit Points during the duration of that Skill, or with the Damage of that Skill.

#### 13th Level: Maximum Aura
The range of your Auras for your Auramancy Feature is increased to 30 feet.

#### 17th Level: Breakthrough Skill
You gain an additional Elite Operator Skill; you may choose from any Elite Operator Skill available to the Guard Class, Defender Class, Marksman Class, Specialist Class, or Vanguard Class. However, ignore the Charging Method for that Skill. When you complete a Long Rest, you may exchange this Operator Skill for a different Elite Operator Skill from one of those classes.

{{imageMaskEdge1,--offset:2%,--rotation:90
<img 
  src='https://arknights.wiki.gg/images/2/26/Saga_Elite_2.png?a778b8&20230927021731' 
  style='position:absolute;top:140px;right:-140px;width:1100px;clip-path:rect(auto 51% auto auto);' />
}}

{{imageMaskEdge1,--offset:4%,--rotation:0
<img 
  src='https://arknights.wiki.gg/images/2/26/Saga_Elite_2.png?a778b8&20230927021731' 
  style='position:absolute;top:140px;right:-140px;width:1100px;clip-path:rect(37% auto auto auto);' />
}}
