{{pageNumber,auto}}

### Standard Bearer
The sight of a banner fluttering triumphantly in the wind has both driven many acts of heroism, and set entire armies to flight. This is the essence of the Standard Bearer; flag waving, exhortation upon their lips, they guide their allies to glorious victory.

{{note
##### Standard Bearer DC
Some Branch Features call for your Standard Bearer DC; this is calculated as 8 + your Charisma Modifier + your Proficiency Bonus.
}}



#### 1st Level: Battle Standard
A Standard Bearer needs a standard. When you choose this Branch, a Standard is added to your starting equipment.
:
**Creating the Standard.** Your Standard is a Melee Weapon with the following traits:
:
**Weapon Category:** Light Melee <br>
**Damage:** 1d8 Piercing <br>
**Properties:** Finesse, Two-Handed <br>
**Weight:** 5 lb. <br>
**Cost:** 200 LMD <br>
:
You can only have one Standard at a time; replacing a Standard costs 200 LMD.
:
Your Standard is a symbol of power; it lifts up your allies, and casts down your foes. While you are wielding it, it projects an Aura of Influence around you in a 10-foot-radius sphere centered on you.  As a Bonus Action while you are wielding it, you can plant the Standard at your current location; A Planted Standard does not impede movement, and can be picked up by a Creature within 5 feet of it as a Bonus Action.

While the Standard is planted, the size of the Aura of Influence is increased to 25 feet, but it is centered on the Standard instead of you. Additionally, when a Creature enters that area, or begins their Turn there, you can use your Tip of the Spear Feature on that Creature (no Action required). Once you have done so, you cannot do so again on that Creature until the beginning of your next Turn.




\column

#### 1st Level: Battle Cry
Those fighting around your Standard find themselves empowered, reminded of why they fight; your punctuating cries surge their morale to higher heights. This surging morale is represented by Fervour. You can gain Fervour in each of the following ways; once you have gained Fervour, you cannot gain Fervour in that way again until the beginning of your next Turn.

You can gain Fervour in each of the following ways, once per Turn:
- +1 when an allied Creature within your Aura of Influence scores a Critical Hit.
- +1 when an allied Creature within your Aura of Influence reduces a hostile Creature to 0 Hit Points.
- +1 when a hostile Creature within your Aura of Influence is reduced to 0 Hit Points.
- +1 when an allied Creature within your Aura of Influence activates an Operator Skill.

{{align-text:left
Fervour has no maximum value, but all of your Fervour is lost when you no longer in combat with hostile Creatures. 
}}
:
You expend Fervour as an Action to use one of your Battlecries, listed below. 
:
**Chorus.** Each Battlecry has a Chorus Effect; when you use a Battlecry, Creatures within your Aura of Influence can expend their Reaction in the way dictated by the chorus effect to join in with your battlecry, gaining a benefit.

#### 4th Level: Symbol of Authority
Your Standard is a symbol of its own; as the name suggests, you are just its bearer. You gain the following benefits:

**King of the Hill.** While your Standard is planted, you or an allied Creature that can see your Standard, who's Speed is more than 0, can use their Bonus Action to either: 
- Replace their Walking Speed with your Walking Speed until the end of that Turn.
- Increase all of their Movement Speeds by 10 feet until the end of that Turn. 

{{align-text:left
If they do so, they must end their Turn within your Aura of Influence.
}}

&nbsp; **Voice of the Herald.** As an Action, choose up to two different Creatures (except yourself) within your Aura of Influence that can see you or hear you. Once before the end of that Creature's next Turn, when they make a Damage Roll, they may roll 1d8 (no Action required) and add the result to the result of the triggering roll.

\page
{{pageNumber,auto}}



#### 10th Level: Power Projection
As an Action while you are wielding your Standard, or are within 5 feet of it if it is planted, you may launch it at a grounded location you can see within 60 feet. For each Creature within 15 feet of the chosen point, you can apply one on the following effects based on whether they are friendly or hostile to you (neutral Creatures are considered be friendly):

**Friendly.** The Creature can gain Temporary Hit Points equal to 1d12 + your Charisma Modifier. 

**Hostile.** The Creature must make a Dexterity Saving Throw against your Standard Bearer DC. On a failed Save, they take 6d6 Thunder Damage, and their Speed is reduced by 15ft until the end of their next Turn. On a successful Save, they take half as much Damage, and their Speed is not reduced. 
:
Your Standard is then planted at the chosen location. Until the end of your next Turn, both you and your Standard project your Aura of Influence, at a 10-foot and 25-foot radius respectively.
:
Once you have used this Feature, you must finish a Short Rest before you may do so again.

#### 13th Level: Proclaimer
When you speak, others listen. When you give commands, others obey, even to defy death. You gain the following benefits:

**Duty Not Yet Done.** When an allied Creature within your Aura of Influence would be reduced to 0 Hit Points but not killed outright, you can use your Reaction to order them to survive. If you do, they instead survive on 1 Hit Point. Once you have used this Feature on a Creature, you may not do so again on that Creature until you have completed a Long Rest.

**Skill Expertise.** You gain Proficiency in the Persuasion and Intimidation Skills. If you already have Proficiency in either Skill, you instead gain Expertise in that Skill. 

\column

#### 17th Level: The Last Stand
You have a voice that can command whole armies... or condemn them. 
:
As an Action during your Turn, while your Standard is planted and you are within 5 feet of it, you can declare a Final Stand. For 1 minute, or until you end the Final Stand as an Action, the following effects are applied:
- Your Speed is reduced to 0.
- Your Standard cannot be moved.
- As an Action, you can cause a willing Creature within your Aura of Influence to immediatley take a full Turn. After this additional Turn ends, your Turn resumes.
- When you would gain Skill Points, you can instead cause a Creature within your Aura of Influence to gain those Skill Points.
- When you use your Tip of the Spear Feature, you can immediately use it one additional time (no Action required), targeting the same Creature.

{{align-text:left
Once you have used this Feature, you cannot do so again until you have completed a Long Rest.
}}

{{imageMaskEdge1,--offset:1%,--rotation:270
<img 
  src='https://arknights.wiki.gg/images/4/4b/Saileach.png?fdd158&20230927020645' 
  style='position:absolute;top:320px;right:-150px;width:800px;clip-path:rect(14% auto auto 29%);' />

{{pageNumber,auto}}  

#### Battlecries
The following Battlecries are listed in alphabetical order.

##### Belligerant Exultation (3 Fervour)
**Effect:** Until the start of your next Turn, all allied Creatures within your Aura of Influence can use their Bonus Action to either take the Dash Action, or make a Weapon Attack.
:
**Chorus:** An allied Creature can expend its Reaction in response to this Battlecry to take one of the above Actions as part of that Reaction. However, they cannot take that Action as part of their Bonus Action next Turn.

##### Cry Havoc (5 Fervour)
**Effect:** You project a terrifying below in a 30-foot cone originating from you; hostile Creatures within this area must make a Wisdom Saving Throw against your Standard Bearer DC. On a failed Save, they take 2d4 Psychic Damage, and are Frightened of you. A Creature may reattempt this Saving Throw at the end of their Turn, ending the Condition on a success. On a successful Save, they take half as much Damage, and are not Frightened. Damage dealt by this Cry does not kill Creatures it reduces to 0 Hit Points, but instead completely breaks their morale, forcing them to flee or panic.
:
**Chorus:** When you declare this Battlecry, allied Creatures can expend their Reaction to increase the length of the cone, and the amount of Psychic Damage dealt, by 10 feet and 1d4, respectively.

##### Defiant Roar (10 Fervour)
**Effect:** Until the end of your next Turn, all allied Creatures within your Aura of Influence have Damage Reduction against Physical Damage equal to your Charisma Modifier. You may extend the duration of this effect by an additional Round as an Action while you are within 5 feet of your Standard, to a maximum of 5 Rounds.
:
**Chorus:** An allied Creature can expend its Reaction in response to this Battlecry to gain Resistance to the next instance of Damage they take within a minute.

\column

##### Heroic Proclamation (5 Fervour)
**Effect:** Until the end of your next Turn, all allied Creatures within your Aura of Influence are immune to the Frightened Condition; if they currently have the Condition, it is suspended while they are under this effect. Additionally, When a Creature under this effect makes an Attack Roll, they can roll 1d4 and add it to the result of their Attack Roll. You may extend the duration of this effect by an additional Round as an Action while you are within 5 feet of your Standard, to a maximum of 5 Rounds.
:
**Chorus:** An allied Creature can expend its Reaction in response to this Battlecry to gain 1d8 Temporary Hit Points that is cumulative with other Temporary Hit Points they have.

##### Rallying Cry (3 Fervour)
**Effect:** All allied Creatures within your Aura of Influence expend and roll one of their Hit Dice (no Action required), recovering Hit Points equal to the amount rolled + your Charisma Modifier. 
:
**Chorus:** Immediately after gaining Hit Points as a result of this Battlecry, a Creature can use their Reaction to expend and roll one of their Hit Dice, recovering Hit Points equal to the amount rolled + your Charisma Modifier. 

##### Strident Boast (10 Fervour)
**Effect:** Allied Creatures within your Area of Influence gain Skill Points equal to your Vanguard Level.  
:
**Chorus:** Immediately after gaining Skill Points as a result of this Battlecry, Creatures may expend their Reaction to gain that many Skill Points again.

<img 
  src='https://arknights.wiki.gg/images/Myrtle.png?eb1bc4' 
  style='position:absolute;top:570px;right:100px;width:275px;clip-path:rect(16% auto auto auto);' />

\page
{{pageNumber,auto}}

### Tactician
Whether through study or experience, Tacticians lead their allies in the most literal sense. They consider their strengths and weaknesses, and orchestrate the battlefield to best make use of both.

#### 1st Level: Lead from the Rear
A Tactician's position is not in the scrum, but guiding proceedings from afar. You gain the following benefits:

**Additional Weapon Proficiencies.** You gain Proficiency with Light Ranged Weapons.

**Forward Planning.** When you complete a Long Rest, you can create a Plan. A Plan is based either in a particular location, or against a particular Creature. When you create each Plan, name either a location or a Creature you are familiar with. Your maximum number of Plans is equal to your Proficiency Bonus; if you would create a Plan when you are at your maximum Plans, you must first discard a Plan of your choice.

{{note
##### Familiarity for Plans
There are some restrictions for being able to claim familiarity with a location or Creature. However, these restrictions are not ironclad, and a DM may allow other reasons on their discretion.

**Creature.** Can name at least two distinguishing features of that Creature (E.G. "A Feline, has a scar over his left eye, and wields a custom crossbow" or "An Originium Slug, twice the size of the rest, with a single horn on its front").

**Location.** Must have access to a map or some other way of knowing the geography of the intended area (E.G. "We're going to be fighting inside this house, so I have this floor plan of the building" or "our battle is going to take place in these city streets, so our allies have got us access to this drone camera so that we can see the streets from here").
}}


When you roll Initiative, and either of the following is true:
- The named Creature is hostile to you, and you can see them.
- You are in the named location.

{{align-text:left
You can activate that Plan (no Action required). You, and any Creatures of your choice, may add your Intelligence or Wisdom Modifier to the result of their Initiative Rolls for that Battle.
:
You can only have one Plan active at a time.
}}

{{note
##### Equipment Exchange
When you choose this Branch, you may exchange 1x Light Melee Weapon or 1x Light Shield from your Starting Equipment for A) 1x Light Ranged Weapon that does not have the Firearm Property.
}}

\column


#### 1st Level: Tactical Knowledge
Your training as a Tactician allows you to direct the battlefield in ways others cannot; you gain access to Basic Tactics (detailed below) that are fueled by your Acumen.

**Acumen:** Acumen is the resource that represents your capacity to bear the mental strain of commanding. Your maximum Acumen is equal to twice your Proficiency Bonus. You regain all expended Acumen when you complete a Long Rest.

**Using Tactics:** You can activate a Tactic either as an Action or a Bonus Action by expending the required Acumen for that Tactic; this means that you can activate two Tactics in the same Turn if you have the Acumen. However, once you have used a Tactic, you cannot use that Tactic again until the start of your next Turn. 

**Recieving the benefit of Tactics.** Many Tactics have no defined range of effect. However, a Creature must be able to hear or see the Tactician clearly to be a valid beneficiary of that Tactic; if an allied Creature is ruled by the DM as being unable to see or hear you clearly, they are not affected by your Tactics even if they are within its affected area. This means that communication through a radio, or through some other remote means, is sufficient for many Tactics to be effective. However, waving from the horizon is not likely to be clear enough to communicate your Tactics.

{{note
##### Example: Delivering Tactics
- The Tactician, Player A, is communicating with Player B, a Guard, through the use of Messenger Kits. Because Player B can hear Player A's instructions clearly, they can benefit when Player A uses the "Fall Back" Tactic on them.

- Player A wants to issue the "Volley Fire" Tactic to Players C and D, Marksmen on a nearby roof. Players C and D do not have Messenger Kits, nor can they hear Player A, but they are close enough to easily visually make out Player A's signalling with their arm, and can this benefit from that Tactic.

- Player A wants to issue the "Move Up" Tactic to Player E, a Caster who is 300 feet away. Player E is too far away to hear Player A's voice, does not have any other way to hear Player A, and while they can technically see them, they are so far away that they cannot make out any individual gesture. They cannot benefit from this Tactic.

}}

From 4th Level, when a Creature expends a Reaction in response to one of your Tactics while they are within your Strategic Zone, you may immediately use your Tip of the Spear Feature on that Creature regardless of range (no Action required).
\page
{{pageNumber,auto}}

#### 4th Level: Strategic Genius
Cerebral; that is your defining characteristic. The battlefield dances to the beat of your drum. This is represented by your Strategic Zones:

**Strategic Zone.**  Your Strategic Zone is a 15-foot cube centered on a grounded location you can see within 60 feet. It remains for 1 minute, or until you dismiss it as a Bonus Action. When you place a Strategic Zone, it cannot overlap with any other Strategic Zone. The maximum number of Strategic Zones you can have at once is equal to your Proficiency Bonus. 
:
**Placing your Strategic Zone.** As an Action, you can designate one of your Strategic Zones. When you do so, you choose a Strategy (described below) to apply to that Strategic Zone for its duration. Alternatively, if a face of the Strategic Zone is touching the face of another of your Strategic Zones, you can instead choose for the new zone to inherit the Strategy placed on the old zone, so that both Zones have the same Strategy applied.
:
**Strategies.** Your Strategies are listed in alphabetical order in the Strategies Table. Once you have applied a Strategy, you cannot apply that Strategy again until you have completed a Long Rest, except in the case of a Zone inheriting the Strategy from another Zone. Additionally, you can change the Strategy in one of your Strategic Zones as a Bonus Action; when you do so, you may also change the Strategy in all connected Strategic Zones. Finally, interconnected Zones are considered to be the same Zone for any Strategy that requires it.

<img 
  src='https://arknights.wiki.gg/images/a/a5/Mitm.png?716648&20240709035319' 
  style='position:absolute;top:570px;right:350px;width:500px' />

\column

##### Strategies
| Strategy | Effect                                                                               |
|:---------|:------------------------------------------------------------------------------------:|
|Choke Point | When a hostile Creature is hit by an Attack while they are within this Strategic Zone, all connected Zones with this Strategy become Difficult Terrain for that Creature until the beginning of their next Turn.
|Elastic Defence | When an allied Creature within this Strategic Zone would be required to make a Dexterity Saving Throw against an effect that targets an area, they can use their Reaction to move up to half their Speed. After this movement, if they are no longer in the affected area, they automatically succeed on that Saving Throw.
|Encirclement | When an allied Creature within this Strategic Zone makes an Attack against a hostile Creature within the same Zone, that is also within the reach of two or more other allied Creatures, that Attack ignores any Damage Reduction that Creature has against that Attack.
|Raid | When an allied Creature begins their Turn in this Zone, or enters this Zone during this Turn, they gain the following benefits until the end of their next Turn: Their Speed is increased by 10 feet, and when they hit a Creature no more than one size larger than them with a Melee Attack, they can knock that Creature away from them 10 feet. 
|Rank Fighting | When a Melee Attack is made against allied Creature in this Zone, other allied Creatures that meet the following conditions can expend their Reaction to make a Melee Weapon Attack against the Attacking Creature, if both the Attacking and Attacked Creature are within their Reach.
|Strength in Numbers | When you use a Tactic that only affects a single Creature (such as "Fall Back", or "Pincer Manoeuvre"), you can affect an additional Creature with that Tactic.
|Superior Firepower | Allied Creatures within this Zone have Advantage on Ranged Attacks against Creatures that have been Attacked since the end of the end of the allied Creature's last Turn. If they already have Advantage, that Attack instead deals 1d8 additional Damage; this additional Damage can only be dealt once per Turn.





\page
{{pageNumber,auto}}

\column

#### 10th Level: Fail to Plan, Plan to Fail.
No plan survives contact with the enemy, but having a plan is essential. You gain the following benefits:

**Lynchpin.** When you would place a Strategic Zone, you can instead attach that Zone to yourself, with the Zone being centered on and moving with you. You can only have one Strategic Zone attached to you in this way, and a Zone attached to you can overlap with your other Strategic Zones, allowing for Creatures to be affected by more than one of your Strategies at a time. Additionally, when the face of a Zone attached to you touches the face of another of your Zones, you can either:
- Cause the other Zones, and all connected Zones, to inherit the Strategy from the Zone attached to you.
- Cause the Zone attached to you to inherit the Strategy from the other Zone. 

**The Big Plan.** When you activate a Plan using your Forward Planning Feature, you can also activate this Feature (no Action required). If you do so, until the end of your first Turn, you can place your Strategic Zones without expending an Action, and you may place them at any range, even to locations you cannot see. Once you have used this Feature, you cannot do so again until you complete a Long Rest.

#### 13th Level: Master Tactician
Your mind is as great a weapon as any steel, and has laid low more enemies than most weapons will ever. You gain the following benefits:

**Tactical Expansion.** You gain access to the Advanced Tactics listed below.

**Well-Drilled.** When an allied Creature begins its Turn within one of your Strategic Zones, it gains a additional Reaction that it can only expend on your Strategies or Tactics.

#### 17th Level: Checkmate
It all comes down to a pivotal moment. That moment was ten steps ago.

When a Creature within your Strategic Zone makes an Attack Roll, or a Creature is making an Attack Roll as part of one of your Tactics, you can use this Feature as a Reaction; that Attack Roll is considered to be a Critical Hit, with all the benefits that entails, and ignores any Resistance that Creature has to the Damage of that Attack. 
:
Once you have used this Feature, you may not do so again until you have completed a Long Rest.


{{imageMaskEdge1,--offset:2%,--rotation:90
<img 
  src='https://arknights.wiki.gg/images/Muelsyse_Skin_2.png?cc6461' 
  style='position:absolute;top:-130px;right:-110px;width:1500px;clip-path:rect(auto 66% auto auto);' />
}}



\page
{{pageNumber,auto}}



#### Basic Tactics
The following Basic Tactics are listed in alphabetical order.

{{note
##### Tactics for Casters
In the world of Terra, Casters are an important part of battle tactica, no more or less than other warriors. For the Tactician's Tactics, this is represented in the following way: when a Tactic would allow a Creature to make a Melee or Ranged Weapon Attack as part of that Tactic, that Creature can instead cast a Cantrip that involves making a Melee or Ranged Spell Attack, respectively, to replace that Melee or Ranged Attack. However, if they do so, the exertion leaves them wobbling; they are Dazed until the end of their next Turn.
}}

##### Charge (3 Acumen)
Choose a point you can see; allied Creatures within 10 feet of the chosen point can immediately use their Reaction to move up to their Speed; they must finish this movement as close to a hostile Creature as possible. If they finish this movement with at least one hostile Creature within their Reach, as part of the same Reaction they can make a Melee Weapon Attack against one of those hostile Creatures.

##### Fall Back (1 Acumen)
You command an ally to carefully fall back, avoiding undue harm. Choose an allied Creature; that Creature can immediately expend their Reaction to move 10 feet without provoking Opportunity Attacks, and then do so again. Each movement must move them away from hostile Creatures; they can only move closer if it is the only way to move closer to safety (E.G. they are surrounded).

##### Move Up (1 Acumen)
You command an ally to move on the double. Choose an allied Creature; that Creature can immediately expend their Reaction to take the Dash Action.

##### Pincer Manoeuvre (2 Acumen)
Choose an allied Creature; that Creature can use their Reaction to move up to their Speed; they must finish this movement as close to a hostile Creature as possible. If they finish this movement with a hostile Creature within their Reach who is also within the Reach of an allied Creature, as part of the same Reaction they can make a Melee Weapon Attack against one of those hostile Creatures; this Attack is made at Advantage.

\column

##### Switch Ranks (2 Acumen)
You command your allies to regroup, switching tired fighters for fresh ones. Choose a number of allied Creatures within 60 feet of you, up to your Intelligence Modifier or Wisdom Modifier. Each of those Creatures can swap places with a willing Creature that has a Speed of more than 0 within 10 feet of themselves, without either Creature provoking Opportunity Attacks. A Creature cannot swap into a space it does not fit in.

##### To My Side (2 Acumen)
You command your allies to form up on your position. Choose a number of allied Creatures within 60 feet of you, up to your Intelligence Modifier or Wisdom Modifier; those Creatures can immediately move up to their Speed as a Reaction, but must finish that Movement within 15 feet of you if able, or as close to you as possible if they cannot.



<img 
  src='https://arknights.wiki.gg/images/6/61/Vigil.png?653f21&format=original' 
  style='position:absolute;top:350px;right:-150px;width:650px' />

\page
{{pageNumber,auto}}

{{wide

#### Advanced Tactics
The following Advanced Tactics are listed in alphabetical order.

##### Bring Them Down (3 Acumen)
You command for your allies to bring a Creature to their knees. 
1) Choose a hostile Creature that is Large or smaller you can see to be the target, and a number of allied Creatures within 30 feet of it up to your Intelligence Modifier or Wisdom Modifier. 
2) Each of these allied Creature may, as a Reaction, move up to their Speed; they must finish this movement as close to the target Creature as possible. 
3) After the movement, if any of the allied Creatures are within 5 feet of the target Creature, the target Creature must succeed on a DC12 Strength Saving Throw or be knocked Prone; this DC is increased by 3 for each additional allied Creature that finished the movement within 5 feet of the target Creature.
4) If the target Creature is knocked Prone, they cannot stand up during their next Turn.

##### Finish Them (3 Acumen)
You command for an allied Creature to bring a swift and violent end to a foe. Choose an allied Creature that is within 5 feet of a hostile Creature that is either Prone, or has no Speeds above 0. The allied Creature can make a Weapon Attack against the hostile Creature; Ranged Attacks are not made at Disadvantage for the Creature being Prone, or for making an Ranged Attack while there is a Creature within 5 feet. If it hits, do not roll Damage for that Attack; instead, it deals the maximum possible Damage, and ignores any Damage Reduction on the target. 

##### Focus Fire (4 Acumen)
You command your allies to loose a devastating volley at a single foe. Choose a point you can see, and a hostile Creature you can see; allied Creatures within 10 feet of the chosen point can immediately use their Reaction to make a Ranged Weapon Attack against the chosen Creature, if able. These Attacks do not deal Damage individually; instead, combine the Damage of all of these Attacks into one instance of Damage. 

##### Shield Wall (2 Acumen)
You command your shielded allies to prepare for an assault, raising their shield. Choose a point you can see; allied Creatures that are wielding a Shield, or have the Defensive Fighting Fighting Style, within 10 feet of the chosen point can immediately use their Reaction to raise their defences. For each Creature that does so, all other Creatures within the area gain a +1 Bonus to AC until the start of your next Turn, or a +2 Bonus if the Shield is a Heavy Shield. Creatures only retain this Bonus so long as they remain within the area.

}}

{{imageMaskEdge7,--offset:14%,--rotation:0
<img 
  src='https://arknights.wiki.gg/images/SilverAsh_the_Reignfrost_Elite_2.png?b10749' 
  style='position:absolute;top:430px;right:-130px;width:1000px;clip-path:rect(21% auto auto auto);' />
